- Create: `apps/web/src/api/http.ts`, `apps/web/src/api/client.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/api/http.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/api/client.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `@docmind/contracts` schemas (`ErrorBody`, `MeResponse`, `ListDocumentsResponse`, `DocumentDto`, `UploadDocumentResponse`, `RetryDocumentResponse`, `ListChatsResponse`, `ChatSessionDto`, `ListMessagesResponse`, `SendMessageResponse`, `StopChatResponse`, `DeleteAccountResponse`, `ErasureStatusResponse`, `SignUpResponse`) and the request types.
- Produces: `ApiError`, `Http`, `createHttp`, `Api`, `createApi`, `apiErrorFrom(status, bodyText, retryAfter)` as in the catalogue. `createApi(http, opts?: { xhr?: () => XMLHttpRequest; onUnauthorized?: () => void })`. `documentContent` is added by Task 197 (it needs a contract that does not exist yet).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

`http.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { z } from "zod";
import { ApiError, createHttp } from "./http";

const json = (status: number, body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...headers } });

describe("createHttp", () => {
  it("sends JSON with same-origin credentials and parses the answer with the schema", async () => {
    const fetch = vi.fn(async () => json(200, { n: 2 }));
    const http = createHttp({ fetch: fetch as never });
    const out = await http.request("POST", "/api/x", { body: { a: 1 }, schema: z.object({ n: z.number() }) });
    expect(out).toEqual({ n: 2 });
    const [url, init] = fetch.mock.calls[0] as unknown as [string, RequestInit];
    expect(url).toBe("/api/x");
    expect(init).toMatchObject({ method: "POST", credentials: "same-origin", body: '{"a":1}' });
    expect((init.headers as Record<string, string>)["content-type"]).toBe("application/json");
  });

  it("maps a failure to an ApiError with the code and Retry-After, and never keeps the server's message", async () => {
    const http = createHttp({
      fetch: (async () =>
        json(429, { error: { code: "too_many_requests", message: "sentinel-title-0123456789" } }, { "retry-after": "7" })) as never,
    });
    const err = await http.request("GET", "/api/x").catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect(err).toMatchObject({ status: 429, code: "too_many_requests", retryAfter: 7, message: "429 too_many_requests" });
    expect(JSON.stringify(err)).not.toContain("sentinel");
  });

  it("an error body that is not ours, or a code that is not a plain word, becomes 'unknown'", async () => {
    const odd = createHttp({ fetch: (async () => new Response("<html>", { status: 502 })) as never });
    expect(await odd.request("GET", "/x").catch((e: unknown) => e)).toMatchObject({ status: 502, code: "unknown" });
    const weird = createHttp({ fetch: (async () => json(400, { error: { code: "Bad Code; drop", message: "m" } })) as never });
    expect(await weird.request("GET", "/x").catch((e: unknown) => e)).toMatchObject({ code: "unknown" });
  });

  it("calls onUnauthorized for the code 'unauthorized' only (a wrong password is also 401 and must not sign anyone out)", async () => {
    const onUnauthorized = vi.fn();
    const expired = createHttp({ onUnauthorized, fetch: (async () => json(401, { error: { code: "unauthorized", message: "m" } })) as never });
    await expect(expired.request("GET", "/api/me")).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
    expect(onUnauthorized).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const wrong = createHttp({ onUnauthorized, fetch: (async () => json(401, { error: { code: "invalid_credentials", message: "m" } })) as never });
    await expect(wrong.request("POST", "/api/auth/login")).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "invalid_credentials" });
    expect(onUnauthorized).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("an answer that fails its schema is a bad_response, and a dropped connection is status 0 'network'", async () => {
    const bad = createHttp({ fetch: (async () => json(200, { n: "two" })) as never });
    await expect(bad.request("GET", "/x", { schema: z.object({ n: z.number() }) })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "bad_response" });
    const down = createHttp({ fetch: (async () => { throw new TypeError("sentinel-title-0123456789"); }) as never });
    const err = await down.request("GET", "/x").catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(err).toMatchObject({ status: 0, code: "network", message: "0 network" });
  });

  it("returns undefined for 204 and lets an abort through untouched", async () => {
    const http = createHttp({ fetch: (async () => new Response(null, { status: 204 })) as never });
    expect(await http.request("DELETE", "/x")).toBeUndefined();
    const aborted = createHttp({ fetch: (async () => { throw new DOMException("x", "AbortError"); }) as never });
    await expect(aborted.request("GET", "/x")).rejects.toMatchObject({ name: "AbortError" });
  });
});
```

`client.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError, createHttp } from "./http";
import { createApi } from "./client";

const ME = { userId: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10", tenantId: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", email: "a@example.com" };
const ok = (body: unknown, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } });

function apiWith(handler: (url: string, init: RequestInit) => Response | Promise<Response>) {
  const calls: { url: string; method: string; body?: unknown }[] = [];
  const fetch = (async (url: string, init: RequestInit) => {
    calls.push({ url, method: init.method as string, ...(init.body ? { body: JSON.parse(init.body as string) } : {}) });
    return handler(url, init);
  }) as never;
  return { api: createApi(createHttp({ fetch })), calls };
}

describe("createApi", () => {
  it("logs in with the credentials as JSON and parses the identity", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = apiWith(() => ok(ME));
    expect(await api.login({ email: "a@example.com", password: "pw" })).toEqual(ME);
    expect(calls).toEqual([{ url: "/api/auth/login", method: "POST", body: { email: "a@example.com", password: "pw" } }]);
  });

  it("builds the query strings: cursor and q are encoded, absent values are left out", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = apiWith(() => ok({ items: [], nextCursor: null }));
    await api.listChats({ q: "a b&c", cursor: "x=y" });
    await api.listChats();
    await api.listDocuments({ limit: 20 });
    await api.listMessages("c1", "1.5");
    expect(calls.map((c) => c.url)).toEqual(["/api/chats?cursor=x%3Dy&q=a+b%26c", "/api/chats", "/api/documents?limit=20", "/api/chats/c1/messages?cursor=1.5"]);
  });

  it("sends, stops, patches and deletes on the documented routes", async () => {
    const chat = { id: "c1", title: null, scope: "all", createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", lastMessageAt: null, messageCount: 0 };
    const { api, calls } = apiWith((url, init) =>
      init.method === "DELETE" ? new Response(null, { status: 204 }) : url.endsWith("/stop") ? ok({ stopped: true }, 202) : url.endsWith("/messages") ? ok({ messageId: "m1" }, 202) : ok(chat),
    );
    await api.sendMessage("c1", { text: "hi", messageId: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10", editOf: 3 });
    expect(await api.stopChat("c1")).toEqual({ stopped: true });
    await api.patchChat("c1", { title: "Renamed" });
    await api.deleteChat("c1");
    await api.deleteDocument("d1");
    expect(calls.map((c) => `${c.method} ${c.url}`)).toEqual(["POST /api/chats/c1/messages", "POST /api/chats/c1/stop", "PATCH /api/chats/c1", "DELETE /api/chats/c1", "DELETE /api/documents/d1"]);
    expect(calls[0]?.body).toEqual({ text: "hi", messageId: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10", editOf: 3 });
  });

  it("encodes an id it builds into a path", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = apiWith(() => new Response(null, { status: 204 }));
    await api.deleteChat("../x?y");
    expect(calls[0]?.url).toBe("/api/chats/..%2Fx%3Fy");
  });
});

class FakeXhr {
  static last: FakeXhr;
  upload: { onprogress?: (e: { lengthComputable: boolean; loaded: number; total: number }) => void } = {};
  status = 0; responseText = ""; withCredentials = false; responseType = "";
  onload?: () => void; onerror?: () => void; onabort?: () => void; ontimeout?: () => void;
  sent?: FormData; method?: string; url?: string; aborted = false;
  open(m: string, u: string) { this.method = m; this.url = u; FakeXhr.last = this; }
  setRequestHeader() {}
  getResponseHeader(name: string) { return name === "retry-after" ? "3" : null; }
  send(body: FormData) { this.sent = body; }
  abort() { this.aborted = true; this.onabort?.(); }
}
const DOC = { id: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", title: "T", filename: "f.txt", mime: "text/plain", sizeBytes: 3, status: "uploaded", error: null, stats: null, summary: null, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z" };

describe("uploadDocument", () => {
  const make = (onUnauthorized = vi.fn()) => createApi(createHttp({ fetch: (async () => ok({})) as never }), { xhr: () => new FakeXhr() as never, onUnauthorized });

  it("posts the file as the multipart field 'file' and reports whole-percent progress", async () => {
    const percents: number[] = [];
    const done = make().uploadDocument(new File(["abc"], "f.txt"), { onProgress: (p) => percents.push(p) });
    const x = FakeXhr.last;
    expect([x.method, x.url]).toEqual(["POST", "/api/documents"]);
    expect((x.sent as FormData).get("file")).toBeInstanceOf(File);
    x.upload.onprogress?.({ lengthComputable: true, loaded: 1, total: 3 });
    x.upload.onprogress?.({ lengthComputable: true, loaded: 3, total: 3 });
    x.upload.onprogress?.({ lengthComputable: false, loaded: 0, total: 0 });
    x.status = 200; x.responseText = JSON.stringify({ document: DOC, duplicate: false }); x.onload?.();
    expect((await done).document.id).toBe(DOC.id);
    expect(percents).toEqual([33, 100]);
  });

  it("maps a refusal to an ApiError and a dead connection to 'network'", async () => {
    const refused = make().uploadDocument(new File(["a"], "f.exe"));
    FakeXhr.last.status = 415; FakeXhr.last.responseText = JSON.stringify({ error: { code: "unsupported_type", message: "sentinel-title-0123456789" } }); FakeXhr.last.onload?.();
    const err = await refused.catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect(err).toMatchObject({ status: 415, code: "unsupported_type", retryAfter: 3 });
    const lost = make().uploadDocument(new File(["a"], "f.txt"));
    FakeXhr.last.onerror?.();
    await expect(lost).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 0, code: "network" });
  });

  it("aborts through the signal, and signs out on an expired session", async () => {
    const ctl = new AbortController();
    const aborted = make().uploadDocument(new File(["a"], "f.txt"), { signal: ctl.signal });
    ctl.abort();
    expect(FakeXhr.last.aborted).toBe(true);
    await expect(aborted).rejects.toMatchObject({ name: "AbortError" });
    const onUnauthorized = vi.fn();
    const expired = make(onUnauthorized).uploadDocument(new File(["a"], "f.txt"));
    FakeXhr.last.status = 401; FakeXhr.last.responseText = JSON.stringify({ error: { code: "unauthorized", message: "m" } }); FakeXhr.last.onload?.();
    await expect(expired).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
    expect(onUnauthorized).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `api/http.ts`.**

```ts
import { ErrorBody } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { ZodType } from "zod";

const CODE = /^[a-z][a-z_]{0,39}$/;

/** A failed call. `message` is "<status> <code>" and never the server's own text (it could carry content). */
export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly code: string;
  readonly retryAfter: number | undefined;
  constructor(status: number, code: string, retryAfter?: number) {
    super(`${status} ${code}`);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
    this.retryAfter = retryAfter;
  }
}

/** The error for a non-2xx answer: its status, the code from our error body (or "unknown"), and Retry-After in seconds. */
export function apiErrorFrom(status: number, bodyText: string, retryAfter: string | null): ApiError {
  let code = "unknown";
  try {
    const parsed = ErrorBody.safeParse(JSON.parse(bodyText));
    if (parsed.success && CODE.test(parsed.data.error.code)) code = parsed.data.error.code;
  } catch {
    // not JSON: "unknown"
  }
  const seconds = retryAfter === null ? Number.NaN : Number(retryAfter);
  return new ApiError(status, code, Number.isFinite(seconds) && seconds >= 0 && seconds <= 3600 ? seconds : undefined);
}

export interface RequestOptions<T> {
  body?: unknown;
  schema?: ZodType<T>;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
}
export interface Http {
  request<T = undefined>(method: string, path: string, o?: RequestOptions<T>): Promise<T>;
}

export function createHttp(deps: { fetch?: typeof fetch; onUnauthorized?: () => void } = {}): Http {
  const doFetch = deps.fetch ?? ((...a: Parameters<typeof fetch>) => fetch(...a));
  return {
    async request<T>(method: string, path: string, o: RequestOptions<T> = {}): Promise<T> {
      const init: RequestInit = {
        method,
        credentials: "same-origin",
        headers: { accept: "application/json", ...(o.body !== undefined && { "content-type": "application/json" }) },
        ...(o.body !== undefined && { body: JSON.stringify(o.body) }),
        ...(o.signal && { signal: o.signal }),
      };
      let res: Response;
      try {
        res = await doFetch(path, init);
      } catch (err) {
        if (err instanceof DOMException && err.name === "AbortError") throw err;
        throw new ApiError(0, "network");
      }
      if (!res.ok) {
        const failure = apiErrorFrom(res.status, await res.text().catch(() => ""), res.headers.get("retry-after"));
        if (res.status === 401 && failure.code === "unauthorized") deps.onUnauthorized?.();
        throw failure;
      }
      if (res.status === 204) return undefined as T;
      let body: unknown;
      try {
        body = await res.json();
      } catch {
        throw new ApiError(res.status, "bad_response");
      }
      if (!o.schema) return body as T;
      const parsed = o.schema.safeParse(body);
      if (!parsed.success) throw new ApiError(res.status, "bad_response"); // the zod error names fields, never values, but it is not shown either
      return parsed.data;
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `api/client.ts`.**

```ts
import {
  ChatSessionDto, DeleteAccountResponse, DocumentDto, ErasureStatusResponse, ListChatsResponse, ListDocumentsResponse,
  ListMessagesResponse, MeResponse, RetryDocumentResponse, SendMessageResponse, SignUpResponse, StopChatResponse,
  UploadDocumentResponse,
  type CreateChatRequest, type DeleteAccountRequest, type LoginRequest, type PatchChatRequest, type SendMessageRequest, type SignUpRequest,
} from "@docmind/contracts";
import { ApiError, type Http, apiErrorFrom } from "./http";

const qs = (o: Record<string, string | number | undefined>): string => {
  const p = new URLSearchParams();
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(o)) if (v !== undefined && v !== "") p.set(k, String(v));
  const s = p.toString();
  return s ? `?${s}` : "";
};
const seg = encodeURIComponent;

export interface Api {
  signUp(req: SignUpRequest): Promise<void>;
  login(req: LoginRequest): Promise<MeResponse>;
  logout(): Promise<void>;
  me(): Promise<MeResponse>;
  deleteAccount(req: DeleteAccountRequest): Promise<DeleteAccountResponse>;
  erasureStatus(receipt: string): Promise<ErasureStatusResponse>;
  listDocuments(q?: { cursor?: string; limit?: number }): Promise<ListDocumentsResponse>;
  getDocument(id: string): Promise<DocumentDto>;
  deleteDocument(id: string): Promise<void>;
  retryDocument(id: string): Promise<DocumentDto>;
  uploadDocument(file: File, o?: { onProgress?(percent: number): void; signal?: AbortSignal }): Promise<UploadDocumentResponse>;
  listChats(q?: { cursor?: string; q?: string }): Promise<ListChatsResponse>;
  createChat(req: CreateChatRequest): Promise<ChatSessionDto>;
  getChat(id: string): Promise<ChatSessionDto>;
  patchChat(id: string, req: PatchChatRequest): Promise<ChatSessionDto>;
  deleteChat(id: string): Promise<void>;
  listMessages(id: string, cursor?: string): Promise<ListMessagesResponse>;
  sendMessage(id: string, req: SendMessageRequest): Promise<SendMessageResponse>;
  stopChat(id: string): Promise<StopChatResponse>;
}

export function createApi(
  http: Http,
  opts: { xhr?: () => XMLHttpRequest; onUnauthorized?: () => void } = {},
): Api {
  const newXhr = opts.xhr ?? (() => new XMLHttpRequest());
  return {
    signUp: async (req) => void (await http.request("POST", "/api/auth/sign-up", { body: req, schema: SignUpResponse })),
    login: (req) => http.request("POST", "/api/auth/login", { body: req, schema: MeResponse }),
    logout: () => http.request("POST", "/api/auth/logout"),
    me: () => http.request("GET", "/api/me", { schema: MeResponse }),
    deleteAccount: (req) => http.request("DELETE", "/api/me", { body: req, schema: DeleteAccountResponse }),
    erasureStatus: (receipt) => http.request("GET", `/api/erasure/${seg(receipt)}`, { schema: ErasureStatusResponse }),
    listDocuments: (q = {}) => http.request("GET", `/api/documents${qs({ cursor: q.cursor, limit: q.limit })}`, { schema: ListDocumentsResponse }),
    getDocument: (id) => http.request("GET", `/api/documents/${seg(id)}`, { schema: DocumentDto }),
    deleteDocument: (id) => http.request("DELETE", `/api/documents/${seg(id)}`),
    retryDocument: async (id) => (await http.request("POST", `/api/documents/${seg(id)}/retry`, { schema: RetryDocumentResponse })).document,
    uploadDocument: (file, o = {}) =>
      new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const xhr = newXhr();
        xhr.open("POST", "/api/documents");
        xhr.withCredentials = true;
        xhr.responseType = "text";
        xhr.setRequestHeader("accept", "application/json");
        xhr.upload.onprogress = (e) => {
          if (e.lengthComputable && e.total > 0) o.onProgress?.(Math.min(100, Math.floor((e.loaded * 100) / e.total)));
        };
        xhr.onerror = xhr.ontimeout = () => reject(new ApiError(0, "network"));
        xhr.onabort = () => reject(new DOMException("aborted", "AbortError"));
        xhr.onload = () => {
          if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
            try {
              const parsed = UploadDocumentResponse.safeParse(JSON.parse(xhr.responseText));
              if (parsed.success) return resolve(parsed.data);
            } catch {
              // fall through
            }
            return reject(new ApiError(xhr.status, "bad_response"));
          }
          const failure = apiErrorFrom(xhr.status, xhr.responseText, xhr.getResponseHeader("retry-after"));
          if (xhr.status === 401 && failure.code === "unauthorized") opts.onUnauthorized?.();
          reject(failure);
        };
        o.signal?.addEventListener("abort", () => xhr.abort(), { once: true });
        const form = new FormData();
        form.append("file", file, file.name);
        xhr.send(form);
      }),
    listChats: (q = {}) => http.request("GET", `/api/chats${qs({ cursor: q.cursor, q: q.q })}`, { schema: ListChatsResponse }),
    createChat: (req) => http.request("POST", "/api/chats", { body: req, schema: ChatSessionDto }),
    getChat: (id) => http.request("GET", `/api/chats/${seg(id)}`, { schema: ChatSessionDto }),
    patchChat: (id, req) => http.request("PATCH", `/api/chats/${seg(id)}`, { body: req, schema: ChatSessionDto }),
    deleteChat: (id) => http.request("DELETE", `/api/chats/${seg(id)}`),
    listMessages: (id, cursor) => http.request("GET", `/api/chats/${seg(id)}/messages${qs({ cursor })}`, { schema: ListMessagesResponse }),
    sendMessage: (id, req) => http.request("POST", `/api/chats/${seg(id)}/messages`, { body: req, schema: SendMessageResponse }),
    stopChat: (id) => http.request("POST", `/api/chats/${seg(id)}/stop`, { schema: StopChatResponse }),
  };
}
```

  Note on `qs`: `URLSearchParams` encodes a space as `+`; the test expects `a+b%26c`. The server reads it with `URL.searchParams`, which decodes `+` as a space.

### Task 180: the SSE client

**Spec:** §9 item 36 (SSE helper), §6.1 (`GET /api/chats/:id/stream` is resumable via `Last-Event-ID`; `GET /api/documents/events`). Decision D3; Finding U3.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/api/sse.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/api/sse.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `SseFrame`, `SseParser`, `SseCloseReason`, `SseOptions`, `openSse`, `OpenSse` (catalogue). `openSse` returns `{ close(): void }`; after `close()` no callback runs. `onFrame` exceptions are reported by class (`report("sse-frame", err)`) and do not end the stream.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** `sse.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { SseParser, openSse, type SseCloseReason, type SseFrame } from "./sse";

describe("SseParser", () => {
  it("parses events across chunk boundaries, CRLF, comments, multi-line data and the id", () => {
    const p = new SseParser();
    expect(p.push("retry: 3000\n\n: heartbeat\n\nid: 1.4\nevent: me")).toEqual([]);
    expect(p.retryMs).toBe(3000);
    const frames = p.push("ssage\r\ndata: {\"a\":1}\r\ndata: more\r\n\r\ndata: bare\n\n");
    expect(frames).toEqual([
      { event: "message", data: '{"a":1}\nmore', id: "1.4" },
      { event: "message", data: "bare", id: "1.4" }, // the id stays until another arrives
    ]);
    expect(p.lastEventId).toBe("1.4");
  });
  it("does not end an event early when a CRLF is split between two chunks", () => {
    const p = new SseParser();
    expect(p.push("data: x\r")).toEqual([]);
    expect(p.push("\ndata: y\r\n\r\n")).toEqual([{ event: "message", data: "x\ny", id: undefined }]);
  });
  it("ignores an event with no data, an unknown field and an id containing NUL", () => {
    const p = new SseParser();
    expect(p.push("event: x\n\nfoo: bar\nid: a\u0000b\ndata: y\n\n")).toEqual([{ event: "message", data: "y", id: undefined }]);
  });
});

/** A fetch whose body yields the given chunks and then ends (or stays open when `hang` is set). */
function body(chunks: string[], opts: { hang?: boolean } = {}): Response {
  const enc = new TextEncoder();
  let i = 0;
  const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
    pull(ctl) {
      if (i < chunks.length) ctl.enqueue(enc.encode(chunks[i++] as string));
      else if (!opts.hang) ctl.close();
    },
  });
  return new Response(stream, { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "text/event-stream" } });
}
const status = (code: number) => new Response(null, { status: code });

function harness(responses: (Response | Error)[]) {
  const frames: SseFrame[] = [];
  const closes: SseCloseReason[] = [];
  const sleeps: number[] = [];
  const seen: { url: string; lastEventId: string | null }[] = [];
  let opens = 0;
  const fetch = vi.fn(async (url: string, init: RequestInit) => {
    seen.push({ url, lastEventId: (init.headers as Record<string, string>)["last-event-id"] ?? null });
    const next = responses.shift();
    if (!next) return body([], { hang: true });
    if (next instanceof Error) throw next;
    return next;
  });
  const done = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
    harnessResolve = resolve;
  });
  const handle = openSse({
    url: "/api/x",
    fetch: fetch as never,
    sleep: async (ms) => { sleeps.push(ms); },
    onOpen: () => { opens++; },
    onFrame: (f) => { frames.push(f); },
    onClose: (r) => { closes.push(r); harnessResolve(); },
  });
  return { handle, frames, closes, sleeps, seen, done, opens: () => opens };
}
let harnessResolve: () => void = () => {};

describe("openSse", () => {
  it("delivers frames, remembers the last id, and reconnects with it after the server ends the stream", async () => {
    const h = harness([body(["retry: 250\nid: 1.1\ndata: a\n\n"]), body(["id: 1.2\ndata: b\n\n"], { hang: true })]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(h.frames.map((f) => f.data)).toEqual(["a", "b"]));
    expect(h.seen.map((s) => s.lastEventId)).toEqual([null, "1.1"]);
    expect(h.sleeps).toEqual([250]); // the server's retry: wins over the base delay
    expect(h.opens()).toBe(2);
    h.handle.close();
  });

  it("backs off 1s, 2s, 4s on failures and resets after a successful open", async () => {
    const h = harness([status(503), new TypeError("x"), status(429), body(["data: ok\n\n"]), status(500), body([], { hang: true })]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(h.seen.length).toBe(6));
    expect(h.sleeps).toEqual([1000, 2000, 4000, 1000, 1000]); // after the good open the counter restarts, and an ended stream waits the base delay
    h.handle.close();
  });

  it.each([
    [401, "unauthorized"],
    [404, "not_found"],
    [400, "rejected"],
    [403, "rejected"],
  ] as const)("a %i stops for good and says %s", async (code, reason) => {
    const h = harness([status(code)]);
    await h.done;
    expect(h.closes).toEqual([reason]);
    expect(h.seen.length).toBe(1);
  });

  it("a throwing frame handler is reported by class and does not end the stream", async () => {
    const error = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    const seen: string[] = [];
    const handle = openSse({
      url: "/api/x",
      fetch: (async () => body(["data: one\n\ndata: two\n\n"], { hang: true })) as never,
      sleep: async () => {},
      onFrame: (f) => { seen.push(f.data); if (f.data === "one") throw new RangeError("sentinel-answer-0123456789"); },
    });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(seen).toEqual(["one", "two"]));
    expect(error.mock.calls).toEqual([["sse-frame RangeError"]]);
    handle.close();
  });

  it("close() aborts the request, releases the body and calls onClose('closed') once", async () => {
    const aborted: boolean[] = [];
    const frames: string[] = [];
    const closes: SseCloseReason[] = [];
    let cancelled = false;
    const enc = new TextEncoder();
    let sent = false;
    const handle = openSse({
      url: "/api/x",
      fetch: (async (_u: string, init: RequestInit) => {
        init.signal?.addEventListener("abort", () => aborted.push(true));
        const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
          pull(ctl) { if (!sent) { sent = true; ctl.enqueue(enc.encode("data: a\n\n")); } },
          cancel() { cancelled = true; },
        });
        return new Response(stream, { status: 200 });
      }) as never,
      sleep: async () => {},
      onFrame: (f) => { frames.push(f.data); },
      onClose: (r) => { closes.push(r); },
    });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(frames).toEqual(["a"]));
    handle.close();
    handle.close(); // idempotent
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(cancelled).toBe(true));
    expect(aborted).toEqual([true]);
    expect(closes).toEqual(["closed"]);
  });

  it("reads the url again on every attempt, so a resume cursor in the query is current", async () => {
    let n = 0;
    const urls: string[] = [];
    const handle = openSse({
      url: () => `/api/x?cursor=${++n}`,
      fetch: (async (u: string) => { urls.push(u); return urls.length === 1 ? body(["data: a\n\n"]) : body([], { hang: true }); }) as never,
      sleep: async () => {},
      onFrame: () => {},
    });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(urls).toEqual(["/api/x?cursor=1", "/api/x?cursor=2"]));
    handle.close();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `api/sse.ts`.**

```ts
import { report } from "../report";

export interface SseFrame {
  event: string;
  data: string;
  id: string | undefined;
}

/** The text/event-stream line protocol. Feed it decoded text in any chunking; it returns the frames completed so far. */
export class SseParser {
  lastEventId: string | undefined;
  retryMs: number | undefined;
  private buffer = "";
  private event = "";
  private data: string[] = [];

  push(chunk: string): SseFrame[] {
    const text = this.buffer + chunk;
    const lines = text.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/);
    this.buffer = lines.pop() ?? ""; // an unterminated last line waits for more text
    // A chunk that ends in CR may be the first half of CRLF: hold that line back until the next chunk shows which.
    if (text.endsWith("\r")) this.buffer = `${lines.pop() ?? ""}\r`;
    const out: SseFrame[] = [];
    for (const line of lines) {
      if (line === "") {
        if (this.data.length > 0) out.push({ event: this.event || "message", data: this.data.join("\n"), id: this.lastEventId });
        this.event = "";
        this.data = [];
        continue;
      }
      if (line.startsWith(":")) continue;
      const at = line.indexOf(":");
      const field = at === -1 ? line : line.slice(0, at);
      let value = at === -1 ? "" : line.slice(at + 1);
      if (value.startsWith(" ")) value = value.slice(1);
      if (field === "event") this.event = value;
      else if (field === "data") this.data.push(value);
      else if (field === "id" && !value.includes("\u0000")) this.lastEventId = value;
      else if (field === "retry" && /^\d{1,6}$/.test(value)) this.retryMs = Number(value);
    }
    return out;
  }
}

export type SseCloseReason = "unauthorized" | "not_found" | "rejected" | "closed";

export interface SseOptions {
  /** Read again before every attempt, so a resume cursor can live in the query. */
  url: string | (() => string);
  fetch?: typeof fetch;
  /** The id to send as `Last-Event-ID` on the first attempt; later attempts use the last id received. */
  lastEventId?: () => string | undefined;
  onOpen?(): void;
  onFrame(frame: SseFrame): void;
  onClose?(reason: SseCloseReason): void;
  sleep?(ms: number, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
  baseDelayMs?: number;
  maxDelayMs?: number;
}
export type OpenSse = typeof openSse;

const realSleep = (ms: number, signal: AbortSignal) =>
  new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
    const t = setTimeout(resolve, ms);
    signal.addEventListener("abort", () => { clearTimeout(t); resolve(); }, { once: true });
  });

/**
 * A resumable event-stream reader over fetch. It reconnects with `Last-Event-ID` and backs off (the server's `retry:` wins
 * over the base delay; failures double up to the maximum; a good open resets the count). 401, 404 and 400/403 end it for
 * good and say why. Frames are delivered in order; a handler that throws is reported by class and the stream goes on.
 */
export function openSse(o: SseOptions): { close(): void } {
  const doFetch = o.fetch ?? ((...a: Parameters<typeof fetch>) => fetch(...a));
  const sleep = o.sleep ?? realSleep;
  const base = o.baseDelayMs ?? 1000;
  const max = o.maxDelayMs ?? 30_000;
  const ctl = new AbortController();
  let closed = false;
  let reading: ReadableStreamDefaultReader<Uint8Array> | undefined;
  const stop = (reason: SseCloseReason) => {
    if (closed) return;
    closed = true;
    ctl.abort();
    void reading?.cancel().catch(() => {}); // release the body even when the platform does not tie it to the signal
    o.onClose?.(reason);
  };

  void (async () => {
    let failures = 0;
    let lastId = o.lastEventId?.();
    while (!closed) {
      let wait = base * 2 ** Math.min(failures, 10);
      try {
        const headers: Record<string, string> = { accept: "text/event-stream", ...(lastId !== undefined && { "last-event-id": lastId }) };
        const res = await doFetch(typeof o.url === "function" ? o.url() : o.url, { headers, credentials: "same-origin", cache: "no-store", signal: ctl.signal });
        if (res.status === 401) return stop("unauthorized");
        if (res.status === 404) return stop("not_found");
        if (res.status === 400 || res.status === 403) return stop("rejected");
        if (!res.ok || !res.body) throw new Error("not open");
        failures = 0;
        wait = base;
        if (!closed) o.onOpen?.();
        const parser = new SseParser();
        const reader = res.body.getReader();
        reading = reader;
        const text = new TextDecoder();
        for (;;) {
          const { value, done } = await reader.read();
          if (done || closed) break;
          for (const frame of parser.push(text.decode(value, { stream: true }))) {
            if (closed) break;
            try {
              o.onFrame(frame);
            } catch (err) {
              report("sse-frame", err);
            }
          }
          if (parser.retryMs !== undefined) wait = parser.retryMs;
          lastId = parser.lastEventId ?? lastId;
        }
      } catch {
        if (closed) return;
        failures++;
      }
      if (closed) return;
      await sleep(wait, ctl.signal);
    }
  })();

  return { close: () => stop("closed") };
}
```

  Check against the tests: a `retry: 250` makes the first wait 250 (the stream ended normally, `failures` is 0). After `status(503)` etc. `failures` increments: waits 1000, 2000, 4000; the good open resets and the stream end waits `base` (1000); the next 500 failure waits `base * 2**1`? `failures` is 1 after the 500, so `wait = 2000`, not 1000. The test therefore expects `[1000, 2000, 4000, 1000, 1000]` where the fifth entry is the wait after the **ended stream** (`wait = base`, failures 0) and the sleep before the sixth fetch comes after the 500 (`failures` = 1 → 2000). Recompute the expected list when running: sequence of sleeps is: 503 (f=1) sleeps 1000; TypeError (f=2) sleeps 2000; 429 (f=3) sleeps 4000; good body ends (wait=base=1000) sleeps 1000; 500 (f=1) sleeps 1000? `wait` is computed at loop top as `base * 2 ** min(failures, 10)` *before* the attempt, with the failures value from the previous round, so a failure that makes `failures` 1 sleeps `base * 2**0`? No: the sleep happens after `failures++` but uses `wait` computed before the increment. So: 503: wait computed with failures 0 → 1000; TypeError: failures 1 → 2000; 429: failures 2 → 4000; good open: wait = base → 1000; 500: failures 0 → 1000. That gives `[1000, 2000, 4000, 1000, 1000]`, which is what the test says. Keep the code as written and the test as written.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { type Route, Router, hrefOf, parseRoute } from "./router";

const ID = "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10";

describe("parseRoute / hrefOf", () => {
  const table: [string, string, Route][] = [
    ["/", "", { name: "chat" }],
    ["/chats", "", { name: "chat" }],
    ["/chats/", "", { name: "chat" }],
    [`/chats/${ID}`, "", { name: "chat", chatId: ID }],
    [`/chats/${ID.toUpperCase()}`, "", { name: "chat", chatId: ID }],
    ["/documents", "", { name: "documents" }],
    [`/documents/${ID}`, "", { name: "document", id: ID }],
    [`/documents/${ID}`, `?passage=${ID}`, { name: "document", id: ID, passage: ID }],
    [`/documents/${ID}`, "?passage=not-an-id", { name: "document", id: ID }],
    ["/sign-in", "", { name: "sign-in" }],
    ["/sign-up", "", { name: "sign-up" }],
    ["/account-deleted", "", { name: "account-deleted" }],
    ["/settings", "", { name: "settings" }],
  ];
  it.each(table)("%s%s", (path, search, route) => expect(parseRoute(path, search)).toEqual(route));

  it("anything else, including a path that is not an id, is not-found", () => {
    for (const path of ["/chats/abc", "/documents/../x", "/nope", "//evil.example.com", "/chats/" + ID + "/x"])
      expect(parseRoute(path)).toEqual({ name: "not-found" });
  });

  it("hrefOf is the inverse for every route that has a path", () => {
    for (const [path, search, route] of table) {
      if (path === "/" || path.endsWith("/") || path.includes(ID.toUpperCase())) continue;
      expect(hrefOf(route)).toBe(path + search);
    }
    expect(hrefOf({ name: "not-found" })).toBe("/chats");
  });
});

function fakeWindow(path = "/chats") {
  const listeners: Record<string, (() => void)[]> = {};
  const win = {
    location: { pathname: path, search: "" },
    history: {
      pushState: vi.fn((_s: unknown, _t: string, url: string) => { win.location.pathname = new URL(url, "http://x").pathname; win.location.search = new URL(url, "http://x").search; }),
      replaceState: vi.fn((_s: unknown, _t: string, url: string) => { win.location.pathname = new URL(url, "http://x").pathname; win.location.search = new URL(url, "http://x").search; }),
    },
    addEventListener: (t: string, fn: () => void) => { (listeners[t] ??= []).push(fn); },
    removeEventListener: (t: string, fn: () => void) => { listeners[t] = (listeners[t] ?? []).filter((f) => f !== fn); },
  };
  return { win: win as never, raw: win, fire: (t: string) => { for (const fn of listeners[t] ?? []) fn(); }, listeners };
}

describe("Router", () => {
  it("starts on the current location, pushes or replaces on navigate, and follows popstate", () => {
    const f = fakeWindow("/documents");
    const router = new Router(f.win);
    router.start();
    expect(router.route).toEqual({ name: "documents" });
    router.navigate({ name: "settings" });
    expect(f.raw.history.pushState).toHaveBeenCalledWith(null, "", "/settings");
    router.navigate({ name: "chat" }, { replace: true });
    expect(f.raw.history.replaceState).toHaveBeenCalledWith(null, "", "/chats");
    f.raw.location.pathname = "/documents";
    f.fire("popstate");
    expect(router.route).toEqual({ name: "documents" });
    const seen: string[] = [];
    router.subscribe((r) => seen.push(r.name));
    router.navigate({ name: "settings" });
    expect(seen).toEqual(["settings"]);
  });

  it("does not push the route it is already on, and stop() removes the listener", () => {
    const f = fakeWindow("/settings");
    const router = new Router(f.win);
    router.start();
    router.navigate({ name: "settings" });
    expect(f.raw.history.pushState).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    router.stop();
    expect(f.listeners.popstate).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ToastStore } from "./toasts";

describe("ToastStore", () => {
  it("adds toasts with increasing ids and dismisses one by id", () => {
    const toasts = new ToastStore();
    const a = toasts.push({ kind: "ok", text: "Saved" });
    const b = toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Failed", action: { label: "Retry", run: () => {} } });
    expect(b).toBeGreaterThan(a);
    expect(toasts.get().map((t) => t.text)).toEqual(["Saved", "Failed"]);
    toasts.dismiss(a);
    expect(toasts.get().map((t) => t.id)).toEqual([b]);
    toasts.dismiss(999);
    expect(toasts.get().length).toBe(1);
  });
  it("keeps at most five, dropping the oldest", () => {
    const toasts = new ToastStore();
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) toasts.push({ kind: "ok", text: `t${i}` });
    expect(toasts.get().map((t) => t.text)).toEqual(["t2", "t3", "t4", "t5", "t6"]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { Router } from "../router";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import "./index";

function services(path: string) {
  const win = { location: { pathname: path, search: "" }, history: { pushState: vi.fn((_s: unknown, _t: string, u: string) => { win.location.pathname = u; }), replaceState: vi.fn() }, addEventListener: () => {}, removeEventListener: () => {} };
  return createServices({ router: new Router(win as never), fetch: (async () => new Response("{}", { status: 404 })) as never });
}
const root = (el: Element) => el.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot;
const settle = async (el: Element) => { await (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete; };

describe("dm-app", () => {
  it("draws the view of the current route, and not-found for a route with no view", async () => {
    const svc = services("/nope");
    const app = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(svc, "dm-app", { services: svc });
    expect(root(app).querySelector("dm-not-found")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(svc.router.route).toEqual({ name: "not-found" });
  });

  it("follows the router", async () => {
    const svc = services("/nope");
    const app = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(svc, "dm-app", { services: svc });
    svc.router.navigate({ name: "documents" });
    await settle(app);
    expect(root(app).querySelector("dm-not-found")).not.toBeNull(); // no view registered yet in this task
  });

  it("turns a click on a same-origin link into a navigation, and leaves modified and external links alone", async () => {
    const svc = services("/nope");
    const app = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(svc, "dm-app", { services: svc });
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = "/settings";
    root(app).append(a);
    a.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("click", { bubbles: true, composed: true, cancelable: true }));
    expect(svc.router.route).toEqual({ name: "settings" });
    const ext = document.createElement("a");
    ext.href = "https://example.com/x";
    root(app).append(ext);
    const ev = new MouseEvent("click", { bubbles: true, composed: true, cancelable: true });
    ext.dispatchEvent(ev);
    expect(ev.defaultPrevented).toBe(false);
    const mod = document.createElement("a");
    mod.href = "/documents";
    root(app).append(mod);
    mod.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("click", { bubbles: true, composed: true, cancelable: true, ctrlKey: true }));
    expect(svc.router.route).toEqual({ name: "settings" });
  });

  it("shows toasts as status or alert and lets the user dismiss them", async () => {
    const svc = services("/nope");
    const app = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(svc, "dm-app", { services: svc });
    svc.toasts.push({ kind: "ok", text: "Saved" });
    svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Failed" });
    await settle(app);
    const toasts = root(app).querySelector("dm-toasts") as HTMLElement;
    await settle(toasts);
    const items = [...root(toasts).querySelectorAll("[data-toast]")];
    expect(items.map((i) => [i.getAttribute("role"), i.textContent?.includes("Saved") || i.textContent?.includes("Failed")])).toEqual([["status", true], ["alert", true]]);
    (root(toasts).querySelector("[data-toast] button[aria-label='Dismiss']") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await settle(toasts);
    expect(root(toasts).querySelectorAll("[data-toast]").length).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

`router.ts`:

```ts
import { Store } from "./state/store";

export type Route =
  | { name: "sign-in" }
  | { name: "sign-up" }
  | { name: "account-deleted" }
  | { name: "chat"; chatId?: string }
  | { name: "documents" }
  | { name: "document"; id: string; passage?: string }
  | { name: "settings" }
  | { name: "not-found" };

const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

export function parseRoute(path: string, search = ""): Route {
  const p = path.replace(/\/+$/, "") || "/";
  switch (p) {
    case "/":
    case "/chats":
      return { name: "chat" };
    case "/documents":
      return { name: "documents" };
    case "/sign-in":
      return { name: "sign-in" };
    case "/sign-up":
      return { name: "sign-up" };
    case "/account-deleted":
      return { name: "account-deleted" };
    case "/settings":
      return { name: "settings" };
  }
  const chat = /^\/chats\/([^/]+)$/.exec(p);
  if (chat && UUID.test(chat[1] as string)) return { name: "chat", chatId: (chat[1] as string).toLowerCase() };
  const doc = /^\/documents\/([^/]+)$/.exec(p);
  if (doc && UUID.test(doc[1] as string)) {
    const passage = new URLSearchParams(search).get("passage");
    return {
      name: "document",
      id: (doc[1] as string).toLowerCase(),
      ...(passage !== null && UUID.test(passage) && { passage: passage.toLowerCase() }),
    };
  }
  return { name: "not-found" };
}

export function hrefOf(r: Route): string {
  switch (r.name) {
    case "chat":
      return r.chatId ? `/chats/${r.chatId}` : "/chats";
    case "document":
      return `/documents/${r.id}${r.passage ? `?passage=${r.passage}` : ""}`;
    case "not-found":
      return "/chats";
    default:
      return `/${r.name}`;
  }
}

export type RouterWindow = Pick<Window, "location" | "history" | "addEventListener" | "removeEventListener">;

/** The current route, kept in sync with the address bar through the History API. */
export class Router extends Store<Route> {
  constructor(private readonly win: RouterWindow = window) {
    super(parseRoute(win.location.pathname, win.location.search));
  }
  get route(): Route {
    return this.get();
  }
  private readonly onPop = () => this.set(parseRoute(this.win.location.pathname, this.win.location.search));
  start(): void {
    this.win.addEventListener("popstate", this.onPop);
  }
  stop(): void {
    this.win.removeEventListener("popstate", this.onPop);
  }
  navigate(r: Route, o: { replace?: boolean } = {}): void {
    const href = hrefOf(r);
    const here = this.win.location.pathname + this.win.location.search;
    if (href !== here) (o.replace ? this.win.history.replaceState : this.win.history.pushState).call(this.win.history, null, "", href);
    this.set(r);
  }
}
```

  (A `Store` compares with `Object.is`, so navigating to an equal-but-new route object notifies once more; the views are idempotent, so that is accepted.)

`state/toasts.ts`:

```ts
import { Store } from "./store";

export interface Toast {
  id: number;
  kind: "ok" | "err";
  text: string;
  action?: { label: string; run(): void };
}
const MAX = 5;

export class ToastStore extends Store<Toast[]> {
  private seq = 0;
  constructor() {
    super([]);
  }
  push(t: Omit<Toast, "id">): number {
    const id = ++this.seq;
    this.update((all) => [...all, { ...t, id }].slice(-MAX));
    return id;
  }
  dismiss(id: number): void {
    this.update((all) => (all.some((t) => t.id === id) ? all.filter((t) => t.id !== id) : all));
  }
}
```

`services.ts`:

```ts
import { createContext } from "@lit/context";
import { type Api, createApi } from "./api/client";
import { createHttp } from "./api/http";
import { type OpenSse, openSse } from "./api/sse";
import { Router } from "./router";
import { ThemeStore } from "./state/theme";
import { ToastStore } from "./state/toasts";

export interface Services {
  api: Api;
  router: Router;
  theme: ThemeStore;
  toasts: ToastStore;
  open: OpenSse;
}

export const servicesContext = createContext<Services>(Symbol.for("dm-services"));

/** What a 401 'unauthorized' does. Task 183 replaces it with `auth.expired()`. */
export const hooks = { unauthorized: (): void => {} };

export interface ServiceOverrides extends Partial<Services> {
  fetch?: typeof fetch;
  xhr?: () => XMLHttpRequest;
}

/** The one composition root of the UI: everything else receives its collaborators from here. */
export function createServices(o: ServiceOverrides = {}): Services {
  const onUnauthorized = () => hooks.unauthorized();
  const http = createHttp({ ...(o.fetch && { fetch: o.fetch }), onUnauthorized });
  const api = o.api ?? createApi(http, { ...(o.xhr && { xhr: o.xhr }), onUnauthorized });
  return {
    api,
    router: o.router ?? new Router(),
    theme: o.theme ?? new ThemeStore(),
    toasts: o.toasts ?? new ToastStore(),
    open: o.open ?? ((opts) => openSse({ ...(o.fetch && { fetch: o.fetch }), ...opts })),
  };
}
```

`testing/mount.ts`:

```ts
import { ContextProvider } from "@lit/context";
import { type Services, servicesContext } from "../services";

/** Mounts `tag` under a services provider, as `dm-app` does, and waits for its first render. */
export async function mountWith<T extends HTMLElement>(
  services: Services,
  tag: string,
  props: Record<string, unknown> = {},
): Promise<T> {
  const host = document.createElement("div");
  new ContextProvider(host, { context: servicesContext, initialValue: services });
  const el = document.createElement(tag) as T;
  Object.assign(el, props);
  host.append(el);
  document.body.append(host);
  await (el as unknown as { updateComplete?: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;
  return el;
}
```

`state/services-controller.ts` (how every later component reaches the services and the stores it renders from):

```ts
import { ContextConsumer } from "@lit/context";
import type { ReactiveController, ReactiveControllerHost } from "lit";
import { type Services, servicesContext } from "../services";
import type { Store } from "./store";

/**
 * Finds the services through the context and re-renders the host when any of the stores `pick` names changes. Use it as
 * `private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.sessions])` and read `this.ctx.services` in `render`.
 */
export class ServicesController implements ReactiveController {
  services: Services | undefined;
  private offs: (() => void)[] = [];
  constructor(
    private readonly host: ReactiveControllerHost & HTMLElement,
    private readonly pick: (s: Services) => Store<unknown>[] = () => [],
  ) {
    host.addController(this);
    new ContextConsumer(host, { context: servicesContext, subscribe: true, callback: (svc) => this.bind(svc) });
  }
  private bind(svc: Services): void {
    this.unbind();
    this.services = svc;
    for (const store of this.pick(svc)) this.offs.push(store.subscribe(() => this.host.requestUpdate()));
    this.host.requestUpdate();
  }
  private unbind(): void {
    for (const off of this.offs) off();
    this.offs = [];
  }
  hostDisconnected(): void {
    this.unbind();
  }
}
```

`components/dm-app.ts`:

```ts
import { ContextProvider } from "@lit/context";
import { LitElement, css } from "lit";
import { html, unsafeStatic } from "lit/static-html.js";
import { parseRoute } from "../router";
import { type Services, createServices, servicesContext } from "../services";
import { StoreController } from "../state/store";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import { VIEWS } from "../views";
import "./dm-not-found";
import "./dm-toasts";

/** The root: provides the services, follows the router and draws the view of the current route. */
export class DmApp extends LitElement {
  static properties = { services: { attribute: false } };
  static styles = [baseStyles, css`:host { display: block; height: 100%; }`];
  declare services: Services;
  private provider: ContextProvider<typeof servicesContext> | undefined;
  private route: StoreController<ReturnType<Services["router"]["get"]>> | undefined;

  connectedCallback() {
    this.services ??= createServices();
    // Controllers are created before super.connectedCallback(), so Lit connects them in the same call.
    this.provider ??= new ContextProvider(this, { context: servicesContext, initialValue: this.services });
    this.route ??= new StoreController(this, this.services.router);
    super.connectedCallback();
    this.services.router.start();
    this.services.theme.apply();
    this.addEventListener("click", this.onClick);
  }
  disconnectedCallback() {
    this.removeEventListener("click", this.onClick);
    this.services.router.stop();
    super.disconnectedCallback();
  }
  /** A plain click on a same-origin link navigates inside the app; modified clicks, other targets and other origins stay the browser's. */
  private readonly onClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
    if (e.defaultPrevented || e.button !== 0 || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.altKey) return;
    const a = e.composedPath().find((n): n is HTMLAnchorElement => n instanceof HTMLAnchorElement);
    if (!a || a.target !== "" || a.hasAttribute("download")) return;
    const url = new URL(a.href, location.href);
    if (url.origin !== location.origin) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    this.services.router.navigate(parseRoute(url.pathname, url.search));
  };
  render() {
    const route = this.route?.value ?? this.services.router.get();
    const tag = unsafeStatic(VIEWS[route.name] ?? "dm-not-found"); // the tag comes from our own table, never from the URL
    return html`<${tag}></${tag}><dm-toasts></dm-toasts>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-app", DmApp);
```

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

export class DmToasts extends LitElement {
  static properties = {};
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { position: fixed; right: 24px; bottom: 24px; z-index: 10; display: grid; gap: 8px; max-width: min(420px, calc(100vw - 32px)); }
      [data-toast] { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; padding: 10px 10px 10px 14px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); background: var(--dm-surface); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-md); font-size: 13.5px; }
      .lead { font-size: 18px; }
      [data-kind="ok"] .lead { color: var(--dm-ok); }
      [data-kind="err"] .lead { color: var(--dm-err); }
      .text { flex: 1; min-width: 0; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
    `,
  ];
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.toasts]);
  render() {
    const toasts = this.ctx.services?.toasts;
    return html`${(toasts?.get() ?? []).map(
      (t) => html`<div data-toast data-kind=${t.kind} role=${t.kind === "err" ? "alert" : "status"}>
        <dm-icon class="lead" name=${t.kind === "ok" ? "check-circle" : "warning-circle"}></dm-icon>
        <span class="text">${t.text}</span>
        ${t.action ? html`<button class="btn ghost" @click=${() => { t.action?.run(); toasts?.dismiss(t.id); }}>${t.action.label}</button>` : null}
        <button class="btn icon" aria-label="Dismiss" @click=${() => toasts?.dismiss(t.id)}><dm-icon name="x"></dm-icon></button>
      </div>`,
    )}`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-toasts", DmToasts);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `http/staticFiles.ts`.**

```ts
import { createReadStream, existsSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { realpath, stat } from "node:fs/promises";
import { extname, join, resolve, sep } from "node:path";
import { StartupConfigError } from "../startupError";
import { type Handler, sendError } from "./respond";

/** The policy of the web app: everything from this origin, no inline script or style, no remote font, image or frame. */
export const WEB_CSP =
  "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data:; font-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'";

const TYPES: Record<string, string> = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
  ".js": "text/javascript; charset=utf-8",
  ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
  ".json": "application/json",
  ".map": "application/json",
  ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
  ".png": "image/png",
  ".ico": "image/x-icon",
  ".woff2": "font/woff2",
  ".txt": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
  ".webmanifest": "application/manifest+json",
};

/** `DOCMIND_WEB_DIR`: unset or empty means the server serves the API only; otherwise a directory with an index.html. */
export function loadWebDir(raw: string | undefined): string | undefined {
  if (raw === undefined || raw === "") return undefined;
  const dir = resolve(raw);
  const ok = existsSync(dir) && statSync(dir).isDirectory() && existsSync(join(dir, "index.html"));
  if (!ok) throw new StartupConfigError("DOCMIND_WEB_DIR must be a directory that holds index.html");
  return dir;
}

/** Serves the built app: files by path, and `index.html` for any path with no extension (a client route). GET and HEAD. */
export function staticHandler(dir: string): Handler {
  const root = resolve(dir);
  return async (req, res) => {
    let rel: string;
    try {
      rel = decodeURIComponent(new URL(req.url ?? "/", "http://localhost").pathname);
    } catch {
      return sendError(res, 400, "bad_request", "invalid path");
    }
    if (rel.includes("\u0000")) return sendError(res, 400, "bad_request", "invalid path");
    // A dotfile or dot-directory anywhere in the path is never served.
    if (rel.split("/").some((part) => part.startsWith("."))) return sendError(res, 404, "not_found", "no such file");
    const isAsset = extname(rel) !== "";
    const wanted = resolve(root, `.${rel}`);
    if (wanted !== root && !wanted.startsWith(root + sep)) return sendError(res, 404, "not_found", "no such file");
    const file = isAsset ? wanted : join(root, "index.html"); // a path with no extension is a client route
    try {
      // The real path must stay inside the real root: a symlink in the directory cannot lead out of it.
      const real = await realpath(file);
      const realRoot = await realpath(root);
      if (!real.startsWith(realRoot + sep)) throw new Error("outside");
      const info = await stat(real);
      if (!info.isFile()) throw new Error("not a file");
      const type = TYPES[extname(real)] ?? "application/octet-stream";
      const page = file === join(root, "index.html");
      res.writeHead(200, {
        "content-type": type,
        "content-length": info.size,
        "cache-control": page ? "no-cache" : rel.startsWith("/assets/") ? "public, max-age=31536000, immutable" : "no-cache",
        "content-security-policy": WEB_CSP,
        "x-content-type-options": "nosniff",
        "referrer-policy": "no-referrer",
      });
      if (req.method === "HEAD") return void res.end();
      await new Promise<void>((done) => {
        const body = createReadStream(real);
        body.on("error", () => { res.destroy(); done(); });
        res.on("close", () => { body.destroy(); done(); });
        body.pipe(res);
      });
    } catch {
      sendError(res, 404, "not_found", "no such file");
    }
  };
}
```

### Task 183: `AuthStore`, the sign-in and sign-up forms, and the route guard

**Spec:** §9 item 37 (sign-in, sign-up), §7 (`dm-auth-form`). Decisions D2, D5; Finding U11.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/state/auth.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-auth-form.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/services.ts` (add `auth`; `hooks.unauthorized = () => auth.expired()`), `apps/web/src/views.ts` (`sign-in`, `sign-up`), `apps/web/src/components/dm-app.ts` (the guard), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/state/auth.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-auth-form.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-app.test.ts` (guard cases)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Api.me/login/logout/signUp`, `ApiError`, `Router`, `ToastStore`.
- Produces: `AuthState`, `AuthStore` (catalogue); `authErrorText(err: unknown): string`; `<dm-auth-form mode="sign-in" | "sign-up">`. Guard rule in `dm-app`: while `auth.status === "unknown"` draw nothing but a busy indicator and call `auth.bootstrap()` once; `anonymous` and a route other than `sign-in`, `sign-up`, `account-deleted` goes to `/sign-in` (replace); `signed-in` on `sign-in` or `sign-up` goes to `/chats` (replace).

`auth.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { AuthStore, authErrorText } from "./auth";

const ME = { userId: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10", tenantId: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", email: "a@example.com" };
const api = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
  ({ me: vi.fn(async () => ME), login: vi.fn(async () => ME), logout: vi.fn(async () => {}), signUp: vi.fn(async () => {}), ...over }) as never;

describe("AuthStore", () => {
  it("bootstrap: a live session is signed-in, a 401 is anonymous, and a dead network stays unknown-free (anonymous)", async () => {
    const live = new AuthStore(api());
    await live.bootstrap();
    expect(live.get()).toEqual({ status: "signed-in", me: ME });
    const gone = new AuthStore(api({ me: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(401, "unauthorized"); }) }));
    await gone.bootstrap();
    expect(gone.get()).toEqual({ status: "anonymous" });
    const down = new AuthStore(api({ me: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(0, "network"); }) }));
    await down.bootstrap();
    expect(down.get()).toEqual({ status: "anonymous" });
  });

  it("login signs in; a refused login stays anonymous and rethrows for the form", async () => {
    const store = new AuthStore(api({ login: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(401, "invalid_credentials"); }) }));
    await store.bootstrap();
    await expect(store.login({ email: "a@example.com", password: "x" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "invalid_credentials" });
    expect(store.get()).toEqual({ status: "anonymous" });
    const ok = new AuthStore(api());
    await ok.login({ email: "a@example.com", password: "x" });
    expect(ok.get()).toEqual({ status: "signed-in", me: ME });
  });

  it("signUp then logs in with the same credentials (sign-up answers 202 whether or not the address exists)", async () => {
    const a = api();
    const store = new AuthStore(a);
    await store.signUp({ email: "a@example.com", password: "a long enough password" });
    expect((a as never as { signUp: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).signUp).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect((a as never as { login: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).login).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ email: "a@example.com", password: "a long enough password" });
    expect(store.get().status).toBe("signed-in");
  });

  it("logout clears the state even when the server call fails; expired() and signedOut() go anonymous", async () => {
    const store = new AuthStore(api({ logout: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(0, "network"); }) }));
    await store.login({ email: "a@example.com", password: "x" });
    await store.logout();
    expect(store.get()).toEqual({ status: "anonymous" });
    await store.login({ email: "a@example.com", password: "x" });
    store.expired();
    expect(store.get()).toEqual({ status: "anonymous" });
  });
});

describe("authErrorText", () => {
  it("answers with fixed sentences by status and code, and the wait time for a throttle", () => {
    expect(authErrorText(new ApiError(401, "invalid_credentials"))).toBe("The email or password is not right.");
    expect(authErrorText(new ApiError(429, "too_many_requests", 12))).toBe("Too many attempts. Try again in 12 seconds.");
    expect(authErrorText(new ApiError(429, "too_many_requests"))).toBe("Too many attempts. Try again in a minute.");
    expect(authErrorText(new ApiError(400, "invalid_request"))).toBe("Check the email and password and try again.");
    expect(authErrorText(new ApiError(0, "network"))).toBe("Could not reach the server. Check your connection.");
    expect(authErrorText(new ApiError(500, "internal"))).toBe("Something went wrong. Try again.");
    expect(authErrorText(new Error("sentinel-title-0123456789"))).toBe("Something went wrong. Try again.");
  });
});
```

`dm-auth-form.test.ts` (uses `mountWith` with a services object built from `createServices({ api })`, where `api` is the fake from above; `createServices` must accept an `auth` override, which this task adds):

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { AuthStore } from "../state/auth";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import "./dm-auth-form";

const ME = { userId: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10", tenantId: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", email: "a@example.com" };
function setup(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const api = { me: vi.fn(async () => ME), login: vi.fn(async () => ME), logout: vi.fn(async () => {}), signUp: vi.fn(async () => {}), ...over } as never;
  const auth = new AuthStore(api);
  const services = createServices({ api, auth, fetch: (async () => new Response("{}")) as never });
  return { services, auth, api };
}
const root = (el: Element) => el.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot;
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => root(el).querySelector(sel) as T;
const settle = (el: Element) => (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;
async function fill(el: HTMLElement, email: string, password: string) {
  q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[type=email]").value = email;
  q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[name=password]").value = password;
  q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[type=email]").dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
  q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[name=password]").dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
}

describe("dm-auth-form: sign in", () => {
  it("labels its fields, submits the credentials, and goes to the chats", async () => {
    const { services, api } = setup();
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-auth-form", { mode: "sign-in" });
    expect(q(el, "label[for=email]").textContent).toBe("Email");
    expect(q(el, "h1").textContent).toBe("Sign in");
    await fill(el, "a@example.com", "pw");
    q<HTMLFormElement>(el, "form").dispatchEvent(new Event("submit", { cancelable: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "chat" }));
    expect((api as never as { login: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).login).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ email: "a@example.com", password: "pw" });
  });

  it("shows a fixed sentence for a wrong password, marks the field invalid, and never the server's text", async () => {
    const { services } = setup({ login: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(401, "invalid_credentials"); }) });
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-auth-form", { mode: "sign-in" });
    await fill(el, "a@example.com", "nope");
    q<HTMLFormElement>(el, "form").dispatchEvent(new Event("submit", { cancelable: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[role=alert]")?.textContent).toContain("The email or password is not right."));
    expect(q(el, "input[name=password]").getAttribute("aria-invalid")).toBe("true");
    expect(services.router.route).not.toEqual({ name: "chat" });
  });

  it("lets the password be shown and hidden, with a name on the toggle", async () => {
    const { services } = setup();
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-auth-form", { mode: "sign-in" });
    const toggle = q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[aria-label='Show password']");
    toggle.click();
    await settle(el);
    expect(q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[name=password]").type).toBe("text");
    expect(q(el, "button[aria-label='Hide password']")).not.toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("dm-auth-form: sign up", () => {

    const { services, api } = setup();
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

`state/auth.ts`:

```ts
import type { LoginRequest, MeResponse, SignUpRequest } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { Api } from "../api/client";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { Store } from "./store";

export type AuthState =
  | { status: "unknown" }
  | { status: "anonymous" }
  | { status: "signed-in"; me: MeResponse };

/** Fixed sentences by status and code. The server's own message is never shown: it could echo what was typed. */
export function authErrorText(err: unknown): string {
  if (err instanceof ApiError) {
    if (err.status === 0) return "Could not reach the server. Check your connection.";
    if (err.status === 401) return "The email or password is not right.";
    if (err.status === 429)
      return err.retryAfter !== undefined ? `Too many attempts. Try again in ${err.retryAfter} seconds.` : "Too many attempts. Try again in a minute.";
    if (err.status === 400) return "Check the email and password and try again.";
  }
  return "Something went wrong. Try again.";
}

export class AuthStore extends Store<AuthState> {
  constructor(private readonly api: Pick<Api, "me" | "login" | "logout" | "signUp">) {
    super({ status: "unknown" });
  }
  /** Asks the server who this browser is. Any failure means anonymous: the sign-in screen is the safe place to land. */
  async bootstrap(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      this.set({ status: "signed-in", me: await this.api.me() });
    } catch {
      this.set({ status: "anonymous" });
    }
  }
  async login(req: LoginRequest): Promise<void> {
    this.set({ status: "signed-in", me: await this.api.login(req) }); // a refusal throws and leaves the state alone
  }
  /** Sign-up answers 202 whether or not the address exists, so the account is only known to work once the sign-in succeeds. */
  async signUp(req: SignUpRequest): Promise<void> {
    await this.api.signUp(req);
    await this.login({ email: req.email, password: req.password });
  }
  async logout(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.api.logout();
    } catch {
      // the cookie may still be valid, but this browser is done: the sign-in screen, and a later 401 if it was not cleared
    }
    this.signedOut();
  }
  /** A 401 'unauthorized' came back from any call: the session ended. */
  expired(): void {
    this.signedOut();
  }
  signedOut(): void {
    this.set({ status: "anonymous" });
  }
}
```

`components/dm-auth-form.ts` (the layout follows the mocks: a form column of at most 400px; the marketing panel of the mocks is not built):

```ts
import { SignUpRequest } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { authErrorText } from "../state/auth";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

export class DmAuthForm extends LitElement {
  static properties = {
    mode: { type: String },
    email: { state: true },
    password: { state: true },
    shown: { state: true },
    busy: { state: true },
    problem: { state: true },
  };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: grid; place-items: center; min-height: 100%; padding: 32px 16px; }
      form { width: min(400px, 100%); display: grid; gap: 16px; }
      h1 { font-size: 30px; margin-bottom: 4px; }
      .field { position: relative; }
      .reveal { position: absolute; right: 2px; top: 22px; }
      .hint { font-size: 12px; color: var(--dm-muted); margin-top: 4px; }
      .btn.primary { min-height: 40px; }
      p.switch { color: var(--dm-muted); }
    `,
  ];
  declare mode: "sign-in" | "sign-up";
  declare email: string;
  declare password: string;
  declare shown: boolean;
  declare busy: boolean;
  declare problem: string | undefined;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this);
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.mode = "sign-in";
    this.email = "";
    this.password = "";
    this.shown = false;
    this.busy = false;
  }
  private async submit(e: Event) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const auth = this.ctx.services?.auth;
    const router = this.ctx.services?.router;
    if (!auth || !router || this.busy) return;
    this.problem = undefined;
    if (this.mode === "sign-up") {
      const ok = SignUpRequest.safeParse({ email: this.email, password: this.password });
      if (!ok.success) {
        this.problem = this.password.length < 12 ? "At least 12 characters." : "Enter a valid email address.";
        return;
      }
    }
    this.busy = true;
    try {
      if (this.mode === "sign-up") await auth.signUp({ email: this.email, password: this.password });
      else await auth.login({ email: this.email, password: this.password });
      this.password = "";
      router.navigate({ name: "chat" }, { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      this.problem =
        this.mode === "sign-up" && (err as { status?: number }).status === 401
          ? "We could not sign you in. If you already have an account, sign in instead."
          : authErrorText(err);
    } finally {
      this.busy = false;
    }
  }
  render() {
    const up = this.mode === "sign-up";
    const invalid = this.problem !== undefined;
    return html`
      <form @submit=${this.submit} novalidate>
        <div>
          <h1>${up ? "Create your account" : "Sign in"}</h1>
          <p class="muted">${up ? "Your documents stay private to you." : "Welcome back."}</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label for="email">Email</label>
          <input class="input" id="email" name="email" type="email" autocomplete="email" required aria-invalid=${invalid} .value=${this.email} @input=${(e: Event) => (this.email = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value)} />
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label for="password">Password</label>
          <input class="input" id="password" name="password" type=${this.shown ? "text" : "password"} autocomplete=${up ? "new-password" : "current-password"} required aria-invalid=${invalid} .value=${this.password} @input=${(e: Event) => (this.password = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value)} />
          <button type="button" class="btn icon reveal" aria-label=${this.shown ? "Hide password" : "Show password"} @click=${() => (this.shown = !this.shown)}><dm-icon name=${this.shown ? "eye-slash" : "eye"}></dm-icon></button>
          ${up ? html`<div class="hint">At least 12 characters</div>` : null}
        </div>
        ${this.problem ? html`<div class="error" role="alert"><dm-icon name="warning-circle"></dm-icon><span>${this.problem}</span></div>` : null}
        <button class="btn primary" type="submit" ?disabled=${this.busy}>${this.busy ? "One moment..." : up ? "Create account" : "Sign in"}</button>
        <p class="switch">${up ? html`Already have an account? <a href="/sign-in">Sign in</a>` : html`New to DocMind? <a href="/sign-up">Create an account</a>`}</p>
      </form>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-auth-form", DmAuthForm);
```

  (The reveal button needs the `eye` and `eye-slash` icons: add them to `icons.ts` (`eye.svg`, `eye-slash.svg`) in this task and raise the icon test's minimum from 30 to 37.)

  `services.ts`: add `auth: AuthStore` to `Services` and to `createServices` (`const auth = o.auth ?? new AuthStore(api)`; `hooks.unauthorized = () => auth.expired()` inside `createServices`, after the stores exist). `views.ts`: `"sign-in": "dm-auth-form"`, `"sign-up": "dm-auth-form"`; `dm-app.render` passes the mode: `html\`<${tag} mode=${route.name}></${tag}>\``. `components/index.ts` imports `./dm-auth-form`.

  The guard in `dm-app`. Replace its `connectedCallback`, `render` and add `decide` and `updated` (the rest of the file from Task 181 stays):

```ts
  private authState: StoreController<AuthState> | undefined;

  connectedCallback() {
    this.services ??= createServices();
    this.provider ??= new ContextProvider(this, { context: servicesContext, initialValue: this.services });
    this.route ??= new StoreController(this, this.services.router);
    this.authState ??= new StoreController(this, this.services.auth);
    super.connectedCallback();
    this.services.router.start();
    this.services.theme.apply();
    this.addEventListener("click", this.onClick);
    if (this.services.auth.get().status === "unknown") void this.services.auth.bootstrap(); // never rejects
  }

  /** What to draw and where to go, from the session and the route alone (pure). */
  private decide(): { wait: boolean; to?: Route } {
    const state = this.services.auth.get();
    if (state.status === "unknown") return { wait: true };
    const name = this.services.router.get().name;
    const open = name === "sign-in" || name === "sign-up" || name === "account-deleted";
    if (state.status === "anonymous" && !open) return { wait: false, to: { name: "sign-in" } };
    if (state.status === "signed-in" && (name === "sign-in" || name === "sign-up")) return { wait: false, to: { name: "chat" } };
    return { wait: false };
  }

  updated() {
    const { to } = this.decide();
    if (to) this.services.router.navigate(to, { replace: true });
  }

  render() {
    if (this.decide().wait) return html`<div class="boot" aria-busy="true"></div><dm-toasts></dm-toasts>`;
    const route = this.route?.value ?? this.services.router.get();
    const tag = unsafeStatic(VIEWS[route.name] ?? "dm-not-found"); // the tag comes from our own table, never from the URL
    return route.name === "sign-in" || route.name === "sign-up"
      ? html`<${tag} mode=${route.name}></${tag}><dm-toasts></dm-toasts>`
      : html`<${tag}></${tag}><dm-toasts></dm-toasts>`;
  }
```

  with `import type { Route } from "../router"; import type { AuthState } from "../state/auth";` added. The guard test helper in `dm-app.test.ts`:

```ts
const ME = { userId: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10", tenantId: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", email: "a@example.com" };
function servicesWithAuth(path: string, over: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const win = { location: { pathname: path, search: "" }, history: { pushState: (_s: unknown, _t: string, u: string) => { win.location.pathname = u; }, replaceState: (_s: unknown, _t: string, u: string) => { win.location.pathname = u; } }, addEventListener: () => {}, removeEventListener: () => {} };
  const api = { me: async () => ME, login: async () => ME, logout: async () => {}, signUp: async () => {}, ...over } as never;
  return createServices({ router: new Router(win as never), api, auth: new AuthStore(api) });
}
```

Because the app now draws a busy marker until the session is known, the Task 181 cases that look for `dm-not-found` or `dm-toasts` right after mounting wait for it: wrap those first assertions in `await vi.waitFor(() => expect(...))`.
