### Task 184a: `dm-dialog`, an accessible modal

**Spec:** §9 item 37 ("Delete my account"), `ui-mocks.md` §4 Dialogs ("Not mocked ... follow the component sheet's existing styles"). Decision D7.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-dialog.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-dialog.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `<dm-dialog heading="..." ?open ?persistent>`: default slot is the body, `slot="actions"` the buttons. It sets `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal="true"` and `aria-labelledby` on its panel, moves focus into the panel on open (the element with `autofocus`, else the first focusable), keeps Tab and Shift+Tab inside it, closes on Escape and (unless `persistent`) on a backdrop click by firing `dm-close` (it never closes itself: the owner sets `open` to false), and gives focus back to what had it when it opened. Also exports `deepActiveElement(): Element | null`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `dm-dialog.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { deepActiveElement } from "./dm-dialog";
import "./dm-dialog";

type Dlg = HTMLElement & { updateComplete: Promise<unknown>; open: boolean };
async function mount(opts: { persistent?: boolean } = {}) {
  const opener = document.createElement("button");
  opener.textContent = "open";
  document.body.append(opener);
  opener.focus();
  const el = document.createElement("dm-dialog") as Dlg;
  el.setAttribute("heading", "Delete chat?");
  if (opts.persistent) el.setAttribute("persistent", "");
  el.innerHTML = `<p>Body</p><input id="first" aria-label="Password"><button slot="actions" id="cancel">Cancel</button><button slot="actions" id="go">Delete</button>`;
  document.body.append(el);
  await el.updateComplete;
  return { el, opener };
}
const panel = (el: Dlg) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("[role=dialog]") as HTMLElement;

describe("dm-dialog", () => {
  it("draws nothing while closed, and a labelled modal panel when open", async () => {
    const { el } = await mount();
    expect(panel(el)).toBeNull();
    el.open = true;
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(panel(el).getAttribute("aria-modal")).toBe("true");
    const labelled = panel(el).getAttribute("aria-labelledby") as string;
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.getElementById(labelled)?.textContent).toBe("Delete chat?");
  });

  it("moves focus in, wraps Tab at both ends, and gives focus back when it closes", async () => {
    const { el, opener } = await mount();
    el.open = true;
    await el.updateComplete;
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect((deepActiveElement() as HTMLElement | null)?.id).toBe("first"));
    const go = el.querySelector("#go") as HTMLButtonElement;
    go.focus();
    panel(el).dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "Tab", bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
    expect((deepActiveElement() as HTMLElement).id).toBe("first");
    panel(el).dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "Tab", shiftKey: true, bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
    expect((deepActiveElement() as HTMLElement).id).toBe("go");
    el.open = false;
    await el.updateComplete;
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(deepActiveElement()).toBe(opener));
  });

  it("asks to close on Escape and on a backdrop click, but not on a click inside the panel or for a persistent one", async () => {
    const { el } = await mount();
    el.open = true;
    await el.updateComplete;
    const closes = vi.fn();
    el.addEventListener("dm-close", closes);
    panel(el).dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "Escape", bubbles: true }));
    expect(closes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    (el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(".backdrop") as HTMLElement).dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("click", { bubbles: true }));
    expect(closes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    panel(el).dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("click", { bubbles: true }));
    expect(closes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(el.open).toBe(true); // it only asks
    const sticky = await mount({ persistent: true });
    sticky.el.open = true;
    await sticky.el.updateComplete;
    const stickyCloses = vi.fn();
    sticky.el.addEventListener("dm-close", stickyCloses);
    (sticky.el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(".backdrop") as HTMLElement).dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("click", { bubbles: true }));
    expect(stickyCloses).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    panel(sticky.el).dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "Escape", bubbles: true }));
    expect(stickyCloses).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1); // Escape always asks
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `dm-dialog.ts`.**

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html, type PropertyValues } from "lit";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";

const FOCUSABLE = "a[href], button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex='-1'])";

/** The element that has focus, looking through open shadow roots. */
export function deepActiveElement(): Element | null {
  let el: Element | null = document.activeElement;
  while (el?.shadowRoot?.activeElement) el = el.shadowRoot.activeElement;
  return el;
}

let ids = 0;

export class DmDialog extends LitElement {
  static properties = { heading: { type: String }, open: { type: Boolean, reflect: true }, persistent: { type: Boolean } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      .backdrop { position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 20; display: grid; place-items: center; padding: 16px; background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--dm-neutral-900) 50%, transparent); }
      .panel { width: min(440px, 100%); max-height: 100%; overflow: auto; padding: 22px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-lg); background: var(--dm-surface); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-lg); display: grid; gap: 14px; }
      h2 { font-size: 20px; }
      .body { font-size: 14px; display: grid; gap: 12px; }
      .actions { display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; gap: 8px; }
    `,
  ];
  declare heading: string;
  declare open: boolean;
  declare persistent: boolean;
  private readonly titleId = `dm-dialog-title-${++ids}`;
  private opener: Element | null = null;

  constructor() {
    super();
    this.heading = "";
    this.open = false;
    this.persistent = false;
  }

  private close() {
    this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-close", { bubbles: true, composed: true }));
  }
  private focusables(): HTMLElement[] {
    const own = [...(this.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE) ?? [])];
    const slotted = [...(this.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll("slot") ?? [])].flatMap((s) =>
      s.assignedElements({ flatten: true }).flatMap((e) => [e as HTMLElement, ...e.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE)]).filter((e) => e.matches(FOCUSABLE)),
    );
    return [...slotted, ...own].filter((e) => !e.hasAttribute("disabled"));
  }
  private onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    if (e.key === "Escape") {
      e.stopPropagation();
      return this.close();
    }
    if (e.key !== "Tab") return;
    const items = this.focusables();
    const first = items[0];
    const last = items[items.length - 1];
    if (!first || !last) return e.preventDefault();
    const current = deepActiveElement();
    if (e.shiftKey && (current === first || !items.includes(current as HTMLElement))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      last.focus();
    } else if (!e.shiftKey && (current === last || !items.includes(current as HTMLElement))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      first.focus();
    }
  }
  protected updated(changed: PropertyValues<this>) {
    if (!changed.has("open")) return;
    if (this.open) {
      this.opener = deepActiveElement();
      const target = this.focusables().find((e) => e.hasAttribute("autofocus")) ?? this.focusables()[0];
      (target ?? this.shadowRoot?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".panel"))?.focus();
    } else if (changed.get("open") === true) {
      (this.opener as HTMLElement | null)?.focus?.();
      this.opener = null;
    }
  }
  render() {
    if (!this.open) return html``;
    return html`
      <div class="backdrop" @click=${() => !this.persistent && this.close()}>
        <div class="panel" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby=${this.titleId} tabindex="-1" @click=${(e: Event) => e.stopPropagation()} @keydown=${this.onKey}>
          <h2 id=${this.titleId}>${this.heading}</h2>
          <div class="body"><slot></slot></div>
          <div class="actions"><slot name="actions"></slot></div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-dialog", DmDialog);
```

  **Probe.** The default slot also receives the `slot="actions"` buttons' siblings only; `slot="actions"` elements go to the named slot, so `focusables()` visits both. If happy-dom's `assignedElements` returns nothing for a slot, collect `[...this.children]` instead (the dialog's light-DOM children) and filter by `matches(FOCUSABLE)` plus their descendants; record it in the PR body.

```ts
const RECEIPT = "A".repeat(43);

async function setup(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const api = { me: async () => ME, login: async () => ME, logout: vi.fn(async () => {}), signUp: async () => {}, deleteAccount: vi.fn(async () => ({ erasure: "accepted", receipt: RECEIPT })), ...over } as never;

}
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as T;
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import "./dm-account-deleted";

const RECEIPT = "B".repeat(43);
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as T;

function setup(receipt: string | undefined, erasureStatus: () => Promise<unknown>) {
  const api = { erasureStatus: vi.fn(erasureStatus) } as never;
  const services = createServices({ api, fetch: (async () => new Response("{}")) as never });
  if (receipt) services.receipt.set(receipt);
  return { services, api };
}

describe("dm-account-deleted", () => {
  it("shows the receipt once with a copy button and the statement that it is not kept", async () => {
    const { services } = setup(RECEIPT, async () => ({ state: "pending" }));
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-account-deleted");
    expect(q(el, "[data-receipt]").textContent).toBe(RECEIPT);
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("shown once");
    const writeText = vi.fn(async () => {});
    Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", { value: { writeText }, configurable: true });
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-copy]").click();
    expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith(RECEIPT);
  });

  it("checks the state with the receipt and says in words whether the erasure is done", async () => {
    const answers = [{ state: "pending" }, { state: "done" }];
    const { services, api } = setup(RECEIPT, async () => answers.shift());
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-account-deleted");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-check]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[data-state]").textContent).toContain("In progress"));
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-check]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[data-state]").textContent).toContain("Done"));
    expect((api as never as { erasureStatus: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).erasureStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith(RECEIPT);
  });

  it("handles an unknown receipt and a dead connection without showing server text", async () => {
    const { services } = setup(RECEIPT, async () => { throw new ApiError(404, "not_found"); });
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-account-deleted");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-check]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[data-state]").textContent).toContain("No deletion matches this receipt."));
    const down = setup(RECEIPT, async () => { throw new ApiError(0, "network"); });
    const el2 = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(down.services, "dm-account-deleted");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el2, "button[data-check]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el2, "[data-state]").textContent).toContain("Could not reach the server."));
  });

  it("without a receipt (a reload) says the page cannot show it, and offers a way back", async () => {
    const { services } = setup(undefined, async () => ({ state: "done" }));
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-account-deleted");
    expect(q(el, "[data-receipt]")).toBeNull();
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("no longer on this page");
    expect(q<HTMLAnchorElement>(el, "a[href='/sign-up']")).not.toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { authErrorText } from "../state/auth";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import type { ThemeChoice } from "../state/theme";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-dialog";
import "./dm-icon";

function deleteErrorText(err: unknown): string {
  if (err instanceof ApiError) {
    if (err.status === 401) return "The password is not right.";
    if (err.status === 409) return "Your account is already being deleted.";
    if (err.status === 429 || err.status === 0) return authErrorText(err);
  }
  return "Something went wrong. Nothing was deleted. Try again.";
}

export class DmSettings extends LitElement {
  static properties = { confirming: { state: true }, password: { state: true }, busy: { state: true }, problem: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; padding: 28px 40px; max-width: 720px; }
      h1 { font-size: 26px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
      section { padding: 16px 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); display: grid; gap: 10px; }
      h2 { font-size: 16px; }
      .row { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 12px; }
      select { min-height: 36px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); background: var(--dm-surface); color: var(--dm-text); border: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); padding: 0 8px; font: inherit; }
    `,
  ];
  declare confirming: boolean;
  declare password: string;
  declare busy: boolean;
  declare problem: string | undefined;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.auth, s.theme]);
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.confirming = false;
    this.password = "";
    this.busy = false;
  }
  private async signOut() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc) return;
    await svc.auth.logout();
    svc.router.navigate({ name: "sign-in" }, { replace: true });
  }
  private async confirmDelete(e: Event) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc || this.busy || this.password === "") return;
    this.busy = true;
    this.problem = undefined;
    try {
      const done = await svc.api.deleteAccount({ password: this.password });
      this.password = "";
      svc.receipt.set(done.receipt); // memory only
      svc.auth.signedOut();
      svc.router.navigate({ name: "account-deleted" }, { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      this.problem = deleteErrorText(err);
    } finally {
      this.busy = false;
    }
  }
  private closeDialog() {
    this.confirming = false;
    this.password = "";
    this.problem = undefined;
  }
  render() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const state = svc?.auth.get();
    return html`
      <h1>Settings</h1>
      <section aria-labelledby="account">
        <h2 id="account">Account</h2>
        <div class="row"><span>Signed in as <strong data-email>${state?.status === "signed-in" ? state.me.email : ""}</strong></span>
          <button class="btn secondary" data-sign-out @click=${this.signOut}><dm-icon name="sign-out"></dm-icon>Sign out</button></div>
      </section>
      <section aria-labelledby="appearance">
        <h2 id="appearance">Appearance</h2>
        <div class="row"><span>Theme</span>
          <select aria-label="Theme" .value=${svc?.theme.get() ?? "system"} @change=${(e: Event) => svc?.theme.choose((e.target as HTMLSelectElement).value as ThemeChoice)}>
            <option value="system">Match system</option><option value="dark">Dark</option><option value="light">Light</option>
          </select></div>
      </section>
      <section aria-labelledby="danger">
        <h2 id="danger">Delete account</h2>
        <p class="muted">Permanently deletes your account, your documents and your chats.</p>
        <div><button class="btn danger" data-delete @click=${() => (this.confirming = true)}>Delete my account</button></div>
      </section>
      <dm-dialog heading="Delete your account?" ?open=${this.confirming} persistent @dm-close=${this.closeDialog}>
        <form id="delete-form" @submit=${this.confirmDelete}>
          <p>This deletes your account, all your documents and all your chats. This cannot be undone. Your data is made unreadable first and then removed, which can take a little while if an answer is still being written.</p>
          <label for="del-pw">Enter your password to confirm</label>
          <input class="input" id="del-pw" type="password" autocomplete="current-password" autofocus .value=${this.password} @input=${(e: Event) => (this.password = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value)} />
          ${this.problem ? html`<div class="error" role="alert"><dm-icon name="warning-circle"></dm-icon><span>${this.problem}</span></div>` : null}
        </form>
        <button slot="actions" class="btn secondary" data-cancel @click=${this.closeDialog}>Cancel</button>
        <button slot="actions" class="btn danger" data-confirm ?disabled=${this.busy || this.password === ""} @click=${this.confirmDelete}>Delete my account</button>
      </dm-dialog>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-settings", DmSettings);
```

`components/dm-account-deleted.ts`:

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

export class DmAccountDeleted extends LitElement {
  static properties = { stateText: { state: true }, checking: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: grid; place-items: center; min-height: 100%; padding: 32px 16px; }
      main { width: min(520px, 100%); display: grid; gap: 14px; }
      h1 { font-size: 26px; }
      code { display: block; padding: 12px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); background: var(--dm-surface); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-sm); overflow-wrap: anywhere; user-select: all; }
      .row { display: flex; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
    `,
  ];
  declare stateText: string | undefined;
  declare checking: boolean;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.receipt]);
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.checking = false;
  }
  private async copy(receipt: string) {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(receipt);
      this.ctx.services?.toasts.push({ kind: "ok", text: "Receipt copied." });
    } catch {
      this.ctx.services?.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not copy. Select the receipt and copy it." });
    }
  }
  private async check(receipt: string) {
    const api = this.ctx.services?.api;
    if (!api || this.checking) return;
    this.checking = true;
    try {
      const { state } = await api.erasureStatus(receipt);
      this.stateText = state === "done" ? "Done. Your account and its data have been erased." : "In progress. Some of your data may remain until it finishes.";
    } catch (err) {
      this.stateText =
        err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404 ? "No deletion matches this receipt." : err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 0 ? "Could not reach the server. Check your connection." : "Could not check right now. Try again.";
    } finally {
      this.checking = false;
    }
  }
  render() {
    const receipt = this.ctx.services?.receipt.get();
    return html`<main>
      <h1>Your account is being deleted</h1>
      ${receipt
        ? html`<p>Keep this receipt. It is the only way to check that the deletion has finished. It is shown once: it is not stored anywhere, and it is gone when you leave this page.</p>
          <code data-receipt>${receipt}</code>
          <div class="row">
            <button class="btn secondary" data-copy @click=${() => this.copy(receipt)}><dm-icon name="copy"></dm-icon>Copy receipt</button>
            <button class="btn primary" data-check ?disabled=${this.checking} @click=${() => this.check(receipt)}>Check status</button>
          </div>
          <p data-state aria-live="polite">${this.stateText ?? ""}</p>`
        : html`<p>The receipt is no longer on this page, so it cannot be shown again. If you deleted your account, it is already unusable.</p>`}
      <p><a href="/sign-up">Create a new account</a></p>
    </main>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-account-deleted", DmAccountDeleted);
```

  `views.ts`: `settings: "dm-settings"`, `"account-deleted": "dm-account-deleted"`. `components/index.ts`: both imports. Add the `copy` icon check: it is in the 35 (copy ✓), `sign-out` ✓.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { relativeTime } from "./time";

const now = new Date("2026-10-21T15:00:00");
const ago = (ms: number) => new Date(now.getTime() - ms).toISOString();
const MIN = 60_000, H = 60 * MIN, D = 24 * H;

describe("relativeTime", () => {
  it.each([
    [10_000, "Just now"],
    [12 * MIN, "12 min ago"],
    [2 * H, "2 h ago"],
    [20 * H, "Yesterday"],        // 19:00 the day before
    [2 * D, "2 days ago"],
    [6 * D, "6 days ago"],
  ])("%i ms ago is %s", (ms, text) => expect(relativeTime(ago(ms), now)).toBe(text));
  it("a week or more ago is a short date, and the future is just now", () => {
    expect(relativeTime(new Date("2026-09-28T09:00:00").toISOString(), now)).toBe("Sep 28");
    expect(relativeTime(new Date("2026-10-21T16:00:00").toISOString(), now)).toBe("Just now");
  });
});
```

`sessions.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { ChatSessionDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { SessionsStore, groupSessions } from "./sessions";

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-21T15:00:00");
const chat = (n: number, over: Partial<ChatSessionDto> = {}): ChatSessionDto => ({
  id: `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`, title: `t${n}`, scope: "all",
  createdAt: new Date("2026-10-01T10:00:00").toISOString(), lastMessageAt: new Date("2026-10-21T10:00:00").toISOString(), messageCount: 1, ...over,
});

describe("groupSessions", () => {
  it("groups by the last activity, falls back to creation, keeps order and drops empty groups", () => {
    const today = chat(1);
    const week = chat(2, { lastMessageAt: new Date("2026-10-17T10:00:00").toISOString() });
    const old = chat(3, { lastMessageAt: null, createdAt: new Date("2026-08-01T10:00:00").toISOString() });
    expect(groupSessions([today, week, old], NOW).map((g) => [g.label, g.items.map((i) => i.title)])).toEqual([
      ["Today", ["t1"]], ["Previous 7 days", ["t2"]], ["Older", ["t3"]],
    ]);
    expect(groupSessions([today], NOW).map((g) => g.label)).toEqual(["Today"]);
    expect(groupSessions([], NOW)).toEqual([]);
  });
});

function setup(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const timers: { fn: () => void; ms: number; cancelled: boolean }[] = [];
  const api = {
    listChats: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [chat(1), chat(2)], nextCursor: "c1" })),
    createChat: vi.fn(async () => chat(9, { title: null })),
    getChat: vi.fn(async () => chat(2, { messageCount: 4 })),
    patchChat: vi.fn(async () => chat(1, { title: "Renamed" })),
    deleteChat: vi.fn(async () => {}),
    ...over,
  };
  const store = new SessionsStore(api as never, (fn, ms) => { const t = { fn, ms, cancelled: false }; timers.push(t); return () => { t.cancelled = true; }; });
  return { store, api, timers };
}

describe("SessionsStore", () => {
  it("loads the first page, then appends the next with the cursor", async () => {
    const { store, api } = setup();
    await store.load();
    expect(store.get()).toMatchObject({ loaded: true, loading: false, nextCursor: "c1", query: "" });
    expect(store.get().items.map((i) => i.title)).toEqual(["t1", "t2"]);
    api.listChats.mockResolvedValueOnce({ items: [chat(3)], nextCursor: null });
    await store.loadMore();
    expect(api.listChats).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ cursor: "c1" });
    expect(store.get().items.map((i) => i.title)).toEqual(["t1", "t2", "t3"]);
    await store.loadMore(); // no cursor left: no call
    expect(api.listChats).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it("debounces a search and asks the server with q; only the last answer counts", async () => {
    const { store, api, timers } = setup();
    await store.load();
    store.search("rep");
    store.search("report");
    expect(store.get().query).toBe("report");
    expect(timers.map((t) => t.cancelled)).toEqual([true, false]);
    expect(timers[1]?.ms).toBe(250);
    let release: (v: unknown) => void = () => {};
    api.listChats.mockImplementationOnce(() => new Promise((r) => { release = r; }));
    timers[1]?.fn();
    expect(api.listChats).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ q: "report" });
    store.search("other");
    api.listChats.mockResolvedValueOnce({ items: [chat(7)], nextCursor: null });
    timers[2]?.fn();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(store.get().items.map((i) => i.title)).toEqual(["t7"]));
    release({ items: [chat(8)], nextCursor: null }); // the stale answer arrives late and is dropped
    await Promise.resolve();
    expect(store.get().items.map((i) => i.title)).toEqual(["t7"]);
  });

  it("create puts the chat first; rename and remove change the list; touch moves a chat to the top", async () => {
    const { store, api } = setup();
    await store.load();
    const made = await store.create("all");
    expect(api.createChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ scope: "all" });
    expect(store.get().items[0]?.id).toBe(made.id);
    await store.rename(chat(1).id, "Renamed");
    expect(api.patchChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith(chat(1).id, { title: "Renamed" });
    expect(store.get().items.find((i) => i.id === chat(1).id)?.title).toBe("Renamed");
    await store.remove(chat(1).id);
    expect(store.get().items.some((i) => i.id === chat(1).id)).toBe(false);
    await store.touch(chat(2).id);
    expect(store.get().items[0]).toMatchObject({ id: chat(2).id, messageCount: 4 });
    expect(store.get().items.filter((i) => i.id === chat(2).id).length).toBe(1);
  });

  it("a failed load leaves the list and sets error; a failed rename or delete rethrows and changes nothing", async () => {
    const { store } = setup({ listChats: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(500, "internal"); }), patchChat: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(404, "not_found"); }), deleteChat: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(0, "network"); }) });
    await store.load();
    expect(store.get()).toMatchObject({ error: true, loaded: true, items: [] });
    await expect(store.rename("x", "y")).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(store.remove("x")).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 0 });
  });
});
```

`state/time.ts`:

```ts
const MIN = 60_000;
const HOUR = 60 * MIN;
const startOfDay = (d: Date) => new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate()).getTime();
const DAY = 24 * HOUR;
const shortDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en", { month: "short", day: "numeric" });

/** "Just now", "12 min ago", "2 h ago", "Yesterday", "2 days ago", then "Sep 28" (English only, in the viewer's time zone). */
export function relativeTime(iso: string, now: Date): string {
  const at = new Date(iso);
  const diff = now.getTime() - at.getTime();
  if (diff < MIN) return "Just now"; // also the future
  if (diff < HOUR) return `${Math.floor(diff / MIN)} min ago`;
  const days = Math.round((startOfDay(now) - startOfDay(at)) / DAY);
  if (days === 0) return `${Math.floor(diff / HOUR)} h ago`;
  if (days === 1) return "Yesterday";
  if (days < 7) return `${days} days ago`;
  return shortDate.format(at);
}
```

`state/sessions.ts`:

```ts
import type { ChatScope, ChatSessionDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { Api } from "../api/client";
import { Store } from "./store";

export const SEARCH_DEBOUNCE_MS = 250;
export type Schedule = (fn: () => void, ms: number) => () => void;
const realSchedule: Schedule = (fn, ms) => {
  const t = setTimeout(fn, ms);
  return () => clearTimeout(t);
};

export interface SessionsState {
  items: ChatSessionDto[];
  nextCursor: string | null;
  query: string;
  loading: boolean;
  loaded: boolean;
  error: boolean;
}

const startOfDay = (d: Date) => new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate()).getTime();

/** Today / Previous 7 days / Older, by the last message (the creation time when there is none). Order within a group is kept. */
export function groupSessions(items: ChatSessionDto[], now: Date) {
  const today = startOfDay(now);
  const week = today - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  const groups = [
    { label: "Today" as const, items: [] as ChatSessionDto[] },
    { label: "Previous 7 days" as const, items: [] as ChatSessionDto[] },
    { label: "Older" as const, items: [] as ChatSessionDto[] },
  ];
  for (const item of items) {
    const at = new Date(item.lastMessageAt ?? item.createdAt).getTime();
    groups[at >= today ? 0 : at >= week ? 1 : 2]?.items.push(item);
  }
  return groups.filter((g) => g.items.length > 0);
}

export class SessionsStore extends Store<SessionsState> {
  private generation = 0;
  private cancelSearch: (() => void) | undefined;
  constructor(
    private readonly api: Pick<Api, "listChats" | "createChat" | "getChat" | "patchChat" | "deleteChat">,
    private readonly schedule: Schedule = realSchedule,
  ) {
    super({ items: [], nextCursor: null, query: "", loading: false, loaded: false, error: false });
  }

  /** The first page for the current query. An answer that arrives after a newer request started is dropped. */
  async load(): Promise<void> {
    const mine = ++this.generation;
    const { query } = this.get();
    this.update((s) => ({ ...s, loading: true, error: false }));
    try {
      const page = await this.api.listChats(query ? { q: query } : {});
      if (mine !== this.generation) return;
      this.update((s) => ({ ...s, items: page.items, nextCursor: page.nextCursor, loading: false, loaded: true }));
    } catch {
      if (mine !== this.generation) return;
      this.update((s) => ({ ...s, loading: false, loaded: true, error: true }));
    }
  }

  async loadMore(): Promise<void> {
    const { nextCursor, query, loading } = this.get();
    if (nextCursor === null || loading) return;
    const mine = this.generation;
    this.update((s) => ({ ...s, loading: true }));
    try {
      const page = await this.api.listChats({ cursor: nextCursor, ...(query && { q: query }) });
      if (mine !== this.generation) return;
      this.update((s) => ({ ...s, items: [...s.items, ...page.items], nextCursor: page.nextCursor, loading: false }));
    } catch {
      this.update((s) => ({ ...s, loading: false, error: true }));
    }
  }

  search(q: string): void {
    this.cancelSearch?.();
    this.update((s) => ({ ...s, query: q }));
    this.cancelSearch = this.schedule(() => void this.load(), SEARCH_DEBOUNCE_MS);
  }

  async create(scope: ChatScope): Promise<ChatSessionDto> {
    const made = await this.api.createChat({ scope });
    this.update((s) => ({ ...s, items: [made, ...s.items.filter((i) => i.id !== made.id)] }));
    return made;
  }
  async rename(id: string, title: string): Promise<void> {
    const next = await this.api.patchChat(id, { title });
    this.update((s) => ({ ...s, items: s.items.map((i) => (i.id === id ? next : i)) }));
  }
  async remove(id: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.api.deleteChat(id);
    this.update((s) => ({ ...s, items: s.items.filter((i) => i.id !== id) }));
  }
  /** A chat had activity: fetch it and move it to the top (it also appears if this list had not seen it). Failures are ignored. */
  async touch(id: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const fresh = await this.api.getChat(id);
      this.update((s) => ({ ...s, items: [fresh, ...s.items.filter((i) => i.id !== id)] }));
    } catch {
      // the list is a convenience; the next load corrects it
    }
  }
}
```

### Task 185b: the session list and the app shell

**Spec:** §9 item 38, §7 (`dm-app-shell`, `dm-session-list`), `ui-mocks.md` §2 App shell and §4 Session list.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-session-list.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-app-shell.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/dm-settings.ts` (wrapped by the shell: no change to its own markup), `apps/web/src/components/dm-app.ts` (draws every signed-in route inside `<dm-app-shell>`), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-session-list.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-app-shell.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SessionsStore`, `groupSessions`, `relativeTime`, `Router`, `ThemeStore`, `AuthStore`, `<dm-dialog>`.
- Produces: `<dm-session-list active="<chat id>">`: the search box (labelled "Search chats", filters through `sessions.search`), the groups, one row per chat (title or "New chat", time), hover and focus actions Rename (inline input, Enter saves, Escape cancels, empty is refused) and Delete (a `<dm-dialog>` "Delete this chat?"), a "Load more" button while `nextCursor` is set, an empty state ("No chats yet. Ask your first question.") and a no-match state ("No chats match."). `<dm-app-shell>`: a 256 px sidebar (logo, "New chat" button, "Documents" link, the list, a footer with the user's email, a theme toggle and a sign-out button) and a main region that holds a slot; below 800 px the sidebar collapses behind a "Menu" button (`aria-expanded`). The shell does not wrap the auth screens or `account-deleted`.

```ts
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createServices } from "../services";

async function setup() {

  });
});
```

`components/dm-session-list.ts`:

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { groupSessions } from "../state/sessions";
import { relativeTime } from "../state/time";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-dialog";
import "./dm-icon";

export class DmSessionList extends LitElement {
  static properties = { active: { type: String }, renaming: { state: true }, draft: { state: true }, deleting: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; min-height: 0; overflow: auto; }
      .search { position: relative; padding: 0 12px 8px; }
      .search dm-icon { position: absolute; left: 22px; top: 9px; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      .search input { width: 100%; min-height: 32px; padding: 0 10px 0 30px; font: inherit; font-size: 13px; color: inherit; background: transparent; border: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); }
      h3 { margin: 14px 8px 4px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 400; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.06em; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      ul { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0 6px; display: grid; gap: 2px; }
      li { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 6px 6px 6px 10px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); }
      li:hover, li:focus-within { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--dm-text) 6%, transparent); }
      li[aria-current="page"] { background: var(--dm-accent-900); box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--dm-accent-800); }
      .text { flex: 1; min-width: 0; display: grid; }
      a.title { color: inherit; text-decoration: none; font-size: 13.5px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }
      .when { font-size: 11.5px; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      .actions { display: none; gap: 2px; }
      li:hover .actions, li:focus-within .actions { display: flex; }
      .actions .btn { width: 26px; min-height: 26px; }
      .empty { padding: 16px; color: var(--dm-muted); font-size: 13px; }
      .more { padding: 8px 12px; }
      input[data-rename] { width: 100%; font: inherit; font-size: 13.5px; color: inherit; background: var(--dm-surface); border: 1px solid var(--dm-accent); border-radius: 4px; padding: 2px 6px; }
    `,
  ];
  declare active: string | undefined;
  declare renaming: string | undefined;
  declare draft: string;
  declare deleting: string | undefined;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.sessions]);
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.draft = "";
  }
  private async saveRename(id: string) {
    const title = this.draft.trim();
    if (title === "") return; // an empty name is refused: the input stays open
    try {
      await this.ctx.services?.sessions.rename(id, title);
      this.renaming = undefined;
    } catch {
      this.ctx.services?.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not rename the chat." });
    }
  }
  private async confirmDelete() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const id = this.deleting;
    if (!svc || !id) return;
    try {
      await svc.sessions.remove(id);
      this.deleting = undefined;
      if (this.active === id) svc.router.navigate({ name: "chat" });
    } catch {
      this.deleting = undefined;
      svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not delete the chat." });
    }
  }
  render() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const state = svc?.sessions.get();
    if (!svc || !state) return html``;
    const groups = groupSessions(state.items, new Date());
    return html`
      <div class="search"><dm-icon name="magnifying-glass"></dm-icon>
        <input type="search" aria-label="Search chats" placeholder="Search chats" .value=${state.query} @input=${(e: Event) => svc.sessions.search((e.target as HTMLInputElement).value)} /></div>
      ${state.loaded && state.items.length === 0 ? html`<p class="empty">${state.query ? "No chats match." : "No chats yet. Ask your first question."}</p>` : null}
      ${groups.map((g) => html`<h3>${g.label}</h3><ul>${g.items.map((c) => html`
        <li aria-current=${c.id === this.active ? "page" : "false"}>
          <span class="text">
            ${this.renaming === c.id
              ? html`<input data-rename aria-label="Chat name" .value=${this.draft} @input=${(e: Event) => (this.draft = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value)}
                  @keydown=${(e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === "Enter") void this.saveRename(c.id); else if (e.key === "Escape") this.renaming = undefined; }} />`
              : html`<a class="title" href="/chats/${c.id}">${c.title ?? "New chat"}</a>`}
            <span class="when">${relativeTime(c.lastMessageAt ?? c.createdAt, new Date())}</span>
          </span>
          <span class="actions">
            <button class="btn icon" aria-label="Rename chat" @click=${() => { this.renaming = c.id; this.draft = c.title ?? ""; }}><dm-icon name="pencil-simple"></dm-icon></button>
            <button class="btn icon" aria-label="Delete chat" @click=${() => (this.deleting = c.id)}><dm-icon name="trash"></dm-icon></button>
          </span>
        </li>`)}</ul>`)}
      ${state.nextCursor ? html`<div class="more"><button class="btn secondary" @click=${() => svc.sessions.loadMore()} ?disabled=${state.loading}>Load more</button></div>` : null}
      <dm-dialog heading="Delete this chat?" ?open=${this.deleting !== undefined} @dm-close=${() => (this.deleting = undefined)}>
        <p>The chat and its messages are removed. This cannot be undone.</p>
        <button slot="actions" class="btn secondary" @click=${() => (this.deleting = undefined)}>Cancel</button>
        <button slot="actions" class="btn danger" data-confirm @click=${() => this.confirmDelete()}>Delete</button>
      </dm-dialog>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-session-list", DmSessionList);
```

`components/dm-app-shell.ts`:

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";
import "./dm-session-list";

export class DmAppShell extends LitElement {
  static properties = { menuOpen: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: flex; height: 100%; }
      aside { flex: none; width: var(--dm-sidebar-w); display: flex; flex-direction: column; background: var(--dm-side); border-right: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); min-height: 0; }
      header { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; padding: 14px 14px 10px; }
      .logo { width: 26px; height: 26px; border-radius: 7px; display: grid; place-items: center; box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--dm-accent); color: var(--dm-accent); }
      .brand { font-size: 14px; font-weight: 500; }
      .block { display: grid; gap: 4px; padding: 4px 12px 8px; }
      .block .btn { justify-content: flex-start; min-height: 36px; }
      a.nav { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; min-height: 34px; padding: 0 10px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); color: inherit; text-decoration: none; }
      a.nav[aria-current="page"] { background: var(--dm-accent-900); color: var(--dm-accent-200); }
      dm-session-list { flex: 1; }
      footer { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 10px 14px; border-top: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); }
      [data-user] { flex: 1; min-width: 0; font-size: 12.5px; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
      main { flex: 1; min-width: 0; min-height: 0; overflow: auto; }
      .menu { display: none; }
      @media (max-width: 800px) {
        aside { position: fixed; inset: 0 auto 0 0; z-index: 5; transform: translateX(-100%); }
        aside[data-open] { transform: none; box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-lg); }
        .menu { display: inline-flex; position: fixed; top: 8px; left: 8px; z-index: 6; }
      }
    `,
  ];
  declare menuOpen: boolean;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.router, s.auth, s.theme]);
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.menuOpen = false;
  }
  private async signOut() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc) return;
    await svc.auth.logout();
    svc.router.navigate({ name: "sign-in" }, { replace: true });
  }
  render() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const route = svc?.router.get();
    const auth = svc?.auth.get();
    const activeChat = route?.name === "chat" ? route.chatId : undefined;
    return html`
      <button class="btn secondary menu" data-menu aria-expanded=${this.menuOpen} aria-controls="sidebar" @click=${() => (this.menuOpen = !this.menuOpen)}><dm-icon name="sidebar-simple"></dm-icon>Menu</button>
      <aside id="sidebar" ?data-open=${this.menuOpen}>
        <header><span class="logo"><dm-icon name="book-open-text"></dm-icon></span><span class="brand">DocMind</span></header>
        <nav aria-label="Primary" class="block">
          <button class="btn primary" data-new-chat @click=${() => { svc?.router.navigate({ name: "chat" }); this.menuOpen = false; }}><dm-icon name="plus"></dm-icon>New chat</button>
          <a class="nav" data-documents href="/documents" aria-current=${route?.name === "documents" || route?.name === "document" ? "page" : "false"}><dm-icon name="files"></dm-icon>Documents</a>
        </nav>
        <dm-session-list .active=${activeChat}></dm-session-list>
        <footer>
          <span data-user>${auth?.status === "signed-in" ? auth.me.email : ""}</span>
          <a class="btn icon" href="/settings" aria-label="Settings"><dm-icon name="gear"></dm-icon></a>
          <button class="btn icon" data-theme-toggle aria-label="Toggle theme" @click=${() => svc?.theme.choose(document.documentElement.dataset.theme === "light" ? "dark" : "light")}><dm-icon name=${document.documentElement.dataset.theme === "light" ? "moon" : "sun"}></dm-icon></button>
          <button class="btn icon" data-sign-out aria-label="Sign out" @click=${this.signOut}><dm-icon name="sign-out"></dm-icon></button>
        </footer>
      </aside>
      <main><slot></slot></main>`;
  }
  firstUpdated() {
    void this.ctx.services?.sessions.load();
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-app-shell", DmAppShell);
```

  The sidebar loads the sessions once when it first renders (`firstUpdated`); the guard has already established that the user is signed in. The theme button chooses the opposite of what is shown now (`dark` or `light`; "system" is chosen in Settings).

## Phase D: chat (spec §9 items 39 to 41)

Seven tasks build the chat from the bottom: the fold of server data into a transcript (186), the transport (187), the answer renderer (188), the thinking block (189), the controller and the view (190), the sources panel (191) and the composer (192). The spike result (Task 176) is what makes `dm-chat-view` DocMind's own element.

### Task 186: `ChatFold`, loom-ui's `ChatFeed` fed with DocMind's messages

**Spec:** §7 ("Transcript state, resumable stream fold: loom-ui `ChatFeed`"), §6.1 (message and stream shapes), §6.5. Findings U2, U3, U4.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/chat/feedAdapter.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/chat/feedAdapter.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ChatFeed` (`@loom-ui/core/chat`): `applyHistory(page, "backward")`, `applyEvent(name, data)`, `value()`, `resumeCursor()`; the type `ChatValue` (`@loom-ui/core/chat-ui`); contracts `ChatMessageDto`, `ChatStreamEvent`, `ListMessagesResponse`, `Citation`, `ChatToolStep`, `ChatReasoningDto`, `ChatOutcome`.
- Produces: `Extras`, `entryIdOf`, `wireOfMessage`, `ChatFold` (catalogue). `ChatFold` fields: `liveSteps: ChatToolStep[]`, `error: boolean`, `olderCursor: string | null` (the server's `nextCursor` of the oldest page loaded; `null` means the start of the chat is in the window), `historyLoaded: boolean`. The feed is created with `{ mode: "owner", reasoning: true }`: a chat is the owner's alone, and live reasoning is shown.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** `feedAdapter.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { ChatMessageDto, ChatStreamEvent } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ChatFold, entryIdOf, wireOfMessage } from "./feedAdapter";

const PASSAGE = "c0ffee00-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const DOC = "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11";
const msg = (ord: number, role: "user" | "assistant", turn: number, over: Partial<ChatMessageDto> = {}): ChatMessageDto => ({
  id: String(ord), turn, role, text: `${role} ${ord}`, status: "complete", citations: [], cursor: `1.${ord * 10}`,
  reasoning: null, steps: [], outcome: null, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});
const page = (items: ChatMessageDto[], nextCursor: string | null = null) => ({ items, nextCursor });
const ev = (e: unknown) => e as ChatStreamEvent;
const text = (fold: ChatFold) => fold.value().entries.map((e) => [e.id, e.status, (e.parts?.[0] as { text: string } | undefined)?.text]);

describe("entryIdOf / wireOfMessage", () => {
  it("names an assistant entry by its turn and a user entry by its record ordinal", () => {
    expect(entryIdOf(msg(2, "assistant", 1))).toBe("t1.assistant");
    expect(entryIdOf(msg(1, "user", 1))).toBe("m1");
    expect(entryIdOf(msg(7, "user", 0, { status: "rejected" }))).toBe("m7");
  });
  it("maps the DTO to the feed's wire shape and a failed or cut-short reply to a fixed reason token", () => {
    const wire = wireOfMessage(msg(2, "assistant", 1, { status: "failed" }));
    expect(wire).toMatchObject({ turn: 1, role: "assistant", status: "failed", cursor: "1.20", ord: 2, reason: "model-error", at: Date.parse("2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z") });
    expect(wireOfMessage(msg(2, "assistant", 1, { outcome: "refusal" }))).toMatchObject({ reason: "refusal" });
    expect(wireOfMessage(msg(2, "assistant", 1, { outcome: "max_tokens" }))).toMatchObject({ reason: "max-tokens" });
    expect(wireOfMessage(msg(2, "assistant", 1, { outcome: "max_iterations" }))).toMatchObject({ reason: "max-iterations" });
    expect(wireOfMessage(msg(1, "user", 0, { status: "rejected" }))).toMatchObject({ reason: "rejected", turn: 0 });
    expect("reason" in wireOfMessage(msg(2, "assistant", 1))).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("ChatFold history", () => {
  it("folds a page (oldest first, as the server sends it) into entries in order, with their extras kept beside", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    const cited = msg(2, "assistant", 1, {
      citations: [{ passageId: PASSAGE, documentId: DOC, docTitle: "Doc", locator: { kind: "page", page: 3 }, snippet: "snip" }],
      steps: [{ name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 8 }],
      reasoning: { text: "I looked", kind: "summary" },
    });
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1), cited, msg(3, "user", 2), msg(4, "assistant", 2)], "1.5"));
    expect(text(fold).map((r) => r[0])).toEqual(["m1", "t1.assistant", "m3", "t2.assistant"]);
    expect(fold.olderCursor).toBe("1.5");
    expect(fold.historyLoaded).toBe(true);
    expect(fold.extrasOf("t1.assistant")).toMatchObject({ ord: 2, citations: [{ passageId: PASSAGE }], steps: [{ count: 8 }], reasoning: { text: "I looked" }, outcome: null });
    expect(fold.extrasOf("m1")).toMatchObject({ ord: 1, citations: [] });
  });

  it("an older page goes in front, and the start of the chat clears the older cursor", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(3, "user", 2), msg(4, "assistant", 2)], "1.25"));
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1)], null));
    expect(text(fold).map((r) => r[0])).toEqual(["m1", "t1.assistant", "m3", "t2.assistant"]);
    expect(fold.olderCursor).toBeNull();
  });

  it("keeps a rejected message (turn 0) so the user sees why it was refused, without its text", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 0, { status: "rejected", text: "sentinel-question-0123456789" })]));
    const [entry] = fold.value().entries;
    expect(entry).toMatchObject({ id: "m1", status: "rejected" });
    expect(JSON.stringify(entry)).not.toContain("sentinel-question");
  });
});

describe("ChatFold stream", () => {
  it("folds status, reasoning, answer deltas and the durable message: the live reply becomes the durable one in place", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1)]));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "status", state: "open", running: true, turns: 1 }));
    expect(fold.value().running).toBe(true);
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reasoning-partial", turn: 1, text: "Looking" }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reasoning", turn: 1, text: " at the invoice" }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 1, text: "Due " }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 1, text: "in 30 days" }));
    const live = fold.value().entries[1];
    expect(live).toMatchObject({ id: "t1.assistant", status: "streaming", reasoning: { text: "Looking at the invoice", streaming: true } });
    expect(text(fold)[1]).toEqual(["t1.assistant", "streaming", "Due in 30 days"]);
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "tool", turnKey: 1, step: { name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 8 } }));
    expect(fold.liveSteps).toHaveLength(1);
    const done = msg(2, "assistant", 1, { text: "Due in 30 days.", steps: [{ name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 8 }] });
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "message", ...done }));
    expect(text(fold)[1]).toEqual(["t1.assistant", "complete", "Due in 30 days."]);
    expect(fold.value().entries[1]?.reasoning).toMatchObject({ text: "Looking at the invoice", streaming: false });
    expect(fold.liveSteps).toEqual([]);
    expect(fold.extrasOf("t1.assistant")?.steps).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("tool steps pile up for the reply in flight and are dropped by tool-reset and reset", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    const step = { name: "search_docs" as const, label: "Searched documents", count: 1 };
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "tool", turnKey: 1, step }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "tool", turnKey: 1, step }));
    expect(fold.liveSteps).toHaveLength(2);
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "tool-reset", turnKey: 1 }));
    expect(fold.liveSteps).toEqual([]);
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "tool", turnKey: 1, step }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reset", turn: 1 }));
    expect(fold.liveSteps).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("a reply that restarts (reset) loses its live text and reasoning", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1)]));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 1, text: "half an answer" }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reasoning-partial", turn: 1, text: "half a thought" }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reset", turn: 1 }));
    expect(fold.value().entries.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["m1"]);
  });

  it("resetLive empties the reply in flight so the snapshot a reconnect starts with does not double the text", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1)]));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reasoning-partial", turn: 1, text: "Thinking" }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 1, text: "Hello" }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "tool", turnKey: 1, step: { name: "search_docs", label: "L", count: 1 } }));
    fold.resetLive(); // the stream just (re)opened
    expect(fold.liveSteps).toEqual([]);
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reasoning-partial", turn: 1, text: "Thinking more" })); // the server's snapshot
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 1, text: "Hello wor" }));
    expect(text(fold)[1]).toEqual(["t1.assistant", "streaming", "Hello wor"]);
    expect(fold.value().entries[1]?.reasoning?.text).toBe("Thinking more");
  });

  it("closed and error frames: the chat closes, and an error is remembered", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "closed" }));
    expect(fold.value().state).toBe("closed");
    expect(fold.error).toBe(false);
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "error", code: "internal" }));
    expect(fold.error).toBe(true);
  });

  it("ignores a frame the feed refuses, without throwing, and exposes the newest durable cursor to resume from", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 0, text: "x" }));
    expect(fold.value().entries).toEqual([]);
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1)]));
    expect(fold.resumeCursor()).toBe("1.20");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `chat/feedAdapter.ts`.**

```ts
import type {
  ChatMessageDto, ChatOutcome, ChatReasoningDto, ChatStreamEvent, ChatToolStep, Citation, ListMessagesResponse,
} from "@docmind/contracts";
import { ChatFeed } from "@loom-ui/core/chat";
import type { ChatValue } from "@loom-ui/core/chat-ui";

/** What the feed's contract has no room for, kept beside it and keyed by the entry id the feed assigns. */
export interface Extras {
  ord: number;
  citations: Citation[];
  steps: ChatToolStep[];
  reasoning: ChatReasoningDto | null;
  outcome: ChatOutcome | null;
}

type Keyed = Pick<ChatMessageDto, "role" | "turn" | "id">;

/** The id `ChatFeed` gives an entry: `t<turn>.assistant` for a reply, `m<ord>` for a user message (its record ordinal). */
export const entryIdOf = (m: Keyed): string => (m.role === "assistant" ? `t${m.turn}.assistant` : `m${Number(m.id)}`);

const REASON: Record<ChatOutcome, string> = { refusal: "refusal", max_tokens: "max-tokens", max_iterations: "max-iterations" };

/** The shape `chatEntryFromWire` reads. The server's `createdAt` (ISO) becomes `at` (epoch ms); the ordinal `id` becomes `ord`. */
export function wireOfMessage(m: ChatMessageDto): Record<string, unknown> {
  const reason = m.status === "failed" ? "model-error" : m.status === "rejected" ? "rejected" : m.outcome ? REASON[m.outcome] : undefined;
  return {
    turn: m.turn,
    role: m.role,
    status: m.status,
    text: m.text,
    cursor: m.cursor,
    at: Date.parse(m.createdAt),
    ord: Number(m.id),
    ...(reason !== undefined && { reason }),
  };
}

const extrasOf = (m: ChatMessageDto): Extras => ({
  ord: Number(m.id),
  citations: m.citations,
  steps: m.steps,
  reasoning: m.reasoning,
  outcome: m.outcome,
});

const MAX_LIVE_STEPS = 16;

/**
 * DocMind's messages and stream frames folded into loom-ui's `ChatFeed`. The feed owns ordering, de-duplication by
 * cursor, bounded live text, sanitising and live reasoning; this class translates the wire (spec §6.1) on the way in
 * and keeps the fields the feed's contract drops (citations, tool steps, history reasoning, outcome) in a side map.
 */
export class ChatFold {
  private readonly feed = new ChatFeed({ mode: "owner", reasoning: true });
  private readonly extras = new Map<string, Extras>();
  /** Tool steps of the reply in flight (the stream numbers them by turn key, which cannot be mapped to a turn). */
  liveSteps: ChatToolStep[] = [];
  error = false;
  /** The server's `nextCursor` of the oldest page loaded; `null` once the start of the chat is in the window. */
  olderCursor: string | null = null;
  historyLoaded = false;

  /** A page of `GET /api/chats/:id/messages` (oldest first within the page). The feed takes backward pages newest first. */
  applyHistory(page: ListMessagesResponse): void {
    for (const m of page.items) this.extras.set(entryIdOf(m), extrasOf(m));
    this.feed.applyHistory({ entries: [...page.items].reverse().map(wireOfMessage), done: page.nextCursor === null }, "backward");
    this.olderCursor = page.nextCursor;
    this.historyLoaded = true;
  }

  applyStream(ev: ChatStreamEvent): void {
    switch (ev.kind) {
      case "message":
        this.extras.set(entryIdOf(ev), extrasOf(ev));
        this.feed.applyEvent("message", wireOfMessage(ev));
        if (ev.role === "assistant") this.liveSteps = [];
        break;
      case "status":
        this.feed.applyEvent("status", { state: ev.state, running: ev.running });
        break;
      case "reasoning":
      case "reasoning-partial":
        this.feed.applyEvent(ev.kind, { turn: ev.turn, text: ev.text });
        break;
      case "answer": // the feed's `delta`
        this.feed.applyEvent("delta", { turn: ev.turn, text: ev.text });
        break;
      case "reset":
        this.feed.applyEvent("reset", { turn: ev.turn });
        this.liveSteps = [];
        break;
      case "tool":
        this.liveSteps = [...this.liveSteps, ev.step].slice(-MAX_LIVE_STEPS);
        break;
      case "tool-reset":
        this.liveSteps = [];
        break;
      case "closed":
        this.feed.applyEvent("closed", {});
        break;
      case "error":
        this.error = true;
        break;
    }
  }

  /** Called when the stream (re)opens: it starts with a snapshot of the reply so far, which must land on an empty live reply. */
  resetLive(): void {
    const live = this.feed.value().entries.find((e) => e.status === "streaming");
    if (live) this.feed.applyEvent("reset", { turn: live.turn });
    this.liveSteps = [];
  }

  value(): ChatValue {
    return this.feed.value();
  }
  extrasOf(entryId: string): Extras | undefined {
    return this.extras.get(entryId);
  }
  resumeCursor(): string | undefined {
    return this.feed.resumeCursor();
  }
}
```

### Task 187: the chat transport

**Spec:** §6.1 (history 30 per page; stream resumable by `Last-Event-ID`), §7 (resumable stream fold). Findings U3, U5; decision D3.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/chat/transport.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/chat/transport.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Api.listMessages`, `ChatFold`, `OpenSse`, `ChatStreamEvent` (zod), `ApiError`.
- Produces: `ChatTransport`, `createChatTransport` (catalogue). `start()`: loads the newest page, then opens `GET /api/chats/<id>/stream` with `Last-Event-ID` set to `fold.resumeCursor()` (read again at every attempt), calling `fold.resetLive()` on every open; frames are parsed with `ChatStreamEvent` and applied; `onChange()` runs after every applied frame and page; `onProblem("load")` when the first page fails, `"gone"` on a 404 (page or stream) or a stream that closes with `not_found`, `"stream"` when the stream closes with `rejected`; a 401 is left to the API's `onUnauthorized`. A `closed` frame closes the stream (no reconnect). `loadOlder()` fetches the next page with `fold.olderCursor`, one at a time. `close()` closes the stream and silences everything.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** `transport.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { ChatMessageDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { SseOptions } from "../api/sse";
import { ChatFold } from "./feedAdapter";
import { createChatTransport } from "./transport";

const msg = (ord: number, role: "user" | "assistant", turn: number, over: Partial<ChatMessageDto> = {}): ChatMessageDto => ({
  id: String(ord), turn, role, text: `${role} ${ord}`, status: "complete", citations: [], cursor: `1.${ord * 10}`,
  reasoning: null, steps: [], outcome: null, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});

function setup(listMessages = vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1)], nextCursor: "1.5" as string | null }))) {
  const fold = new ChatFold();
  const sse: { opts?: SseOptions; closed: boolean } = { closed: false };
  const open = vi.fn((opts: SseOptions) => { sse.opts = opts; return { close: () => { sse.closed = true; } }; });
  const onChange = vi.fn();
  const onProblem = vi.fn();
  const transport = createChatTransport({ api: { listMessages } as never, chatId: "c1", fold, open: open as never, onChange, onProblem });
  const frame = (event: string, data: unknown) => sse.opts?.onFrame({ event, data: JSON.stringify(data), id: undefined });
  return { fold, sse, open, onChange, onProblem, transport, listMessages, frame };
}

describe("createChatTransport", () => {
  it("loads the newest page, then opens the stream resuming after the newest durable cursor", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.transport.start();
    expect(t.listMessages).toHaveBeenCalledWith("c1", undefined);
    expect(t.fold.value().entries.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["m1", "t1.assistant"]);
    expect((t.sse.opts?.url as () => string)()).toBe("/api/chats/c1/stream");
    expect(t.sse.opts?.lastEventId?.()).toBe("1.20");
    expect(t.onChange).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("applies frames by their event name, and resets the live reply every time the stream opens", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.transport.start();
    t.frame("answer", { turn: 2, text: "Hel" });
    expect(t.fold.value().entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ status: "streaming" });
    t.sse.opts?.onOpen?.(); // a reconnect
    expect(t.fold.value().entries.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["m1", "t1.assistant"]);
    t.frame("answer", { turn: 2, text: "Hello" }); // the snapshot
    t.frame("message", msg(4, "assistant", 2, { text: "Hello there" }));
    expect(t.fold.value().entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ id: "t2.assistant", status: "complete" });
  });

  it("drops a frame that is not one of ours, without applying it and without throwing", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.transport.start();
    t.onChange.mockClear();
    t.frame("answer", { turn: "two" });
    t.frame("unheard-of", {});
    t.sse.opts?.onFrame({ event: "answer", data: "{not json", id: undefined });
    expect(t.onChange).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(t.fold.value().entries.length).toBe(2);
  });

  it("closes the stream for good on a closed frame, and reports the chat gone or rejected by the stream's own end", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.transport.start();
    t.frame("closed", {});
    expect(t.sse.closed).toBe(true);
    const gone = setup();
    await gone.transport.start();
    gone.sse.opts?.onClose?.("not_found");
    expect(gone.onProblem).toHaveBeenCalledWith("gone");
    const rejected = setup();
    await rejected.transport.start();
    rejected.sse.opts?.onClose?.("rejected");
    expect(rejected.onProblem).toHaveBeenCalledWith("stream");
    const ours = setup();
    await ours.transport.start();
    ours.sse.opts?.onClose?.("closed"); // we closed it ourselves: not a problem
    expect(ours.onProblem).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("a failed first page is a 'load' problem (and 'gone' for a 404) and opens no stream", async () => {
    const down = setup(vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(0, "network"); }));
    await down.transport.start();
    expect(down.onProblem).toHaveBeenCalledWith("load");
    expect(down.open).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    const missing = setup(vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(404, "not_found"); }));
    await missing.transport.start();
    expect(missing.onProblem).toHaveBeenCalledWith("gone");
  });

  it("loadOlder fetches the page before the window, one at a time, and stops at the start", async () => {
    const listMessages = vi.fn(async (_id: string, cursor?: string) =>
      cursor === undefined ? { items: [msg(3, "user", 2), msg(4, "assistant", 2)], nextCursor: "1.25" as string | null } : { items: [msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1)], nextCursor: null },
    );
    const t = setup(listMessages as never);
    await t.transport.start();
    const first = t.transport.loadOlder();
    const second = t.transport.loadOlder(); // while the first is in flight: ignored
    await Promise.all([first, second]);
    expect(listMessages).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(listMessages).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("c1", "1.25");
    expect(t.fold.value().entries.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["m1", "t1.assistant", "m3", "t2.assistant"]);
    await t.transport.loadOlder(); // the start is reached
    expect(listMessages).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it("close() closes the stream and silences later frames and pages", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.transport.start();
    t.transport.close();
    expect(t.sse.closed).toBe(true);
    t.onChange.mockClear();
    t.frame("status", { state: "open", running: true, turns: 1 });
    await t.transport.loadOlder();
    expect(t.onChange).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run to see them fail.** `pnpm --filter @docmind/web exec vitest run src/chat/transport.test.ts`. Expected: FAIL.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `chat/transport.ts`.**

```ts
import { ChatStreamEvent } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { Api } from "../api/client";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { OpenSse, SseFrame } from "../api/sse";
import { report } from "../report";
import type { ChatFold } from "./feedAdapter";

export interface ChatTransport {
  start(): Promise<void>;
  loadOlder(): Promise<void>;
  close(): void;
}

export interface TransportDeps {
  api: Pick<Api, "listMessages">;
  chatId: string;
  fold: ChatFold;
  open: OpenSse;
  onChange(): void;
  onProblem(p: "load" | "stream" | "gone"): void;
}

export function createChatTransport(d: TransportDeps): ChatTransport {
  let closed = false;
  let stream: { close(): void } | undefined;
  let loadingOlder = false;

  const failure = (err: unknown): "load" | "gone" => (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404 ? "gone" : "load");

  const onFrame = (frame: SseFrame) => {
    if (closed) return;
    let data: unknown;
    try {
      data = JSON.parse(frame.data);
    } catch {
      return; // not JSON: not ours
    }
    const parsed = ChatStreamEvent.safeParse({ kind: frame.event, ...(typeof data === "object" && data !== null ? data : {}) });
    if (!parsed.success) return;
    d.fold.applyStream(parsed.data);
    if (parsed.data.kind === "closed") stream?.close();
    d.onChange();
  };

  return {
    async start() {
      try {
        d.fold.applyHistory(await d.api.listMessages(d.chatId, undefined));
      } catch (err) {
        if (!closed) d.onProblem(failure(err));
        return;
      }
      if (closed) return;
      d.onChange();
      stream = d.open({
        url: () => `/api/chats/${encodeURIComponent(d.chatId)}/stream`,
        lastEventId: () => d.fold.resumeCursor(),
        onOpen: () => {
          if (closed) return;
          d.fold.resetLive();
          d.onChange();
        },
        onFrame,
        onClose: (reason) => {
          if (closed || reason === "closed") return;
          if (reason === "not_found") d.onProblem("gone");
          else if (reason === "rejected") d.onProblem("stream");
          // "unauthorized": the API client already signed the user out
        },
      });
    },
    async loadOlder() {
      const cursor = d.fold.olderCursor;
      if (closed || loadingOlder || cursor === null) return;
      loadingOlder = true;
      try {
        d.fold.applyHistory(await d.api.listMessages(d.chatId, cursor));
        if (!closed) d.onChange();
      } catch (err) {
        report("chat-older", err);
        if (!closed) d.onProblem("load");
      } finally {
        loadingOlder = false;
      }
    },
    close() {
      closed = true;
      stream?.close();
    },
  };
}
```

  **Note on `lastEventId` and `url`.** `openSse` reads `lastEventId()` only for its first attempt; later attempts use the last id the parser saw. A `message` frame's SSE id is its cursor (spec §6.1), so after a reconnect the server resumes after the newest durable message.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run it.** Expected: PASS (7 tests). The test "close() ... silences" relies on `closed` being checked in `onFrame`; if `loadOlder` after `close()` still calls `listMessages`, the guard order in `loadOlder` is wrong (the `closed` check comes first).

- [ ] **Step 5: Gate and commit.**

```bash
pnpm lint:fix && pnpm build && pnpm typecheck && pnpm typecheck:tests && pnpm lint && pnpm test
git add apps/web
git commit -m "feat(web): the chat transport: newest page, resumable stream, older pages

Co-Authored-By: <executing model>"
```
