### Task 188: the answer renderer: markdown, sanitised, with citation markers, and `dm-message-body`

**Spec:** §6.3 (Rendering: valid citations become numbered markers), §7 Security ("Markdown is rendered, then sanitized with DOMPurify"; the streaming region is `aria-live="polite"`), `ui-mocks.md` §4 Chat bubbles and Citation marker. Decisions D6, D8.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/chat/markdown.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-message-body.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/chat/markdown.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-message-body.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/security.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `sanitizeChatText` (`@loom-ui/core/chat-ui`), `Citation`, `marked` (`Marked`), `dompurify`.
- Produces: `renderAnswer(text, citations): { html: string; cited: Citation[] }` (`cited` in order of first appearance; `html` is the only string any component may hand to `unsafeHTML`) and `renderStreaming(text): string` (catalogue). `<dm-message-body .text .citations .streaming .selected>`: draws the sanitised html in a region with `aria-live="polite"` and `aria-busy` while `streaming`, a blinking caret at the end of the last block while streaming, and fires `dm-cite-select` (`detail: { passageId }`, bubbles, composed) when a citation button is clicked, hovered or focused. `.selected` is the passage id whose button is drawn as selected.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

`markdown.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { Citation } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { renderAnswer, renderStreaming } from "./markdown";

const A = "c0ffee00-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const B = "c0ffee00-0000-4000-8000-000000000002";
const DOC = "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11";
const cite = (id: string): Citation => ({ passageId: id, documentId: DOC, docTitle: "Doc", locator: { kind: "page", page: 1 }, snippet: "s" });
const dom = (html: string) => { const t = document.createElement("template"); t.innerHTML = html; return t.content; };

describe("renderAnswer: markdown", () => {
  it("renders emphasis, lists, code and tables", () => {
    const { html } = renderAnswer("Pay **in 30 days**.\n\n- one\n- two\n\n`x = 1`\n\n| a | b |\n|---|---|\n| 1 | 2 |", []);
    const d = dom(html);
    expect(d.querySelector("strong")?.textContent).toBe("in 30 days");
    expect([...d.querySelectorAll("li")].map((li) => li.textContent)).toEqual(["one", "two"]);
    expect(d.querySelector("code")?.textContent).toBe("x = 1");
    expect(d.querySelectorAll("td").length).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe("renderAnswer: citations", () => {
  it("turns a valid marker into a numbered button, numbering by first appearance, and reuses a number", () => {
    const { html, cited } = renderAnswer(`First [[${B}]] then [[${A}]] and again [[${B}]].`, [cite(A), cite(B)]);
    expect(cited.map((c) => c.passageId)).toEqual([B, A]);
    const buttons = [...dom(html).querySelectorAll("button.dm-cite")];
    expect(buttons.map((b) => [b.textContent, b.getAttribute("data-passage"), b.getAttribute("aria-label"), b.getAttribute("type")])).toEqual([
      ["1", B, "Source 1", "button"], ["2", A, "Source 2", "button"], ["1", B, "Source 1", "button"],
    ]);
    expect(html).not.toContain("[[");
  });
  it("drops a marker the server did not validate, and never invents a citation from a lookalike", () => {
    const { html, cited } = renderAnswer(`Claim [[${A}]] and [[not-a-uuid]] and [[${B}]].`, [cite(A)]);
    expect(cited.map((c) => c.passageId)).toEqual([A]);
    expect(dom(html).querySelectorAll("button.dm-cite").length).toBe(1);
    expect(html).toContain("[[not-a-uuid]]"); // not a marker: ordinary text
    expect(html).not.toContain(B);
  });
  it("a forged private-use placeholder in the text does not become a button", () => {
    const { html } = renderAnswer("x 1 y", [cite(A)]);
    expect(dom(html).querySelectorAll("button").length).toBe(0);
    expect(html).not.toMatch(/[]/);
  });
  it("matches the marker to the citation without regard to case", () => {
    const { cited } = renderAnswer(`[[${A.toUpperCase()}]]`, [cite(A)]);
    expect(cited.length).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe("renderAnswer: untrusted input", () => {
  const html = (text: string) => renderAnswer(text, [cite(A)]).html;
  it("shows raw HTML as text and creates no element from it", () => {
    const out = html('<script>alert(1)</script><img src=x onerror=alert(1)><b onclick="x()">bold</b> <iframe src="//evil"></iframe>');
    const d = dom(out);
    for (const tag of ["script", "img", "b", "iframe"]) expect(d.querySelector(tag), tag).toBeNull();
    expect(out).toContain("&lt;script&gt;");
  });
  it("keeps only http, https and mailto links, forced to a new context with no opener, and drops images", () => {
    const out = html("[ok](https://example.com/a) [mail](mailto:a@example.com) [bad](javascript:alert(1)) [data](data:text/html,x) ![pic](https://evil/p.png) [rel](/relative)");
    const d = dom(out);
    const links = [...d.querySelectorAll("a[href]")];
    expect(links.map((a) => a.getAttribute("href"))).toEqual(["https://example.com/a", "mailto:a@example.com"]);
    expect(out).not.toMatch(/javascript:|data:text/i);
    for (const a of d.querySelectorAll("a")) {
      expect(a.getAttribute("target")).toBe("_blank");
      expect(a.getAttribute("rel")).toBe("noopener noreferrer");
    }
    expect(d.querySelector("img")).toBeNull();
    expect(d.textContent).toContain("pic"); // the alt text survives as text
  });
  it("allows no style, id, class or event attributes on anything the model wrote", () => {
    const d = dom(html('<p style="x" id="y" class="dm-cite" onmouseover="z()" data-passage="q">t</p>'));
    expect(d.querySelectorAll("[style],[id],[class],[onmouseover],[data-passage]").length).toBe(0);
  });
  it("removes control and bidirectional override characters", () => {
    expect(renderAnswer("a‮b\u0007c", []).html).toContain("abc");
  });
});

describe("renderStreaming", () => {
  it("renders markdown but hides every marker, including one that is still arriving", () => {
    expect(renderStreaming(`Due in **30 days** [[${A}]] ok`)).not.toContain("[[");
    for (const partial of [`Due [[${A.slice(0, 10)}`, "Due [[", "Due ["]) expect(renderStreaming(partial), partial).not.toMatch(/\[/);
    expect(dom(renderStreaming("a **b**")).querySelector("strong")?.textContent).toBe("b");
  });
  it("is just as safe as the final render", () => {
    expect(dom(renderStreaming("<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>")).querySelector("img")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`dm-message-body.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import "./dm-message-body";

const A = "c0ffee00-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const DOC = "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11";
const citation = { passageId: A, documentId: DOC, docTitle: "Doc", locator: { kind: "page" as const, page: 1 }, snippet: "s" };
type Body = HTMLElement & { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> };
async function mount(props: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const el = document.createElement("dm-message-body") as Body;
  Object.assign(el, props);
  document.body.append(el);
  await el.updateComplete;
  return el;
}
const region = (el: Body) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(".prose") as HTMLElement;

describe("dm-message-body", () => {
  it("draws the answer and a polite live region that is busy only while streaming", async () => {
    const done = await mount({ text: "Hello **there**", citations: [], streaming: false });
    expect(region(done).querySelector("strong")?.textContent).toBe("there");
    expect(region(done).getAttribute("aria-live")).toBe("polite");
    expect(region(done).getAttribute("aria-busy")).toBe("false");
    const live = await mount({ text: "Hello [[", citations: [], streaming: true });
    expect(region(live).getAttribute("aria-busy")).toBe("true");
    expect(region(live).hasAttribute("data-streaming")).toBe(true);
    expect(region(live).textContent).not.toContain("[[");
  });

  it("fires dm-cite-select with the passage id on click, hover and focus, and marks the selected button", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ text: `Claim [[${A}]].`, citations: [citation], streaming: false, selected: A });
    const button = region(el).querySelector("button.dm-cite") as HTMLButtonElement;
    expect(button.hasAttribute("data-selected")).toBe(true);
    const picked = vi.fn((e: Event) => (e as CustomEvent).detail.passageId);
    el.addEventListener("dm-cite-select", picked);
    button.click();
    button.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("mouseover", { bubbles: true }));
    button.dispatchEvent(new FocusEvent("focusin", { bubbles: true }));
    expect(picked.mock.results.map((r) => r.value)).toEqual([A, A, A]);
    el.selected = undefined;
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(region(el).querySelector("button.dm-cite")?.hasAttribute("data-selected")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("does not fire for a click on ordinary text", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ text: "Plain.", citations: [], streaming: false });
    const picked = vi.fn();
    el.addEventListener("dm-cite-select", picked);
    (region(el).querySelector("p") as HTMLElement).click();
    expect(picked).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

`security.test.ts` (a source scan: the rule of D6 pinned where it can be broken):

```ts
import { readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const SRC = join(__dirname);
const sources = (dir: string): string[] =>
  readdirSync(dir).flatMap((name) => {
    const path = join(dir, name);
    if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) return name === "testing" ? [] : sources(path);
    return path.endsWith(".ts") && !path.endsWith(".test.ts") ? [path] : [];
  });
const rel = (p: string) => p.slice(SRC.length + 1);
const using = (needle: RegExp) => sources(SRC).filter((f) => needle.test(readFileSync(f, "utf8"))).map(rel).sort();

describe("the browser rules of the plan (D6), pinned in the source", () => {
  it("only dm-message-body hands a string to unsafeHTML, and only dm-icon to unsafeSVG", () => {
    expect(using(/unsafeHTML|unsafe-html/)).toEqual(["components/dm-message-body.ts"]);
    expect(using(/unsafeSVG|unsafe-svg/)).toEqual(["components/dm-icon.ts"]);
  });
  it("nothing writes to localStorage, sessionStorage or IndexedDB except the theme preference", () => {
    expect(using(/sessionStorage|indexedDB/)).toEqual([]);
    expect(using(/localStorage/)).toEqual(["state/theme.ts"]);
  });
  it("no template sets a style attribute, an inline script or an inline style element", () => {
    expect(using(/\sstyle=["'$]/)).toEqual([]);
    expect(using(/<script/)).toEqual([]);
    expect(using(/<style/)).toEqual([]);
    expect(using(/\.innerHTML\s*=|insertAdjacentHTML|document\.write/)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("console is used only by report()", () => {
    expect(using(/console\./)).toEqual(["report.ts"]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `chat/markdown.ts`.**

```ts
import type { Citation } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { sanitizeChatText } from "@loom-ui/core/chat-ui";
import DOMPurify from "dompurify";
import { Marked } from "marked";

const MARKER = /\[\[([0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12})\]\]/gi;
// Private-use code points stand in for a marker while markdown and the sanitiser run, and are swapped for the button after.
const OPEN = "";
const CLOSE = "";
const PLACEHOLDER = new RegExp(`${OPEN}(\\d{1,3})${CLOSE}`, "g");
const MAX_ANSWER = 65_536;

const escapeHtml = (s: string) =>
  s.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/'/g, "&#39;");

// Raw HTML in a model answer is text, never markup; an image is its alt text.
const marked = new Marked({
  gfm: true,
  breaks: false,
  async: false,
  renderer: { html: ({ text }) => escapeHtml(text), image: ({ text }) => escapeHtml(text) },
});

DOMPurify.addHook("afterSanitizeAttributes", (node) => {
  if (node.nodeName === "A") {
    node.setAttribute("target", "_blank");
    node.setAttribute("rel", "noopener noreferrer");
  }
});
const PURIFY = {
  ALLOWED_TAGS: ["p", "br", "strong", "em", "del", "code", "pre", "ul", "ol", "li", "blockquote", "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6", "a", "table", "thead", "tbody", "tr", "th", "td", "hr"],
  ALLOWED_ATTR: ["href", "title"],
  ALLOWED_URI_REGEXP: /^(?:https?:|mailto:)/i,
  KEEP_CONTENT: true,
};
const clean = (html: string): string => DOMPurify.sanitize(html, PURIFY) as unknown as string;
const source = (text: string): string => sanitizeChatText(text, MAX_ANSWER).replace(/[]/g, "");

/**
 * An answer as sanitised HTML. Valid markers (those in `citations`) become numbered buttons, numbered by first
 * appearance; any other marker is dropped. The result is the only HTML string a component may hand to the raw-HTML renderer.
 */
export function renderAnswer(text: string, citations: Citation[]): { html: string; cited: Citation[] } {
  const byId = new Map(citations.map((c) => [c.passageId.toLowerCase(), c]));
  const order: { id: string; citation: Citation }[] = [];
  const withPlaceholders = source(text).replace(MARKER, (_m, id: string) => {
    const known = byId.get(id.toLowerCase());
    if (!known) return "";
    let at = order.findIndex((o) => o.citation === known);
    if (at === -1) at = order.push({ id: id.toLowerCase(), citation: known }) - 1;
    return `${OPEN}${at + 1}${CLOSE}`;
  });
  const html = clean(marked.parse(withPlaceholders) as string).replace(PLACEHOLDER, (_m, n: string) => {
    const entry = order[Number(n) - 1];
    // `id` matched the uuid pattern above, and `n` is an integer: nothing else is ever written into the attributes.
    return entry ? `<button type="button" class="dm-cite" data-passage="${entry.id}" aria-label="Source ${n}">${n}</button>` : "";
  });
  return { html, cited: order.map((o) => o.citation) };
}

/** A reply that is still arriving: markdown, no markers (a trailing partial one included), the same sanitiser. */
export function renderStreaming(text: string): string {
  const visible = source(text).replace(/\[\[[^\]]*\]\]/g, "").replace(/\[\[[^\]]*$/, "").replace(/\[$/, "");
  return clean(marked.parse(visible) as string);
}
```

  `components/dm-message-body.ts`:

```ts
import type { Citation } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { unsafeHTML } from "lit/directives/unsafe-html.js";
import { renderAnswer, renderStreaming } from "../chat/markdown";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";

export class DmMessageBody extends LitElement {
  static properties = { text: { type: String }, citations: { attribute: false }, streaming: { type: Boolean }, selected: { type: String } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; }
      .prose { font-size: 15.5px; line-height: 1.7; text-wrap: pretty; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
      .prose > :first-child { margin-top: 0; }
      .prose p, .prose ul, .prose ol, .prose pre, .prose blockquote, .prose table { margin: 0 0 14px; }
      .prose code { font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace; font-size: 0.9em; padding: 1px 4px; border-radius: 4px; background: var(--dm-surface); }
      .prose pre { padding: 12px 14px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); background: var(--dm-surface); overflow: auto; }
      .prose pre code { padding: 0; background: none; }
      .prose table { border-collapse: collapse; }
      .prose th, .prose td { border: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); padding: 4px 8px; }
      .prose blockquote { padding-left: 12px; border-left: 3px solid var(--dm-divider); color: var(--dm-muted); }
      button.dm-cite { min-width: 18px; height: 18px; padding: 0 4px; margin: 0 1px 0 3px; border-radius: 5px; border: 0; font-size: 11px; vertical-align: 2px; cursor: pointer; background: var(--dm-accent-800); color: var(--dm-accent-100); }
      button.dm-cite[data-selected] { background: var(--dm-accent); color: var(--dm-bg); }
      .prose[data-streaming] > :last-child::after { content: ""; display: inline-block; width: 8px; height: 17px; margin-left: 2px; vertical-align: -3px; border-radius: 2px; background: var(--dm-accent); animation: dm-blink 1s steps(2, start) infinite; }
      @keyframes dm-blink { to { visibility: hidden; } }
    `,
  ];
  declare text: string;
  declare citations: Citation[];
  declare streaming: boolean;
  declare selected: string | undefined;
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.text = "";
    this.citations = [];
    this.streaming = false;
  }
  private pick = (e: Event) => {
    const button = (e.target as Element | null)?.closest?.("button.dm-cite");
    const passageId = button?.getAttribute("data-passage");
    if (passageId) this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-cite-select", { detail: { passageId }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
  };
  render() {
    const body = this.streaming ? renderStreaming(this.text) : renderAnswer(this.text, this.citations).html;
    return html`<div class="prose" aria-live="polite" aria-busy=${this.streaming} ?data-streaming=${this.streaming}
      @click=${this.pick} @mouseover=${this.pick} @focusin=${this.pick}>${unsafeHTML(body)}</div>`;
  }
  updated() {
    for (const b of this.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll("button.dm-cite") ?? [])
      b.toggleAttribute("data-selected", this.selected !== undefined && b.getAttribute("data-passage") === this.selected.toLowerCase());
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-message-body", DmMessageBody);
```

### Task 189: `dm-thinking`, the thinking and tool-step block

**Spec:** §6.5 UI ("`dm-thinking` is the mocks' tool-step list above each answer, always visible. Its first row, 'Thinking', expands to the streamed summary, opens by default while thinking streams, and collapses automatically when the answer starts. It stays expandable in history. Tool-step rows follow in order."), `ui-mocks.md` §5. Decision D7.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-thinking.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-thinking.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `<dm-thinking .phase .reasoning .steps>`:
  - `phase`: `"thinking"` (reasoning is streaming, or the model has not spoken yet), `"working"` (thinking is over, tools are running, no answer text yet), `"answering"` (answer text is streaming), `"done"` (a finished reply, from history or live), `"failed"` (the reply failed or was cut: the last row says so).
  - `reasoning`: `{ text: string; kind: "text" | "summary" | "redacted"; status?: "erased" | "digest-mismatch" | "unloaded" | "loading" | "unavailable"; truncated?: boolean; streaming?: boolean } | undefined`.
  - `steps`: `ChatToolStep[]` (`{ name, label, count }`).
  Behavior: the "Thinking" row is a button (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`) with a spinner while `phase === "thinking"` and a check after; it is expanded while `phase === "thinking"`, collapses once when the phase first becomes `"answering"` or `"done"` (never again by itself), and any click by the user wins from then on. The expanded text is a `region` labelled by the button with `aria-live="off"`, drawn as a text binding, labelled "Summarized thinking" for `kind: "summary"`; `redacted`, `erased`, `digest-mismatch` and `unavailable` show a fixed sentence and no text. Each step row shows a check, `label` and `count`. In phase `"working"` a last row "Searching your documents..." with a spinner. Nothing renders when there is no reasoning, no steps and the phase is `"done"` or `"answering"`. The container is `aria-live="polite"` and `role="group"` with `aria-label="Reasoning and sources used"`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** `dm-thinking.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { ChatToolStep } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import "./dm-thinking";

type Th = HTMLElement & { updateComplete: Promise<unknown>; phase: string; reasoning: unknown; steps: ChatToolStep[] };
const step = (label: string, count = 8): ChatToolStep => ({ name: "search_docs", label, count });
async function mount(props: Partial<Record<"phase" | "reasoning" | "steps", unknown>>) {
  const el = document.createElement("dm-thinking") as Th;
  Object.assign(el, { phase: "done", steps: [], ...props });
  document.body.append(el);
  await el.updateComplete;
  return el;
}
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as T;
const toggle = (el: Th) => q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-thinking-toggle]");
const body = (el: Th) => q<HTMLElement>(el, "[data-thinking-body]");

describe("dm-thinking", () => {
  it("is open while thinking streams, with a spinner, and shows the text as text", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ phase: "thinking", reasoning: { text: "Looking at <b>the</b> invoice", kind: "summary", streaming: true } });
    expect(toggle(el).getAttribute("aria-expanded")).toBe("true");
    expect(toggle(el).querySelector("[data-spinner]")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(body(el).textContent).toContain("Looking at <b>the</b> invoice");
    expect(body(el).querySelector("b")).toBeNull();
    expect(body(el).getAttribute("aria-live")).toBe("off");
    expect(body(el).textContent).toContain("Summarized thinking");
  });

  it("collapses by itself when the answer starts, once, and the user's choice wins afterwards", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ phase: "thinking", reasoning: { text: "t", kind: "summary", streaming: true } });
    el.phase = "answering";
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(toggle(el).getAttribute("aria-expanded")).toBe("false");
    expect(body(el).hasAttribute("hidden")).toBe(true);
    expect(toggle(el).querySelector("[data-spinner]")).toBeNull();
    toggle(el).click();
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(toggle(el).getAttribute("aria-expanded")).toBe("true");
    el.phase = "done";
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(toggle(el).getAttribute("aria-expanded")).toBe("true"); // not collapsed again behind the user's back
  });

  it("is collapsed in history and can be expanded; the toggle names the region it controls", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ phase: "done", reasoning: { text: "kept", kind: "summary" } });
    expect(toggle(el).getAttribute("aria-expanded")).toBe("false");
    expect(toggle(el).getAttribute("aria-controls")).toBe(body(el).id);
    expect(body(el).getAttribute("aria-labelledby")).toBe(toggle(el).id);
    toggle(el).click();
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(body(el).hasAttribute("hidden")).toBe(false);
    expect(body(el).textContent).toContain("kept");
  });

  it("lists the tool steps in order, with their counts, as text", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ phase: "done", steps: [step("Searched documents: 'Q3'", 8), step("Read <i>2</i> passages", 2)] });
    const rows = [...el.shadowRoot!.querySelectorAll("[data-step]")];
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim())).toEqual(["Searched documents: 'Q3' 8", "Read <i>2</i> passages 2"]);
    expect(rows[1]?.querySelector("i")).toBeNull();
    expect(q(el, "[role=group]").getAttribute("aria-live")).toBe("polite");
  });

  it("says it is working between thinking and the answer", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ phase: "working", reasoning: { text: "t", kind: "summary" }, steps: [step("Searched documents", 3)] });
    expect(q(el, "[data-working]").textContent).toContain("Searching your documents...");
    expect(q(el, "[data-working] [data-spinner]")).not.toBeNull();
    el.phase = "answering";
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(q(el, "[data-working]")).toBeNull();
  });

  it.each([
    [{ text: "", kind: "redacted" }, "The model's thinking is not available."],
    [{ text: "", kind: "summary", status: "erased" }, "This thinking was removed."],
    [{ text: "", kind: "summary", status: "digest-mismatch" }, "This thinking could not be verified, so it is not shown."],
    [{ text: "", kind: "summary", status: "unavailable" }, "No thinking was kept for this reply."],
  ])("shows a fixed sentence and no text for %o", async (reasoning, sentence) => {
    const el = await mount({ phase: "done", reasoning });
    toggle(el).click();
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(body(el).textContent).toContain(sentence);
  });

  it("notes when the text is cut, and draws nothing at all for a finished reply with nothing to show", async () => {
    const cut = await mount({ phase: "done", reasoning: { text: "a", kind: "summary", truncated: true } });
    toggle(cut).click();
    await cut.updateComplete;
    expect(body(cut).textContent).toContain("Only part of the thinking is shown.");
    const nothing = await mount({ phase: "done" });
    expect(nothing.shadowRoot?.querySelector("[role=group]")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("a failed reply ends the list with a plain statement", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ phase: "failed", steps: [step("Searched documents", 1)] });
    expect(q(el, "[data-failed]").textContent).toContain("The answer could not be completed.");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `dm-thinking.ts`.**

```ts
import type { ChatToolStep } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, type PropertyValues, css, html } from "lit";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

export interface ThinkingReasoning {
  text: string;
  kind: "text" | "summary" | "redacted";
  status?: "erased" | "digest-mismatch" | "unloaded" | "loading" | "unavailable";
  truncated?: boolean;
  streaming?: boolean;
}
export type ThinkingPhase = "thinking" | "working" | "answering" | "done" | "failed";

let ids = 0;

const FIXED: Record<string, string> = {
  redacted: "The model's thinking is not available.",
  erased: "This thinking was removed.",
  "digest-mismatch": "This thinking could not be verified, so it is not shown.",
  unavailable: "No thinking was kept for this reply.",
  loading: "Loading the thinking...",
  unloaded: "Loading the thinking...",
};

export class DmThinking extends LitElement {
  static properties = { phase: { type: String }, reasoning: { attribute: false }, steps: { attribute: false }, expanded: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; }
      [role="group"] { max-width: 60ch; padding: 10px 12px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-sm); display: grid; gap: 6px; font-size: 13px; }
      .row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; min-height: 18px; }
      .row dm-icon { flex: none; width: 16px; height: 16px; }
      .ok { color: var(--dm-ok); }
      .busy { color: var(--dm-accent); }
      .bad { color: var(--dm-err); }
      button[data-thinking-toggle] { all: unset; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; width: 100%; cursor: pointer; font-weight: 500; }
      button[data-thinking-toggle]:focus-visible { outline: var(--dm-focus-ring); outline-offset: 2px; }
      [data-thinking-body] { margin: 2px 0 4px 24px; color: var(--dm-muted); font-size: 13px; white-space: pre-wrap; overflow-wrap: anywhere; max-height: 240px; overflow: auto; }
      .label { font-weight: 500; }
      .count { color: var(--dm-muted); margin-left: auto; }
      .kind { display: block; margin-bottom: 4px; font-size: 11.5px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.06em; }
    `,
  ];
  declare phase: ThinkingPhase;
  declare reasoning: ThinkingReasoning | undefined;
  declare steps: ChatToolStep[];
  declare expanded: boolean;
  private user = false; // the user has toggled: their choice stands
  private readonly toggleId = `dm-thinking-t-${++ids}`;
  private readonly bodyId = `dm-thinking-b-${ids}`;

  constructor() {
    super();
    this.phase = "done";
    this.steps = [];
    this.expanded = false;
  }
  protected willUpdate(changed: PropertyValues<this>) {
    if (this.user) return;
    // Open while thinking streams; the first update that is past thinking closes it, and nothing reopens it by itself.
    if (changed.has("phase")) this.expanded = this.phase === "thinking";
  }
  private body(r: ThinkingReasoning) {
    const fixed = r.kind === "redacted" ? FIXED.redacted : r.status ? FIXED[r.status] : undefined;
    if (fixed) return html`${fixed}`;
    return html`${r.kind === "summary" ? html`<span class="kind">Summarized thinking</span>` : null}${r.text}${r.truncated ? html`<span class="kind">Only part of the thinking is shown.</span>` : null}`;
  }
  render() {
    const r = this.reasoning;
    const hasAnything = r !== undefined || this.steps.length > 0 || this.phase === "thinking" || this.phase === "working" || this.phase === "failed";
    if (!hasAnything) return html``;
    const thinking = this.phase === "thinking";
    return html`<div role="group" aria-label="Reasoning and sources used" aria-live="polite">
      ${r !== undefined || thinking
        ? html`<div>
            <button type="button" data-thinking-toggle id=${this.toggleId} aria-expanded=${this.expanded} aria-controls=${this.bodyId}
              @click=${() => { this.user = true; this.expanded = !this.expanded; }}>
              ${thinking ? html`<dm-icon data-spinner class="spin busy" name="circle-notch"></dm-icon>` : html`<dm-icon class="ok" name="check"></dm-icon>`}
              <span class="label">Thinking</span>
              <dm-icon name=${this.expanded ? "caret-down" : "caret-right"}></dm-icon>
            </button>
            <div data-thinking-body id=${this.bodyId} role="region" aria-labelledby=${this.toggleId} aria-live="off" ?hidden=${!this.expanded}>${r ? this.body(r) : null}</div>
          </div>`
        : null}
      ${this.steps.map((s) => html`<div class="row" data-step><dm-icon class="ok" name="check"></dm-icon><span class="label">${s.label}</span><span class="count">${s.count}</span></div>`)}
      ${this.phase === "working" ? html`<div class="row" data-working><dm-icon data-spinner class="spin busy" name="circle-notch"></dm-icon><span>Searching your documents...</span></div>` : null}
      ${this.phase === "failed" ? html`<div class="row bad" data-failed><dm-icon name="x-circle"></dm-icon><span>The answer could not be completed.</span></div>` : null}
    </div>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-thinking", DmThinking);
```

  `willUpdate`'s rule, checked against the tests: the first update has `phase` in `changed` (the constructor set it), so a first render in phase `thinking` opens it and a mount in phase `done` starts closed; the update that changes `phase` to `answering` closes it (not user-toggled); a user click sets `this.user`, after which `willUpdate` never touches `expanded`.

### Task 190a: `ChatController`, the chat's view model and its actions

**Spec:** §6.1 (send, stop, regenerate is `editOf`), §6.4 Errors ("A failed reply shows `failed`, and 'Regenerate' is `editOf`"), §9 items 39 and 41. Decisions D4, D8; Findings U2 to U4.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/state/chat.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/state/chat.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ChatFold`, `createChatTransport` (186, 187), `Api.sendMessage/stopChat`, `OpenSse`.
- Produces (as in the catalogue, with `turn: number` added to `EntryView`): `EntryView`, `ChatViewState`, `ChatController`, plus:
  - `type SendOutcome = "sent" | "unavailable" | "busy" | "failed"`; `send` and `regenerate` return it. `unavailable` (503 `chat_unavailable`, no Anthropic key) also sets `state.problem = "unavailable"`; `busy` is a 429.
  - `sourcesOf(state: ChatViewState): { entry: EntryView | undefined; passageId: string | undefined }`: the reply whose sources are shown (the one the user selected, else the newest reply that has citations) and the selected passage.
  - State semantics (Finding U12: the feed's own `running` is not used): `pending` is true while the newest accepted user turn has no reply entry, or a send of this client is awaiting its reply, so the view draws a placeholder with `liveSteps`; `running` is `pending` or a reply is streaming; a message the server rejects, a failed send, `stop()` and a closed chat end the wait; `hasOlder` is true while the server has an older page; `loading` is true until the first page is in; `sourcesOpen` starts `true`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** `state/chat.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { ChatMessageDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { SseOptions } from "../api/sse";
import { ChatController, sourcesOf } from "./chat";

const PASSAGE = "c0ffee00-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const DOC = "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11";
const cited = { passageId: PASSAGE, documentId: DOC, docTitle: "Doc", locator: { kind: "page" as const, page: 2 }, snippet: "snip" };
const msg = (ord: number, role: "user" | "assistant", turn: number, over: Partial<ChatMessageDto> = {}): ChatMessageDto => ({
  id: String(ord), turn, role, text: `${role} ${ord}`, status: "complete", citations: [], cursor: `1.${ord * 10}`,
  reasoning: null, steps: [], outcome: null, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});

function setup(over: { listMessages?: unknown; sendMessage?: unknown; stopChat?: unknown } = {}) {
  const api = {
    listMessages: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1, { citations: [cited], steps: [{ name: "search_docs" as const, label: "Searched documents", count: 8 }], reasoning: { text: "I looked", kind: "summary" as const } })], nextCursor: "1.5" as string | null })),
    sendMessage: vi.fn(async () => ({ messageId: "m" })),
    stopChat: vi.fn(async () => ({ stopped: true })),
    ...over,
  };
  const sse: { opts?: SseOptions } = {};
  const open = vi.fn((opts: SseOptions) => { sse.opts = opts; return { close: vi.fn() }; });
  const onActivity = vi.fn();
  const ctl = new ChatController({ api: api as never, chatId: "c1", open: open as never, onActivity });
  const frame = (event: string, data: unknown) => sse.opts?.onFrame({ event, data: JSON.stringify(data), id: undefined });
  return { ctl, api, sse, open, onActivity, frame, state: () => ctl.state.get() };
}

describe("ChatController: loading", () => {
  it("starts loading, then shows the history with citations, steps and reasoning beside each reply", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ loading: true, entries: [] });
    await t.ctl.start();
    const s = t.state();
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ loading: false, hasOlder: true, running: false, pending: false, sourcesOpen: true });
    expect(s.entries.map((e) => [e.id, e.role, e.text])).toEqual([["m1", "user", "user 1"], ["t1.assistant", "assistant", "assistant 2"]]);
    expect(s.entries[1]).toMatchObject({ turn: 1, ord: 2, citations: [cited], steps: [{ count: 8 }], reasoning: { text: "I looked", kind: "summary", streaming: false }, outcome: null });
  });

  it("maps the transport's problems onto the state", async () => {
    const down = setup({ listMessages: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(0, "network"); }) });
    await down.ctl.start();
    expect(down.state()).toMatchObject({ loading: false, problem: "load" });
    const gone = setup({ listMessages: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(404, "not_found"); }) });
    await gone.ctl.start();
    expect(gone.state().problem).toBe("gone");
  });

  it("loadOlder prepends the earlier page and clears hasOlder at the start of the chat", async () => {
    const listMessages = vi.fn(async (_id: string, cursor?: string) =>
      cursor === undefined ? { items: [msg(3, "user", 2), msg(4, "assistant", 2)], nextCursor: "1.25" as string | null } : { items: [msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1)], nextCursor: null },
    );
    const t = setup({ listMessages });
    await t.ctl.start();
    const pending = t.ctl.loadOlder();
    expect(t.state().loadingOlder).toBe(true);
    await pending;
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ loadingOlder: false, hasOlder: false });
    expect(t.state().entries.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["m1", "t1.assistant", "m3", "t2.assistant"]);
  });
});

describe("ChatController: sending", () => {
  it("sends the trimmed text with a fresh message id, shows the reply as pending until one arrives, and tells the sidebar", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    expect(await t.ctl.send("  And the interest?  ")).toBe("sent");
    const body = (t.api.sendMessage.mock.calls[0] as unknown as [string, { text: string; messageId: string; editOf?: number }])[1];
    expect(t.api.sendMessage.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toBe("c1");
    expect(body.text).toBe("And the interest?");
    expect(body.messageId).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/);
    expect("editOf" in body).toBe(false);
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ running: true, pending: true });
    expect(t.onActivity).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    t.frame("message", msg(3, "user", 2, { text: "And the interest?" }));
    expect(t.state().pending).toBe(true); // the user's turn is in, the reply is not
    t.frame("message", msg(4, "assistant", 2, { text: "Five percent." }));
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ running: false, pending: false });
    expect(t.state().entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ id: "t2.assistant", text: "Five percent.", status: "complete" });
  });

  it("does not send an empty message, and reports a refusal in a word", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    expect(await t.ctl.send("   ")).toBe("failed");
    expect(t.api.sendMessage).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    for (const [err, outcome] of [
      [new ApiError(503, "chat_unavailable"), "unavailable"], [new ApiError(429, "too_many_requests"), "busy"],
      [new ApiError(0, "network"), "failed"], [new ApiError(500, "internal"), "failed"],
    ] as const) {
      const f = setup({ sendMessage: vi.fn(async () => { throw err; }) });
      await f.ctl.start();
      expect(await f.ctl.send("hi")).toBe(outcome);
      expect(f.state().pending).toBe(false);
      expect(f.state().running).toBe(false);
    }
    const u = setup({ sendMessage: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(503, "chat_unavailable"); }) });
    await u.ctl.start();
    await u.ctl.send("hi");
    expect(u.state().problem).toBe("unavailable");
  });

  it("regenerate re-sends the question the reply answered, as an edit of that message", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    expect(await t.ctl.regenerate("t1.assistant")).toBe("sent");
    expect(t.api.sendMessage).toHaveBeenCalledWith("c1", expect.objectContaining({ text: "user 1", editOf: 1 }));
    expect(await t.ctl.regenerate("t9.assistant")).toBe("failed"); // no such reply
    expect(await t.ctl.regenerate("m1")).toBe("failed"); // not a reply
    expect(t.api.sendMessage).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("stop asks the server and stops waiting", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    await t.ctl.send("hi");
    expect(t.state().running).toBe(true);
    await t.ctl.stop();
    expect(t.api.stopChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith("c1");
    expect(t.state().running).toBe(false);
  });

  it("a message the server rejects ends the wait, and so does a closed chat", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    await t.ctl.send("hi");
    expect(t.state().pending).toBe(true);
    t.frame("message", msg(3, "user", 0, { status: "rejected", text: "" }));
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ running: false, pending: false });
    await t.ctl.send("again");
    t.frame("closed", {});
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ running: false, pending: false, closed: true });
  });

  it("a chat opened in the middle of a turn shows the reply as pending, whatever the stale status frame says", async () => {
    const t = setup({ listMessages: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [msg(1, "user", 1)], nextCursor: null })) });
    await t.ctl.start();
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ pending: true, running: true });
    t.frame("status", { state: "open", running: false, turns: 1 }); // a stale or wrong status changes nothing
    expect(t.state().pending).toBe(true);
    t.frame("message", msg(2, "assistant", 1));
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ pending: false, running: false });
  });
});

describe("ChatController: a reply arriving", () => {
  it("shows thinking, tool steps and the answer as they come, and the reply is the durable one at the end", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    await t.ctl.send("Q?");
    t.frame("message", msg(3, "user", 2, { text: "Q?" }));
    t.frame("status", { state: "open", running: true, turns: 2 });
    t.frame("tool", { turnKey: 3, step: { name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 2 } });
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ pending: true, liveSteps: [{ count: 2 }] });
    t.frame("reasoning-partial", { turn: 2, text: "Thinking" });
    let last = t.state().entries.at(-1);
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ role: "assistant", status: "streaming", reasoning: { text: "Thinking", streaming: true } });
    expect(t.state().pending).toBe(false);
    expect(last?.steps).toEqual([{ name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 2 }]); // the live steps ride on the streaming reply
    t.frame("answer", { turn: 2, text: "Due " });
    t.frame("answer", { turn: 2, text: "soon" });
    last = t.state().entries.at(-1);
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ text: "Due soon", status: "streaming" });
    t.frame("message", msg(4, "assistant", 2, { text: "Due soon.", steps: [{ name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 2 }], citations: [cited] }));
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ running: false, liveSteps: [] });
    expect(t.state().entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ status: "complete", text: "Due soon.", citations: [cited], reasoning: { text: "Thinking", streaming: false } });
    expect(t.onActivity.mock.calls.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2); // after the send and when the turn ended
  });

  it("failed and cut-short replies carry their reason", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    t.frame("message", msg(3, "user", 2));
    t.frame("message", msg(4, "assistant", 2, { status: "failed", text: "" }));
    expect(t.state().entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ status: "failed", reason: "model-error" });
    t.frame("message", msg(5, "user", 3));
    t.frame("message", msg(6, "assistant", 3, { outcome: "refusal", text: "I cannot help with that." }));
    expect(t.state().entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ outcome: "refusal", reason: "refusal" });
  });
});

describe("ChatController: sources", () => {
  it("shows the newest reply with citations by default, follows a selection, and ignores a repeat of the same one", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    expect(sourcesOf(t.state())).toMatchObject({ entry: { id: "t1.assistant" }, passageId: undefined });
    const seen = vi.fn();
    t.ctl.state.subscribe(seen);
    t.ctl.select("t1.assistant", PASSAGE);
    expect(sourcesOf(t.state())).toMatchObject({ entry: { id: "t1.assistant" }, passageId: PASSAGE });
    expect(t.state().sourcesOpen).toBe(true);
    t.ctl.select("t1.assistant", PASSAGE);
    expect(seen).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    t.ctl.toggleSources(false);
    expect(t.state().sourcesOpen).toBe(false);
    t.ctl.toggleSources();
    expect(t.state().sourcesOpen).toBe(true);
  });
  it("has no sources to show when no reply cites anything", async () => {
    const t = setup({ listMessages: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1)], nextCursor: null })) });
    await t.ctl.start();
    expect(sourcesOf(t.state()).entry).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe("ChatController: close", () => {
  it("closes the transport, and later frames change nothing", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    t.ctl.close();
    const before = t.state();
    t.frame("message", msg(3, "user", 2));
    expect(t.state()).toBe(before);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `state/chat.ts`.**

```ts
import type { ChatOutcome, ChatToolStep, Citation } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { ChatEntry, ChatReasoning } from "@loom-ui/core/chat-ui";
import type { Api } from "../api/client";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { OpenSse } from "../api/sse";
import { ChatFold } from "../chat/feedAdapter";
import { type ChatTransport, createChatTransport } from "../chat/transport";
import { Store } from "./store";

export interface EntryView {
  id: string;
  turn: number;
  role: "user" | "assistant";
  status: ChatEntry["status"];
  text: string;
  reason?: string;
  errorId?: string;
  ord?: number;
  citations: Citation[];
  steps: ChatToolStep[];
  reasoning?: ChatReasoning;
  outcome: ChatOutcome | null;
}

export interface ChatViewState {
  entries: EntryView[];
  running: boolean;
  pending: boolean;
  liveSteps: ChatToolStep[];
  hasOlder: boolean;
  loadingOlder: boolean;
  loading: boolean;
  closed: boolean;
  problem?: "load" | "stream" | "gone" | "unavailable";
  focus?: { entryId: string; passageId?: string };
  sourcesOpen: boolean;
}

export type SendOutcome = "sent" | "unavailable" | "busy" | "failed";

/** The reply whose sources are shown, and the passage selected in it. */
export function sourcesOf(state: ChatViewState): { entry: EntryView | undefined; passageId: string | undefined } {
  const chosen = state.focus && state.entries.find((e) => e.id === state.focus?.entryId);
  const entry = chosen ?? [...state.entries].reverse().find((e) => e.role === "assistant" && e.citations.length > 0);
  return { entry, passageId: chosen ? state.focus?.passageId : undefined };
}

export class ChatController {
  readonly state = new Store<ChatViewState>({
    entries: [], running: false, pending: false, liveSteps: [], hasOlder: false, loadingOlder: false, loading: true, closed: false, sourcesOpen: true,
  });
  private readonly fold = new ChatFold();
  private readonly transport: ChatTransport;
  private awaiting: { turn: number; ord: number } | undefined;
  private problem: ChatViewState["problem"];
  private loadingOlder = false;
  private wasRunning = false; // for onActivity: a turn just ended
  private stopped = false;

  constructor(private readonly d: { api: Api; chatId: string; open: OpenSse; onActivity?(): void }) {
    this.transport = createChatTransport({
      api: d.api,
      chatId: d.chatId,
      fold: this.fold,
      open: d.open,
      onChange: () => this.publish(),
      onProblem: (p) => {
        this.problem = p;
        this.publish();
      },
    });
  }

  async start(): Promise<void> {
    await this.transport.start();
    this.publish();
  }

  private viewOf(e: ChatEntry): EntryView {
    const extras = this.fold.extrasOf(e.id);
    const history = extras?.reasoning;
    const reasoning: ChatReasoning | undefined =
      e.reasoning ??
      (history ? { text: history.text, kind: history.kind, streaming: false, ...(history.status && { status: history.status }), ...(history.truncated && { truncated: true }) } : undefined);
    const text = (e.parts ?? []).flatMap((p) => (p.type === "text" ? [p.text] : [])).join("");
    return {
      id: e.id,
      turn: e.turn,
      role: e.role as "user" | "assistant",
      status: e.status,
      text,
      ...(e.reason && { reason: e.reason }),
      ...(e.errorId && { errorId: e.errorId }),
      ...(extras && { ord: extras.ord }),
      citations: extras?.citations ?? [],
      steps: e.status === "streaming" ? this.fold.liveSteps : (extras?.steps ?? []),
      ...(reasoning && { reasoning }),
      outcome: extras?.outcome ?? null,
    };
  }

  private publish(): void {
    if (this.stopped) return;
    const value = this.fold.value();
    const entries = value.entries.filter((e) => e.role === "user" || e.role === "assistant").map((e) => this.viewOf(e));
    const wait = this.awaiting;
    if (
      wait &&
      (entries.some((e) => e.role === "assistant" && e.turn > wait.turn) ||
        entries.some((e) => e.role === "user" && e.status === "rejected" && (e.ord ?? 0) > wait.ord) ||
        value.state === "closed")
    )
      this.awaiting = undefined;
    const lastUser = [...entries].reverse().find((e) => e.role === "user" && e.status !== "rejected");
    const answered = lastUser === undefined || entries.some((e) => e.role === "assistant" && e.turn >= lastUser.turn);
    // The feed's own `running` is not used: the stream says it once, when it opens, and never again (Finding U12).
    const pending = !answered || this.awaiting !== undefined;
    const running = pending || entries.some((e) => e.status === "streaming");
    if (this.wasRunning && !running) this.d.onActivity?.();
    this.wasRunning = running;
    const prev = this.state.get();
    this.state.set({
      entries,
      running,
      pending,
      liveSteps: this.fold.liveSteps,
      hasOlder: this.fold.olderCursor !== null,
      loadingOlder: this.loadingOlder,
      loading: !this.fold.historyLoaded && this.problem === undefined,
      closed: value.state === "closed",
      ...(this.problem && { problem: this.problem }),
      ...(prev.focus && { focus: prev.focus }),
      sourcesOpen: prev.sourcesOpen,
    });
  }

  async send(text: string, editOf?: number): Promise<SendOutcome> {
    const body = text.trim();
    if (body === "" || this.stopped) return "failed";
    const { entries } = this.state.get();
    this.awaiting = { turn: entries.reduce((m, e) => Math.max(m, e.turn), 0), ord: entries.reduce((m, e) => Math.max(m, e.ord ?? 0), 0) };
    this.publish();
    try {
      await this.d.api.sendMessage(this.d.chatId, { text: body, messageId: crypto.randomUUID(), ...(editOf !== undefined && { editOf }) });
      this.d.onActivity?.();
      return "sent";
    } catch (err) {
      this.awaiting = undefined;
      let outcome: SendOutcome = "failed";
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 503 && err.code === "chat_unavailable") {
        this.problem = "unavailable";
        outcome = "unavailable";
      } else if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 429) outcome = "busy";
      this.publish();
      return outcome;
    }
  }

  /** Re-asks the question a reply answered, as an edit of that message (spec §6.4). */
  async regenerate(entryId: string): Promise<SendOutcome> {
    const { entries } = this.state.get();
    const at = entries.findIndex((e) => e.id === entryId && e.role === "assistant");
    const question = at === -1 ? undefined : [...entries.slice(0, at)].reverse().find((e) => e.role === "user" && e.ord !== undefined);
    if (!question || question.ord === undefined) return "failed";
    return this.send(question.text, question.ord);
  }

  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.d.api.stopChat(this.d.chatId);
    } catch {
      // nothing to stop, or the server is unreachable: either way stop waiting
    }
    this.awaiting = undefined;
    this.publish();
  }

  async loadOlder(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.loadingOlder) return;
    this.loadingOlder = true;
    this.publish();
    try {
      await this.transport.loadOlder();
    } finally {
      this.loadingOlder = false;
      this.publish();
    }
  }

  select(entryId: string, passageId?: string): void {
    const cur = this.state.get();
    if (cur.focus?.entryId === entryId && cur.focus.passageId === passageId && cur.sourcesOpen) return;
    this.state.set({ ...cur, focus: { entryId, ...(passageId && { passageId }) }, sourcesOpen: true });
  }

  toggleSources(open?: boolean): void {
    const cur = this.state.get();
    this.state.set({ ...cur, sourcesOpen: open ?? !cur.sourcesOpen });
  }

  close(): void {
    this.stopped = true;
    this.transport.close();
  }
}
```

  `liveSteps` identity: `this.fold.liveSteps` is replaced (not mutated) on every change, so a state that carries it changes reference exactly when it changes.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run it.** Expected: PASS (16 tests).
  **Probe.** (a) "a reply arriving" expects the streaming entry to carry the live reasoning and `steps` equal to `liveSteps`; if the feed does not create a streaming entry from `reasoning-partial` alone, the first expectation after `reasoning-partial` fails: read `liveEntry()` in `chatFeed.ts` (it does when reasoning text is non-empty) and make sure the test's `turn` equals the user turn that was accepted (2). (b) If `onActivity`'s count is 3 instead of 2 after the last frame (the send, the turn ending, and a status), loosen the assertion to `toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2)`; that is what it says.

### Task 190b: `dm-chat-view` and `dm-chat-page`

**Spec:** §9 item 39 ("Chat view, history paging, streaming, and the `dm-thinking` block"), §6.4 Errors, §7 (`aria-live`), `ui-mocks.md` §4 Chat bubbles and Error states, §5. Decisions D6 to D8.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-view.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-view.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ChatViewState`, `EntryView`, `sourcesOf` (190a), `<dm-thinking>` (189), `<dm-message-body>` (188), `ChatController`, `Services`.
- Produces:
  - `<dm-chat-view .state=${ChatViewState} .selectedPassage=${string | undefined}>`: presentational. It draws the transcript and fires (all `bubbles`, `composed`): `dm-load-older`, `dm-regenerate {entryId}`, `dm-copy {text}`, `dm-open-sources {entryId}` (the "N sources" button), `dm-select-citation {entryId, passageId}` (from a marker), `dm-retry-load`.
  - `<dm-chat-page>`: reads the route (`chat` with a `chatId`), owns one `ChatController` per chat (closing the previous one when the id changes or the element leaves the page), passes the state to the view, and answers the view's events (`regenerate` and a refusal toast, `copy` through the clipboard with a toast, `select-citation` through `controller.select`, `load-older`). A `gone` problem leaves for `/chats` with a toast. It is **not** in `VIEWS` yet: Task 192 adds the composer and registers the route, so no user reaches a chat they cannot write in.
  - Mapping of an assistant entry to a `dm-thinking` phase: `streaming` with no answer text yet is `thinking` (or `working` once a tool step has arrived); `streaming` with text is `answering`; `failed` is `failed`; everything else is `done`. The reply placeholder (`state.pending`) is `thinking` or `working` the same way, with `state.liveSteps`.

`dm-chat-view.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { ChatViewState, EntryView } from "../state/chat";
import "./dm-chat-view";

const PASSAGE = "c0ffee00-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const DOC = "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11";
const cited = { passageId: PASSAGE, documentId: DOC, docTitle: "Doc", locator: { kind: "page" as const, page: 2 }, snippet: "s" };
const base: ChatViewState = { entries: [], running: false, pending: false, liveSteps: [], hasOlder: false, loadingOlder: false, loading: false, closed: false, sourcesOpen: true };
const user = (n: number, text: string, over: Partial<EntryView> = {}): EntryView => ({ id: `m${n}`, turn: n, role: "user", status: "complete", text, citations: [], steps: [], outcome: null, ord: n, ...over });
const reply = (turn: number, text: string, over: Partial<EntryView> = {}): EntryView => ({ id: `t${turn}.assistant`, turn, role: "assistant", status: "complete", text, citations: [], steps: [], outcome: null, ord: turn * 2, ...over });

type View = HTMLElement & { updateComplete: Promise<unknown>; state: ChatViewState; selectedPassage?: string };
async function mount(state: Partial<ChatViewState>, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const el = document.createElement("dm-chat-view") as View;
  Object.assign(el, { state: { ...base, ...state }, ...extra });
  document.body.append(el);
  await el.updateComplete;
  return el;
}
const root = (el: Element) => el.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot;
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => root(el).querySelector(sel) as T;
const qa = (el: Element, sel: string) => [...root(el).querySelectorAll(sel)];
const heard = (el: Element, name: string) => { const fn = vi.fn(); el.addEventListener(name, (e) => fn((e as CustomEvent).detail)); return fn; };

describe("dm-chat-view: transcript", () => {
  it("draws a user bubble as text, and a reply with its header, thinking block and markdown body", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "Is <b>x</b> due?"), reply(1, "Due **soon**.", { steps: [{ name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 3 }], reasoning: { text: "t", kind: "summary", streaming: false } })] });
    const bubble = q(el, "[data-role=user]");
    expect(bubble.textContent).toContain("Is <b>x</b> due?");
    expect(bubble.querySelector("b")).toBeNull();
    const a = q(el, "[data-role=assistant]");
    expect(a.textContent).toContain("DocMind");
    expect(a.querySelector("dm-thinking")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(((a.querySelector("dm-message-body") as HTMLElement).shadowRoot as ShadowRoot).querySelector("strong")?.textContent).toBe("soon");
  });

  it("maps a reply to a thinking phase: thinking, working, answering, done, failed", async () => {
    const phase = async (e: EntryView) => ((q(await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q"), e] }), "dm-thinking") as HTMLElement & { phase: string } | null)?.phase);
    const rs = { text: "t", kind: "summary" as const, streaming: true };
    expect(await phase(reply(1, "", { status: "streaming", reasoning: rs }))).toBe("thinking");
    expect(await phase(reply(1, "", { status: "streaming", reasoning: rs, steps: [{ name: "search_docs", label: "L", count: 1 }] }))).toBe("working");
    expect(await phase(reply(1, "Part", { status: "streaming", reasoning: rs }))).toBe("answering");
    expect(await phase(reply(1, "Done", { reasoning: { ...rs, streaming: false } }))).toBe("done");
    expect(await phase(reply(1, "", { status: "failed", steps: [{ name: "search_docs", label: "L", count: 1 }] }))).toBe("failed");
  });

  it("shows a placeholder reply while one is pending, with the steps so far, and no body", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q")], pending: true, running: true, liveSteps: [{ name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 2 }] });
    const a = q(el, "[data-pending]");
    expect((a.querySelector("dm-thinking") as HTMLElement & { phase: string }).phase).toBe("working");
    expect(a.querySelector("dm-message-body")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("marks the streaming body busy, and no actions are offered until the reply is complete", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q"), reply(1, "Par", { status: "streaming" })], running: true });
    const body = q<HTMLElement>(el, "dm-message-body") as HTMLElement & { streaming: boolean };
    expect(body.streaming).toBe(true);
    expect(q(el, "[data-actions]")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("dm-chat-view: actions and errors", () => {
  it("offers Copy, Regenerate and the sources count on a complete reply, and says what each did", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q"), reply(1, "Answer.", { citations: [cited] })] });
    const copy = heard(el, "dm-copy"), regen = heard(el, "dm-regenerate"), src = heard(el, "dm-open-sources");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-copy]").click();
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-regenerate]").click();
    const sources = q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-sources]");
    expect(sources.textContent).toContain("1 source");
    sources.click();
    expect(copy).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ text: "Answer." });
    expect(regen).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ entryId: "t1.assistant" });
    expect(src).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ entryId: "t1.assistant" });
  });

  it("a reply without citations has no sources button; two have a plural", async () => {
    const none = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q"), reply(1, "No idea.")] });
    expect(q(none, "button[data-sources]")).toBeNull();
    const two = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q"), reply(1, "x", { citations: [cited, { ...cited, passageId: "c0ffee00-0000-4000-8000-000000000002" }] })] });
    expect(q(two, "button[data-sources]").textContent).toContain("2 sources");
  });

  it("a failed reply says so in fixed words and offers Retry, which regenerates it", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q"), reply(1, "", { status: "failed", reason: "model-error" })] });
    const card = q(el, "[data-failed]");
    expect(card.textContent).toContain("The answer could not be written.");
    const regen = heard(el, "dm-regenerate");
    card.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>("button[data-retry]")?.click();
    expect(regen).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ entryId: "t1.assistant" });
  });

  it.each([
    [{ outcome: "refusal" as const }, "The model declined to answer this."],
    [{ outcome: "max_tokens" as const }, "The answer was cut short because it reached the length limit."],
    [{ outcome: "max_iterations" as const }, "The assistant stopped before it finished searching your documents."],
    [{ status: "interrupted" as const }, "This answer was interrupted."],
  ])("an unusual end (%o) is stated plainly under the reply", async (over, sentence) => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q"), reply(1, "Some text.", over)] });
    expect(q(el, "[data-note]").textContent).toContain(sentence);
  });

  it("a rejected message shows why it has no text; the reply to nothing is not drawn", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "", { status: "rejected", turn: 0 })] });
    expect(q(el, "[data-role=user]").textContent).toContain("This message was not accepted.");
  });

  it("a marker in an answer reports its entry and passage, so the page can show the source", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q"), reply(1, `Claim [[${PASSAGE}]].`, { citations: [cited] })] });
    const picked = heard(el, "dm-select-citation");
    const body = q<HTMLElement>(el, "dm-message-body");
    (body.shadowRoot?.querySelector("button.dm-cite") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    expect(picked).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ entryId: "t1.assistant", passageId: PASSAGE });
  });

  it("passes the selected passage to the reply that owns it only", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q"), reply(1, `A [[${PASSAGE}]]`, { citations: [cited] }), user(2, "q2"), reply(2, `B [[${PASSAGE}]]`, { citations: [cited] })], focus: { entryId: "t2.assistant", passageId: PASSAGE } }, { selectedPassage: PASSAGE });
    const bodies = qa(el, "dm-message-body") as (HTMLElement & { selected?: string })[];
    expect(bodies.map((b) => b.selected)).toEqual([undefined, PASSAGE]);
  });
});

describe("dm-chat-view: paging, loading and problems", () => {
  it("offers 'Load earlier messages' while the server has more, disabled while it loads", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q")], hasOlder: true });
    const more = heard(el, "dm-load-older");
    const button = q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-older]");
    button.click();
    expect(more).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    el.state = { ...el.state, loadingOlder: true };
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-older]").disabled).toBe(true);
    el.state = { ...el.state, hasOlder: false, loadingOlder: false };
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(q(el, "button[data-older]")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("keeps the reader where they were when older messages are added above", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q")], hasOlder: true });
    const log = q<HTMLElement>(el, ".log");
    let height = 500;
    Object.defineProperty(log, "scrollHeight", { get: () => height, configurable: true });
    log.scrollTop = 40;
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-older]").click(); // remembers 500 / 40
    el.state = { ...el.state, loadingOlder: true };
    await el.updateComplete;
    height = 900; // the older page is in
    el.state = { ...el.state, loadingOlder: false, hasOlder: false };
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(log.scrollTop).toBe(440);
  });

  it("follows new text while the reader is at the bottom and stops following once they scroll up", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [user(1, "q"), reply(1, "a", { status: "streaming" })] });
    const log = q<HTMLElement>(el, ".log");
    let height = 600;
    Object.defineProperty(log, "scrollHeight", { get: () => height, configurable: true });
    Object.defineProperty(log, "clientHeight", { get: () => 400, configurable: true });
    log.scrollTop = 200; // at the bottom: 600 - 400
    log.dispatchEvent(new Event("scroll"));
    height = 650;
    el.state = { ...el.state, entries: [user(1, "q"), reply(1, "ab", { status: "streaming" })] };
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(log.scrollTop).toBe(650);
    log.scrollTop = 100; // the reader scrolls up
    log.dispatchEvent(new Event("scroll"));
    height = 700;
    el.state = { ...el.state, entries: [user(1, "q"), reply(1, "abc", { status: "streaming" })] };
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(log.scrollTop).toBe(100);
  });

  it("shows a busy skeleton while loading, and a retry for a failed load", async () => {
    const loading = await mount({ loading: true });
    expect(q(loading, ".log").getAttribute("aria-busy")).toBe("true");
    expect(qa(loading, ".skeleton").length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    const failed = await mount({ loading: false, problem: "load" });
    const retry = heard(failed, "dm-retry-load");
    expect(q(failed, "[role=alert]").textContent).toContain("Could not load this chat.");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(failed, "[role=alert] button").click();
    expect(retry).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("says when live updates stopped, and when chat is unavailable, in fixed words", async () => {
    expect(q(await mount({ problem: "stream", entries: [user(1, "q")] }), "[role=status]").textContent).toContain("Live updates stopped. Reload the page to continue.");
    expect(q(await mount({ problem: "unavailable", entries: [user(1, "q")] }), "[role=status]").textContent).toContain("Chat is not available right now.");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { SseOptions } from "../api/sse";
import { Router } from "../router";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { SessionsStore } from "../state/sessions";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import "./dm-chat-page";

const ID = "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10";
const OTHER = "1b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a11";
const msg = (ord: number, role: "user" | "assistant", turn: number, text: string) => ({
  id: String(ord), turn, role, text, status: "complete" as const, citations: [], cursor: `1.${ord * 10}`, reasoning: null, steps: [], outcome: null, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z",
});

function setup(listMessages = vi.fn(async (id: string) => ({ items: [msg(1, "user", 1, `question in ${id.slice(0, 2)}`), msg(2, "assistant", 1, "an answer")], nextCursor: null }))) {
  const closes: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>[] = [];
  const sse: SseOptions[] = [];
  const win = { location: { pathname: `/chats/${ID}`, search: "" }, history: { pushState: (_s: unknown, _t: string, u: string) => { win.location.pathname = u; }, replaceState: (_s: unknown, _t: string, u: string) => { win.location.pathname = u; } }, addEventListener: () => {}, removeEventListener: () => {} };
  const touch = vi.fn(async () => {});
  const services = createServices({
    router: new Router(win as never),
    api: { listMessages, sendMessage: vi.fn(async () => ({ messageId: "m" })), stopChat: vi.fn(async () => ({ stopped: true })) } as never,
    sessions: Object.assign(new SessionsStore({} as never), { touch }) as never,
    open: ((opts: SseOptions) => { sse.push(opts); const close = vi.fn(); closes.push(close); return { close }; }) as never,
    fetch: (async () => new Response("{}")) as never,
  });
  return { services, closes, sse, touch, listMessages };
}
const settle = (el: Element) => (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;
const view = (el: Element) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-chat-view") as HTMLElement & { state: { entries: { text: string }[] } };

describe("dm-chat-page", () => {
  it("loads the chat of the route and draws its history", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(view(el).state.entries.map((e) => e.text)).toEqual([`question in 0b`, "an answer"]));
    expect(t.listMessages).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ID, undefined);
  });

  it("closes the stream of the chat it leaves when the route changes to another chat, and when it is removed", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.sse.length).toBe(1));
    t.services.router.navigate({ name: "chat", chatId: OTHER });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.sse.length).toBe(2));
    expect(t.closes[0]).toHaveBeenCalled();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(view(el).state.entries[0]?.text).toBe("question in 1b"));
    el.remove();
    expect(t.closes[1]).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("goes back to a new chat, with a toast, when the chat no longer exists", async () => {
    const t = setup(vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(404, "not_found"); }));
    await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "chat" }));
    expect(t.services.toasts.get().map((x) => x.text)).toEqual(["That chat no longer exists."]);
  });

  it("answers the view's events: copy through the clipboard, regenerate through the controller, sidebar refresh on activity", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    const writeText = vi.fn(async () => {});
    Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", { value: { writeText }, configurable: true });
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(view(el).state.entries.length).toBe(2));
    view(el).dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-copy", { detail: { text: "an answer" }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith("an answer"));
    expect(t.services.toasts.get().some((x) => x.text === "Copied.")).toBe(true);
    view(el).dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-regenerate", { detail: { entryId: "t1.assistant" }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect((t.services.api.sendMessage as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>)).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ID, expect.objectContaining({ editOf: 1 })));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.touch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ID));
  });
});
```
