- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `dm-chat-view.ts`.**

```ts
import { LitElement, type PropertyValues, css, html, nothing } from "lit";
import type { ChatViewState, EntryView } from "../state/chat";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import type { ThinkingPhase } from "./dm-thinking";
import "./dm-icon";
import "./dm-message-body";
import "./dm-thinking";

const NOTES: Record<string, string> = {
  refusal: "The model declined to answer this.",
  max_tokens: "The answer was cut short because it reached the length limit.",
  max_iterations: "The assistant stopped before it finished searching your documents.",
};
const NEAR_BOTTOM = 48;

function phaseOf(e: Pick<EntryView, "status" | "text" | "steps">): ThinkingPhase {
  if (e.status === "streaming") return e.text !== "" ? "answering" : e.steps.length > 0 ? "working" : "thinking";
  return e.status === "failed" ? "failed" : "done";
}

export class DmChatView extends LitElement {
  static properties = { state: { attribute: false }, selectedPassage: { type: String } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-height: 0; height: 100%; }
      .log { flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow-y: auto; padding: 24px 40px 16px; overscroll-behavior: contain; }
      .turn { max-width: var(--dm-measure); margin: 0 auto 22px; display: grid; gap: 10px; }
      [data-role="user"] { justify-self: end; max-width: 56ch; padding: 11px 16px; border-radius: 14px 14px 4px 14px; background: var(--dm-surface); font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.6; white-space: pre-wrap; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
      [data-role="user"][data-status="rejected"] { color: var(--dm-muted); font-style: italic; }
      .who { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      .logo { width: 20px; height: 20px; border-radius: 6px; display: grid; place-items: center; box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--dm-accent); color: var(--dm-accent); font-size: 12px; }
      [data-actions] { display: flex; gap: 2px; margin-left: -6px; color: var(--dm-muted); font-size: 13px; }
      [data-actions] .btn { font-size: 13px; color: var(--dm-muted); min-height: 28px; }
      [data-note] { color: var(--dm-muted); font-size: 13px; }
      [data-failed] { max-width: 62ch; padding: 14px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); background: var(--dm-err-bg); display: grid; gap: 8px; }
      [data-failed] strong { color: var(--dm-err); display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: center; }
      .older { display: grid; justify-items: center; margin-bottom: 14px; }
      .skeleton { height: 14px; margin: 10px auto; max-width: 60ch; }
      .notice { max-width: var(--dm-measure); margin: 0 auto 14px; font-size: 13px; color: var(--dm-muted); }
    `,
  ];
  declare state: ChatViewState;
  declare selectedPassage: string | undefined;
  private pinned = true;
  private anchor: { height: number; top: number } | undefined;

  private emit(name: string, detail?: unknown) {
    this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(name, { detail, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
  }
  private get log(): HTMLElement | null {
    return this.shadowRoot?.querySelector(".log") ?? null;
  }
  private onScroll = () => {
    const log = this.log;
    if (log) this.pinned = log.scrollHeight - log.scrollTop - log.clientHeight < NEAR_BOTTOM;
  };
  private older = () => {
    const log = this.log;
    if (log) this.anchor = { height: log.scrollHeight, top: log.scrollTop };
    this.pinned = false;
    this.emit("dm-load-older");
  };
  protected updated(_changed: PropertyValues<this>) {
    const log = this.log;
    if (!log || !this.state) return;
    if (this.anchor && !this.state.loadingOlder) {
      log.scrollTop = this.anchor.top + (log.scrollHeight - this.anchor.height);
      this.anchor = undefined;
    } else if (this.pinned) log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight;
  }

  private reply(e: EntryView) {
    const phase = phaseOf(e);
    const focused = this.state.focus?.entryId === e.id;
    const done = e.status !== "streaming";
    return html`<article class="turn" data-role="assistant" data-id=${e.id}>
      <div class="who"><span class="logo"><dm-icon name="book-open-text"></dm-icon></span>DocMind</div>
      ${e.reasoning || e.steps.length > 0 || phase === "thinking" || phase === "working" || phase === "failed"
        ? html`<dm-thinking .phase=${phase} .reasoning=${e.reasoning} .steps=${e.steps}></dm-thinking>`
        : nothing}
      ${e.status === "failed"
        ? html`<div data-failed role="group"><strong><dm-icon name="cloud-x"></dm-icon>The answer could not be written.</strong><span>Something went wrong while the answer was being written.</span><div><button class="btn primary" data-retry @click=${() => this.emit("dm-regenerate", { entryId: e.id })}>Retry</button></div></div>`
        : html`<dm-message-body .text=${e.text} .citations=${e.citations} .streaming=${e.status === "streaming"} .selected=${focused ? this.selectedPassage : undefined}
            @dm-cite-select=${(ev: CustomEvent<{ passageId: string }>) => { ev.stopPropagation(); this.emit("dm-select-citation", { entryId: e.id, passageId: ev.detail.passageId }); }}></dm-message-body>`}
      ${e.status === "interrupted" ? html`<div data-note>This answer was interrupted.</div>` : e.outcome ? html`<div data-note>${NOTES[e.outcome]}</div>` : nothing}
      ${done && e.status !== "failed"
        ? html`<div data-actions>
            <button class="btn ghost" data-copy @click=${() => this.emit("dm-copy", { text: e.text })}><dm-icon name="copy"></dm-icon>Copy</button>
            <button class="btn ghost" data-regenerate @click=${() => this.emit("dm-regenerate", { entryId: e.id })}><dm-icon name="arrow-clockwise"></dm-icon>Regenerate</button>
            ${e.citations.length > 0 ? html`<button class="btn ghost" data-sources @click=${() => this.emit("dm-open-sources", { entryId: e.id })}><dm-icon name="stack"></dm-icon>${e.citations.length} ${e.citations.length === 1 ? "source" : "sources"}</button>` : nothing}
          </div>`
        : nothing}
    </article>`;
  }

  render() {
    const s = this.state;
    if (!s) return html``;
    return html`<div class="log" tabindex="0" aria-label="Conversation" aria-busy=${s.loading} @scroll=${this.onScroll}>
      ${s.loading ? html`<div class="skeleton"></div><div class="skeleton"></div><div class="skeleton"></div>` : nothing}
      ${s.problem === "load" ? html`<div class="notice" role="alert">Could not load this chat. <button class="btn secondary" @click=${() => this.emit("dm-retry-load")}>Try again</button></div>` : nothing}
      ${s.problem === "stream" ? html`<div class="notice" role="status">Live updates stopped. Reload the page to continue.</div>` : nothing}
      ${s.problem === "unavailable" ? html`<div class="notice" role="status">Chat is not available right now.</div>` : nothing}
      ${s.hasOlder ? html`<div class="older"><button class="btn secondary" data-older ?disabled=${s.loadingOlder} @click=${this.older}>Load earlier messages</button></div>` : nothing}
      ${s.entries.map((e) =>
        e.role === "user"
          ? html`<div class="turn"><div data-role="user" data-status=${e.status}>${e.status === "rejected" ? "This message was not accepted." : e.text}</div></div>`
          : this.reply(e),
      )}
      ${s.pending && !s.entries.some((e) => e.status === "streaming")
        ? html`<article class="turn" data-pending><div class="who"><span class="logo"><dm-icon name="book-open-text"></dm-icon></span>DocMind</div>
            <dm-thinking .phase=${s.liveSteps.length > 0 ? "working" : "thinking"} .steps=${s.liveSteps}></dm-thinking></article>`
        : nothing}
    </div>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-chat-view", DmChatView);
```

  `dm-chat-page.ts`:

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { ChatController, sourcesOf } from "../state/chat";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-chat-view";

export class DmChatPage extends LitElement {
  static properties = {};
  static styles = [baseStyles, css`:host { display: flex; flex-direction: column; height: 100%; min-height: 0; }`];
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.router]);
  private ctl: ChatController | undefined;
  private chatId: string | undefined;
  private off: (() => void) | undefined;
  private left = false;

  private routeChatId(): string | undefined {
    const route = this.ctx.services?.router.get();
    return route?.name === "chat" ? route.chatId : undefined;
  }
  private attach(id: string | undefined) {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    this.off?.();
    this.ctl?.close();
    this.ctl = undefined;
    this.chatId = id;
    this.left = false;
    if (!svc || !id) return;
    const ctl = new ChatController({ api: svc.api, chatId: id, open: svc.open, onActivity: () => void svc.sessions.touch(id) });
    this.ctl = ctl;
    this.off = ctl.state.subscribe((s) => {
      this.requestUpdate();
      if (s.problem === "gone" && !this.left) {
        this.left = true;
        svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "That chat no longer exists." });
        svc.router.navigate({ name: "chat" }, { replace: true });
      }
    });
    void ctl.start();
  }
  protected willUpdate() {
    const id = this.routeChatId();
    if (this.ctx.services && id !== this.chatId) this.attach(id);
  }
  disconnectedCallback() {
    this.off?.();
    this.ctl?.close();
    this.ctl = undefined;
    this.chatId = undefined;
    super.disconnectedCallback();
  }
  private async regenerate(entryId: string) {
    const outcome = await this.ctl?.regenerate(entryId);
    if (outcome === "busy") this.ctx.services?.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Too many messages are waiting. Try again in a moment." });
    else if (outcome === "failed" || outcome === "unavailable") this.ctx.services?.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not regenerate the answer." });
  }
  private async copy(text: string) {
    const toasts = this.ctx.services?.toasts;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      toasts?.push({ kind: "ok", text: "Copied." });
    } catch {
      toasts?.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not copy." });
    }
  }
  render() {
    const state = this.ctl?.state.get();
    if (!state || !this.ctl) return html``;
    const ctl = this.ctl;
    return html`<dm-chat-view .state=${state} .selectedPassage=${sourcesOf(state).passageId}
      @dm-load-older=${() => void ctl.loadOlder()}
      @dm-retry-load=${() => this.attach(this.chatId)}
      @dm-regenerate=${(e: CustomEvent<{ entryId: string }>) => void this.regenerate(e.detail.entryId)}
      @dm-copy=${(e: CustomEvent<{ text: string }>) => void this.copy(e.detail.text)}
      @dm-select-citation=${(e: CustomEvent<{ entryId: string; passageId: string }>) => ctl.select(e.detail.entryId, e.detail.passageId)}
      @dm-open-sources=${(e: CustomEvent<{ entryId: string }>) => ctl.select(e.detail.entryId)}></dm-chat-view>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-chat-page", DmChatPage);
```

  Add both imports to `components/index.ts`; do **not** add the `chat` entry to `VIEWS` (Task 192).

- [ ] **Step 4: Run them.** `pnpm --filter @docmind/web exec vitest run`. Expected: PASS (view 18, page 4).
  **Probe.** (a) happy-dom may implement `scrollHeight`/`clientHeight` as 0 with a settable `scrollTop`; the tests define them with `Object.defineProperty`. If `scrollTop` is not settable on happy-dom elements, define it the same way (`get`/`set` on a closure variable) in the test helper. (b) `willUpdate` in `DmChatPage` runs before the services arrive (`this.ctx.services` undefined on the first pass); the `ServicesController` calls `requestUpdate` when the context resolves, which runs `willUpdate` again with the services present.

**Spec:** §9 item 40 ("Citation markers and sources panel"), §6.3 Rendering (source cards: document, page or heading, snippet), `ui-mocks.md` §2 Sources panel (docked 340 px on desktop, an overlay with a scrim below 1200 px) and §4 Source card. Decision D8; open question 7.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-source-panel.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.ts` (header, panel, layout), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-source-panel.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.test.ts` (new cases)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `renderAnswer(...).cited` (188: the citations in the order of their markers, which is also their number), `sourcesOf`, `ChatController.select/toggleSources`, `Citation`.
- Produces: `<dm-source-panel .entry=${EntryView | undefined} .selected=${string | undefined} .loading=${boolean}>`: an `<aside aria-label="Sources">` with the title, a sub-line ("searching...", "N cited" or "No sources"), a close button (fires `dm-close-sources`), then one card per citation in marker order (number badge, the document's title, a location line, the snippet in quotes, an "Open in document" link to `/documents/<id>?passage=<passage id>`). A card is `data-selected` and `aria-current="true"` when selected, scrolls into view when it becomes selected, and fires `dm-select-citation {passageId}` when clicked (the link does not). `loading` shows three skeleton cards and no real ones.
  `locationOf(locator: Citation["locator"]): string`: `page` and `region` as "Page N"; `section` as "Section N" (index + 1); `chars` as "Characters a-b"; `paragraphs` as "Paragraphs a-b" (from + 1, to + 1); anything else "Passage".

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

`dm-source-panel.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { EntryView } from "../state/chat";
import { locationOf } from "./dm-source-panel";
import "./dm-source-panel";

const A = "c0ffee00-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const B = "c0ffee00-0000-4000-8000-000000000002";
const DOC = "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11";
const cite = (id: string, over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({ passageId: id, documentId: DOC, docTitle: "Q3 <b>report</b>", locator: { kind: "page" as const, page: 12 }, snippet: "Payment is due in 30 days.", ...over });
const entry = (text: string, citations = [cite(A), cite(B, { locator: { kind: "section", index: 2 } })]): EntryView => ({ id: "t1.assistant", turn: 1, role: "assistant", status: "complete", text, citations, steps: [], outcome: null });

type Panel = HTMLElement & { updateComplete: Promise<unknown>; entry?: EntryView; selected?: string; loading?: boolean };
async function mount(props: Partial<Panel>) {
  const el = document.createElement("dm-source-panel") as Panel;
  Object.assign(el, props);
  document.body.append(el);
  await el.updateComplete;
  return el;
}
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as T;
const qa = (el: Element, sel: string) => [...(el.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll(sel) ?? [])];

describe("locationOf", () => {
  it.each([
    [{ kind: "page", page: 12 }, "Page 12"], [{ kind: "region", page: 3, x: 0, y: 0, w: 1, h: 1 }, "Page 3"], [{ kind: "section", index: 2 }, "Section 3"],
    [{ kind: "chars", start: 10, end: 90 }, "Characters 10-90"], [{ kind: "paragraphs", from: 0, to: 2 }, "Paragraphs 1-3"], [{ kind: "other" }, "Passage"],
  ])("%o is %s", (locator, text) => expect(locationOf(locator as never)).toBe(text));
});

describe("dm-source-panel", () => {
  it("lists the cited passages in the order of their markers, numbered, as text, with a link into the document", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entry: entry(`See [[${B}]] and then [[${A}]].`) });
    expect(q(el, "aside").getAttribute("aria-label")).toBe("Sources");
    expect(q(el, "[data-sub]").textContent).toBe("2 cited");
    const cards = qa(el, "[data-card]");
    expect(cards.map((c) => c.getAttribute("data-passage"))).toEqual([B, A]);
    expect(cards.map((c) => c.querySelector("[data-number]")?.textContent)).toEqual(["1", "2"]);
    expect(cards[1]?.querySelector("[data-title]")?.textContent).toBe("Q3 <b>report</b>");
    expect(cards[1]?.querySelector("b")).toBeNull();
    expect(cards[0]?.querySelector("[data-location]")?.textContent).toBe("Section 3");
    expect(cards[1]?.querySelector("[data-quote]")?.textContent).toBe("“Payment is due in 30 days.”");
    expect(cards[1]?.querySelector("a")?.getAttribute("href")).toBe(`/documents/${DOC}?passage=${A}`);
    expect(cards[1]?.querySelector("a")?.textContent).toContain("Open in document");
  });

  it("marks the selected card, scrolls it into view when the selection changes, and reports a click on a card but not on its link", async () => {
    const scroll = vi.fn();
    Element.prototype.scrollIntoView = scroll;
    const el = await mount({ entry: entry(`[[${A}]] [[${B}]]`), selected: A });
    const [first, second] = qa(el, "[data-card]") as HTMLElement[];
    expect(first?.hasAttribute("data-selected")).toBe(true);
    expect(first?.getAttribute("aria-current")).toBe("true");
    expect(second?.hasAttribute("data-selected")).toBe(false);
    const picked = vi.fn((e: Event) => (e as CustomEvent).detail);
    el.addEventListener("dm-select-citation", picked);
    second?.click();
    expect(picked).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ passageId: B });
    second?.querySelector("a")?.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("click", { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
    expect(picked).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    scroll.mockClear();
    el.selected = B;
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(scroll).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("shows skeleton cards and 'searching...' while loading, an empty state when nothing is cited, and closes", async () => {
    const loading = await mount({ entry: entry(`[[${A}]]`), loading: true });
    expect(q(loading, "[data-sub]").textContent).toBe("searching...");
    expect(qa(loading, "[data-card]").length).toBe(0);
    expect(qa(loading, ".skeleton").length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
    const none = await mount({ entry: entry("No idea.", []) });
    expect(q(none, "[data-sub]").textContent).toBe("No sources");
    expect(none.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("No supporting passages");
    const closed = vi.fn();
    none.addEventListener("dm-close-sources", closed);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(none, "button[aria-label='Close sources']").click();
    expect(closed).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const absent = await mount({});
    expect(q(absent, "[data-sub]").textContent).toBe("No sources");
  });
});
```

New cases for `dm-chat-page.test.ts` (reuse its `setup`, giving the second history message a citation and the assistant text a marker; `matchMedia` is stubbed per test with `vi.stubGlobal`):

```ts
const PASSAGE = "c0ffee00-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const DOC = "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11";
const citedHistory = () => vi.fn(async () => ({
  items: [msg(1, "user", 1, "q"), { ...msg(2, "assistant", 1, `Due in 30 days [[${PASSAGE}]].`), citations: [{ passageId: PASSAGE, documentId: DOC, docTitle: "Q3", locator: { kind: "page", page: 2 }, snippet: "30 days" }] }],
  nextCursor: null,
}));
const media = (matches: boolean) => vi.stubGlobal("matchMedia", (q: string) => ({ matches, media: q, addEventListener: () => {}, removeEventListener: () => {} }));
const q2 = (el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as HTMLElement | null;

describe("dm-chat-page: sources", () => {
  it("docks the panel beside the transcript on a wide screen, with the count on the toggle", async () => {
    media(true);
    const t = setup(citedHistory());
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q2(el, "dm-source-panel")).not.toBeNull());
    expect(q2(el, "[data-layout]")?.getAttribute("data-layout")).toBe("docked");
    const toggle = q2(el, "button[data-sources-toggle]") as HTMLButtonElement;
    expect(toggle.getAttribute("aria-pressed")).toBe("true");
    expect(toggle.textContent).toContain("1");
    expect(q2(el, "[data-scrim]")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("overlays the panel with a scrim on a narrow screen, and the scrim closes it", async () => {
    media(false);
    const t = setup(citedHistory());
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q2(el, "dm-source-panel")).not.toBeNull());
    expect(q2(el, "[data-layout]")?.getAttribute("data-layout")).toBe("overlay");
    (q2(el, "[data-scrim]") as HTMLElement).click();
    await settle(el);
    expect(q2(el, "dm-source-panel")).toBeNull();
    expect((q2(el, "button[data-sources-toggle]") as HTMLButtonElement).getAttribute("aria-pressed")).toBe("false");
  });

  it("a marker in the answer selects its card, and a card selects its marker", async () => {
    media(true);
    const t = setup(citedHistory());
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q2(el, "dm-source-panel")).not.toBeNull());
    const viewEl = q2(el, "dm-chat-view") as HTMLElement;
    const body = viewEl.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-message-body") as HTMLElement;
    (body.shadowRoot?.querySelector("button.dm-cite") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await settle(el);
    const panel = q2(el, "dm-source-panel") as HTMLElement & { selected?: string };
    expect(panel.selected).toBe(PASSAGE);
    panel.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-close-sources", { bubbles: true, composed: true }));
    await settle(el);
    expect(q2(el, "dm-source-panel")).toBeNull();
    (q2(el, "button[data-sources-toggle]") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await settle(el);
    expect(q2(el, "dm-source-panel")).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("draws no panel and no toggle count for a chat nobody has cited anything in", async () => {
    media(true);
    const t = setup();
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(view(el).state.entries.length).toBe(2));
    expect(q2(el, "dm-source-panel")).toBeNull();
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `dm-source-panel.ts`.**

```ts
import type { Citation } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, type PropertyValues, css, html, nothing } from "lit";
import { renderAnswer } from "../chat/markdown";
import type { EntryView } from "../state/chat";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

export function locationOf(l: Citation["locator"]): string {
  switch (l.kind) {
    case "page":
    case "region":
      return `Page ${(l as { page: number }).page}`;
    case "section":
      return `Section ${(l as { index: number }).index + 1}`;
    case "chars":
      return `Characters ${(l as { start: number }).start}-${(l as { end: number }).end}`;
    case "paragraphs":
      return `Paragraphs ${(l as { from: number }).from + 1}-${(l as { to: number }).to + 1}`;
    default:
      return "Passage";
  }
}

export class DmSourcePanel extends LitElement {
  static properties = { entry: { attribute: false }, selected: { type: String }, loading: { type: Boolean } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; height: 100%; }
      aside { height: 100%; display: flex; flex-direction: column; background: var(--dm-bg); border-left: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); }
      header { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; min-height: 56px; padding: 0 14px; }
      h2 { font-size: 15px; }
      [data-sub] { font-size: 12px; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      .cards { flex: 1; overflow: auto; padding: 2px 14px 14px; display: grid; gap: 10px; align-content: start; }
      [data-card] { display: grid; gap: 6px; padding: 12px 14px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-sm); cursor: pointer; transition: background 0.15s; }
      [data-card][data-selected] { background: var(--dm-accent-900); box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--dm-accent); }
      .row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; min-width: 0; }
      [data-number] { min-width: 18px; height: 18px; padding: 0 4px; border-radius: 5px; font-size: 11px; display: inline-grid; place-items: center; background: var(--dm-accent-800); color: var(--dm-accent-100); flex: none; }
      [data-card][data-selected] [data-number] { background: var(--dm-accent); color: var(--dm-bg); }
      [data-title] { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 500; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
      [data-location] { font-size: 12px; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      [data-quote] { font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.6; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
      a { font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--dm-accent-300); display: inline-flex; gap: 4px; align-items: center; }
      .skeleton { height: 78px; }
      .empty { padding: 20px 4px; color: var(--dm-muted); font-size: 13px; }
    `,
  ];
  declare entry: EntryView | undefined;
  declare selected: string | undefined;
  declare loading: boolean;
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.loading = false;
  }
  private cards(): Citation[] {
    return this.entry ? renderAnswer(this.entry.text, this.entry.citations).cited : [];
  }
  private pick(passageId: string) {
    this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-select-citation", { detail: { passageId }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
  }
  protected updated(changed: PropertyValues<this>) {
    if (!changed.has("selected") || !this.selected) return;
    const card = [...(this.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll("[data-card]") ?? [])].find((c) => c.getAttribute("data-passage") === this.selected?.toLowerCase());
    card?.scrollIntoView?.({ block: "nearest" });
  }
  render() {
    const cards = this.loading ? [] : this.cards();
    const sub = this.loading ? "searching..." : cards.length > 0 ? `${cards.length} cited` : "No sources";
    return html`<aside aria-label="Sources">
      <header><div><h2>Sources</h2><div data-sub>${sub}</div></div>
        <button class="btn icon" aria-label="Close sources" @click=${() => this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-close-sources", { bubbles: true, composed: true }))}><dm-icon name="x"></dm-icon></button></header>
      <div class="cards">
        ${this.loading ? html`<div class="skeleton"></div><div class="skeleton"></div><div class="skeleton"></div>` : nothing}
        ${!this.loading && cards.length === 0 ? html`<p class="empty">No supporting passages</p>` : nothing}
        ${cards.map((c, i) => {
          const on = this.selected?.toLowerCase() === c.passageId.toLowerCase();
          return html`<article data-card data-passage=${c.passageId.toLowerCase()} ?data-selected=${on} aria-current=${on ? "true" : "false"} @click=${() => this.pick(c.passageId)}>
            <div class="row"><span data-number>${i + 1}</span><dm-icon name="file-text"></dm-icon><span data-title>${c.docTitle}</span></div>
            <div data-location>${locationOf(c.locator)}</div>
            <div data-quote>“${c.snippet}”</div>
            <a href="/documents/${c.documentId}?passage=${c.passageId}" @click=${(e: Event) => e.stopPropagation()}>Open in document<dm-icon name="arrow-up-right"></dm-icon></a>
          </article>`;
        })}
      </div>
    </aside>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-source-panel", DmSourcePanel);
```

  `dm-chat-page.ts`: change the services line to `private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.router, s.sessions]);` (the header shows the chat's title, which the sessions store learns later), then replace `render()` and add a width query. New members and render:

```ts
  static properties = { docked: { state: true } };
  declare docked: boolean;
  private mq: MediaQueryList | undefined;
  private readonly onMq = () => { this.docked = this.mq?.matches ?? true; };
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.docked = true;
  }
  connectedCallback() {
    super.connectedCallback();
    this.mq = window.matchMedia("(min-width: 1200px)");
    this.docked = this.mq.matches;
    this.mq.addEventListener("change", this.onMq);
  }
  disconnectedCallback() {
    this.mq?.removeEventListener("change", this.onMq);
    this.off?.();
    this.ctl?.close();
    this.ctl = undefined;
    this.chatId = undefined;
    super.disconnectedCallback();
  }
  render() {
    const state = this.ctl?.state.get();
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!state || !this.ctl || !svc) return html``;
    const ctl = this.ctl;
    const { entry, passageId } = sourcesOf(state);
    const title = svc.sessions.get().items.find((c) => c.id === this.chatId)?.title ?? "New chat";
    const count = entry?.citations.length ?? 0;
    const showPanel = state.sourcesOpen && (count > 0 || state.running);
    const loading = state.running && !state.focus;
    return html`
      <header class="bar"><h1>${title}</h1>
        <button class="btn secondary" data-sources-toggle aria-pressed=${showPanel} @click=${() => ctl.toggleSources()}><dm-icon name="stack"></dm-icon>Sources${count > 0 ? html` <span class="tag accent">${count}</span>` : nothing}</button></header>
      <div class="body" data-layout=${this.docked ? "docked" : "overlay"}>
        <div class="main"><dm-chat-view .state=${state} .selectedPassage=${passageId}
          @dm-load-older=${() => void ctl.loadOlder()}
          @dm-retry-load=${() => this.attach(this.chatId)}
          @dm-regenerate=${(e: CustomEvent<{ entryId: string }>) => void this.regenerate(e.detail.entryId)}
          @dm-copy=${(e: CustomEvent<{ text: string }>) => void this.copy(e.detail.text)}
          @dm-select-citation=${(e: CustomEvent<{ entryId: string; passageId: string }>) => ctl.select(e.detail.entryId, e.detail.passageId)}
          @dm-open-sources=${(e: CustomEvent<{ entryId: string }>) => ctl.select(e.detail.entryId)}></dm-chat-view></div>
        ${showPanel
          ? html`${this.docked ? nothing : html`<div class="scrim" data-scrim @click=${() => ctl.toggleSources(false)}></div>`}
            <dm-source-panel class=${this.docked ? "docked" : "overlay"} .entry=${loading ? undefined : entry} .selected=${passageId} .loading=${loading}
              @dm-select-citation=${(e: CustomEvent<{ passageId: string }>) => entry && ctl.select(entry.id, e.detail.passageId)}
              @dm-close-sources=${() => ctl.toggleSources(false)}></dm-source-panel>`
          : nothing}
      </div>`;
  }
```

  with styles added: `.bar { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; min-height:56px; padding:0 20px 0 40px; border-bottom:1px solid var(--dm-divider) } h1 { font-size:15px; font-weight:500; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap } .body { position:relative; flex:1; min-height:0; display:flex } .main { flex:1; min-width:0 } dm-source-panel.docked { flex:none; width:var(--dm-panel-w) } dm-source-panel.overlay { position:absolute; right:0; top:0; bottom:0; width:var(--dm-panel-w-overlay); z-index:2; background:var(--dm-surface); box-shadow:var(--dm-shadow-lg) } .scrim { position:absolute; inset:0; z-index:1; background:var(--dm-scrim) }`, and `nothing` added to the lit import, `import "./dm-source-panel"`. The earlier dm-chat-page cases (Task 190b) still pass: they never look inside the page header, and `dm-chat-view` is still found by the same selector.

### Task 192: `dm-composer`, stop, and the new-chat flow; the chat route goes live

**Spec:** §9 item 41 ("Composer, scope picker, stop and regenerate"; the scope picker is Task 194), §6.1 (send is 202; "A message sent while a turn runs interrupts it"; `POST /api/chats` is the only place a conversation starts; 503 `chat_unavailable`), `ui-mocks.md` §4 Composer and Empty states, §5 (Stop). Decisions D5, D7.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-composer.ts`, `apps/web/src/state/outbox.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/services.ts` (add `outbox`), `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.ts` (composer, empty state, new-chat flow; the full file is below), `apps/web/src/views.ts` (`chat: "dm-chat-page"`), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-composer.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.test.ts` (new cases)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ChatController.send/stop` and `SendOutcome` (190a), `SessionsStore.create` (185a), `Router`, `ToastStore`.
- Produces: `<dm-composer ?disabled ?running ?attachable placeholder maxChars=8000>`: a labelled textarea ("Message"); Enter sends, Shift+Enter breaks the line, Enter during IME composition does neither; Escape asks to stop while `running`; the send button becomes a Stop button while `running` (Enter still sends, which interrupts). It fires `dm-send {text}` (trimmed, then it clears itself), `dm-stop`, and, when `attachable`, `dm-attach {files: File[]}`. Public `fill(text)` sets the text and focuses (used to give a message back after a failed send and for the suggested prompts). A character count appears from 90% of `maxChars`; over the limit it turns into an error and sending is disabled. A `toolbar` slot holds the scope picker (Task 194).
- `<dm-chat-page>` is complete and the `chat` route is registered. Without a chat id it shows the empty chat (heading, three suggested prompts that fill the composer, the composer); the first send creates the chat (`sessions.create(scope)`, scope `"all"` until Task 194), puts the message in `services.outbox` (`Store<Outbox>`, `{ chatId, text }`, in `state/outbox.ts`: `export type Outbox = { chatId: string; text: string } | undefined; export const createOutbox = () => new Store<Outbox>(undefined)`), replaces the URL with `/chats/<id>`, and the page sends the outbox message once that chat's controller is running (any view can use the outbox to start a chat with a question: Task 198 does). A failed send gives the text back to the composer and says why in a toast: "Too many messages are waiting. Try again in a moment." (429), "Chat is not available right now." (503), "Could not send your message." (anything else); a chat that cannot be created says "Chat is not available right now." or "Could not start the chat.".

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

`dm-composer.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import "./dm-composer";

type Comp = HTMLElement & { updateComplete: Promise<unknown>; fill(t: string): void; running: boolean; disabled: boolean; attachable: boolean };
async function mount(props: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const el = document.createElement("dm-composer") as Comp;
  Object.assign(el, props);
  document.body.append(el);
  await el.updateComplete;
  return el;
}
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as T;
const input = (el: Comp) => q<HTMLTextAreaElement>(el, "textarea");
async function type(el: Comp, text: string) {
  input(el).value = text;
  input(el).dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
  await el.updateComplete;
}
const key = (el: Comp, init: KeyboardEventInit) => { const e = new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { bubbles: true, cancelable: true, ...init }); input(el).dispatchEvent(e); return e; };
const heard = (el: Element, name: string) => { const fn = vi.fn(); el.addEventListener(name, (e) => fn((e as CustomEvent).detail)); return fn; };

describe("dm-composer: sending", () => {
  it("has a named textarea and a named send button that stays disabled until there is text", async () => {
    const el = await mount();
    expect(input(el).getAttribute("aria-label")).toBe("Message");
    const send = q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[aria-label='Send message']");
    expect(send.disabled).toBe(true);
    await type(el, "  ");
    expect(send.disabled).toBe(true);
    await type(el, "Hello");
    expect(q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[aria-label='Send message']").disabled).toBe(false);
  });

  it("Enter sends the trimmed text and clears the box; Shift+Enter does not send; an IME confirmation does not send", async () => {
    const el = await mount();
    const sent = heard(el, "dm-send");
    await type(el, "  What is due?  ");
    expect(key(el, { key: "Enter", shiftKey: true }).defaultPrevented).toBe(false);
    key(el, { key: "Enter", isComposing: true });
    key(el, { key: "Enter", keyCode: 229 });
    expect(sent).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(key(el, { key: "Enter" }).defaultPrevented).toBe(true);
    expect(sent).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ text: "What is due?" });
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(input(el).value).toBe("");
    key(el, { key: "Enter" }); // nothing to send now
    expect(sent).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("the send button sends too", async () => {
    const el = await mount();
    const sent = heard(el, "dm-send");
    await type(el, "Hi");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[aria-label='Send message']").click();
    expect(sent).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ text: "Hi" });
  });

  it("fill() puts text in the box and moves focus to it", async () => {
    const el = await mount();
    el.fill("Summarize my latest upload");
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(input(el).value).toBe("Summarize my latest upload");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(el.shadowRoot?.activeElement).toBe(input(el)));
  });
});

describe("dm-composer: limits, stop and disabled", () => {
  it("counts from 90% of the limit, and over the limit says so, keeps the text and refuses to send", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ maxChars: 100 });
    const sent = heard(el, "dm-send");
    await type(el, "x".repeat(50));
    expect(q(el, "[data-count]")).toBeNull();
    await type(el, "x".repeat(95));
    expect(q(el, "[data-count]").textContent).toBe("95 / 100");
    await type(el, "x".repeat(101));
    expect(q(el, "[data-count]").textContent).toBe("101 / 100");
    expect(q(el, "[role=alert]").textContent).toContain("Shorten your message");
    expect(q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[aria-label='Send message']").disabled).toBe(true);
    key(el, { key: "Enter" });
    expect(sent).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(input(el).value.length).toBe(101);
  });

  it("while running the send button becomes Stop, Escape stops, and Enter still sends (it interrupts)", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ running: true });
    const stop = heard(el, "dm-stop"), sent = heard(el, "dm-send");
    expect(q(el, "button[aria-label='Send message']")).toBeNull();
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[aria-label='Stop generating']").click();
    expect(stop).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    key(el, { key: "Escape" });
    expect(stop).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    await type(el, "interrupt");
    key(el, { key: "Enter" });
    expect(sent).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ text: "interrupt" });
    el.running = false;
    await el.updateComplete;
    key(el, { key: "Escape" });
    expect(stop).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2); // idle: Escape is nothing
  });

  it("disabled turns everything off and says why in the placeholder", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ disabled: true });
    expect(input(el).disabled).toBe(true);
    expect(q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[aria-label='Send message']").disabled).toBe(true);
  });

  it("offers attach only when asked to, and reports the chosen files", async () => {
    const plain = await mount();
    expect(q(plain, "button[aria-label='Attach documents']")).toBeNull();
    const el = await mount({ attachable: true });
    const attached = heard(el, "dm-attach");
    const file = new File(["a"], "a.txt");
    const files = q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[type=file]");
    Object.defineProperty(files, "files", { value: [file], configurable: true });
    files.dispatchEvent(new Event("change"));
    expect(attached).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ files: [file] });
    expect(q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[aria-label='Attach documents']").getAttribute("title")).toContain("uploads to your library");
  });

  it("makes room as the message grows, up to eight lines", async () => {
    const el = await mount();
    expect(input(el).rows).toBe(2);
    await type(el, "a\nb\nc\nd");
    expect(input(el).rows).toBe(4);
    await type(el, "\n".repeat(20));
    expect(input(el).rows).toBe(8);
  });
});
```

New cases appended to `dm-chat-page.test.ts` (the `setup` helper gains `create` and a `chat` factory; `media(true)` from the Task 191 cases is reused):

```ts
const comp = (el: Element) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-composer") as HTMLElement & { fill(t: string): void; updateComplete: Promise<unknown> };
const sendFrom = (el: Element, text: string) => comp(el).dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-send", { detail: { text }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
const dto = (id: string) => ({ id, title: null, scope: "all" as const, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", lastMessageAt: null, messageCount: 0 });

describe("dm-chat-page: writing", () => {
  it("sends the composer's text to the open chat, and gives it back with a reason when the send fails", async () => {
    media(true);
    const t = setup();
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(view(el).state.entries.length).toBe(2));
    sendFrom(el, "And the interest?");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.services.api.sendMessage).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ID, expect.objectContaining({ text: "And the interest?" })));
    (t.services.api.sendMessage as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(429, "too_many_requests"));
    const fill = vi.spyOn(comp(el), "fill");
    sendFrom(el, "again");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(fill).toHaveBeenCalledWith("again"));
    expect(t.services.toasts.get().at(-1)?.text).toBe("Too many messages are waiting. Try again in a moment.");
  });

  it("stops the running answer from the composer", async () => {
    media(true);
    const t = setup(vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [msg(1, "user", 1, "q")], nextCursor: null })));
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect((comp(el) as unknown as { running: boolean }).running).toBe(true)); // the last question has no reply yet
    comp(el).dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-stop", { bubbles: true, composed: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.services.api.stopChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ID));
  });

  it("disables the composer and says so when chat is not available", async () => {
    media(true);
    const t = setup();
    (t.services.api.sendMessage as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(503, "chat_unavailable"));
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(view(el).state.entries.length).toBe(2));
    sendFrom(el, "hello");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect((comp(el) as unknown as { disabled: boolean }).disabled).toBe(true));
  });
});

describe("dm-chat-page: a new chat", () => {
  function fresh() {
    const t = setup();
    (t.services.router as unknown as { navigate: (r: unknown, o?: unknown) => void }).navigate({ name: "chat" }, { replace: true });
    const created = dto(ID);
    (t.services.sessions as unknown as { create: unknown }).create = vi.fn(async () => created);
    return { ...t, created };
  }
  it("shows the empty chat with three prompts that fill the composer", async () => {
    media(true);
    const t = fresh();
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(el.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll("button[data-prompt]").length).toBe(3));
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Ask your documents anything");
    const fill = vi.spyOn(comp(el), "fill");
    (el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("button[data-prompt]") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    expect(fill).toHaveBeenCalledWith("Summarize my latest upload");
  });

  it("the first message creates the chat, moves to it and sends once it is running, in that order", async () => {
    media(true);
    const t = fresh();
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(comp(el)).not.toBeNull());
    sendFrom(el, "Hello there");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "chat", chatId: ID }));
    expect((t.services.sessions as unknown as { create: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).create).toHaveBeenCalledWith("all");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.services.api.sendMessage).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ID, expect.objectContaining({ text: "Hello there" })));
    const order = [(t.listMessages as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.invocationCallOrder[0] as number, (t.services.api.sendMessage as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.invocationCallOrder[0] as number];
    expect(order[0]).toBeLessThan(order[1] as number); // history was loaded before the message was sent
  });

  it("gives the text back when the chat cannot be created", async () => {
    media(true);
    const t = fresh();
    (t.services.sessions as unknown as { create: unknown }).create = vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(503, "chat_unavailable"); });
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(comp(el)).not.toBeNull());
    const fill = vi.spyOn(comp(el), "fill");
    sendFrom(el, "Hello there");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(fill).toHaveBeenCalledWith("Hello there"));
    expect(t.services.toasts.get().at(-1)?.text).toBe("Chat is not available right now.");
    expect(t.services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "chat" });
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `dm-composer.ts`.**

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html, nothing } from "lit";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

export class DmComposer extends LitElement {
  static properties = {
    text: { state: true },
    disabled: { type: Boolean },
    running: { type: Boolean },
    attachable: { type: Boolean },
    placeholder: { type: String },
    maxChars: { type: Number },
  };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; }
      .box { display: grid; gap: 8px; padding: 12px 10px 10px 16px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-lg); background: var(--dm-surface); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-sm); }
      textarea { width: 100%; resize: none; border: 0; outline: 0; background: transparent; color: var(--dm-text); font: inherit; font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.55; caret-color: var(--dm-accent); }
      .box:focus-within { box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--dm-accent); }
      .bar { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; }
      .spacer { flex: 1; }
      .hint { margin-top: 6px; font-size: 11.5px; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      [data-count] { font-size: 12px; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      [data-count][data-over] { color: var(--dm-err); }
      .btn.send { width: 32px; min-height: 32px; padding: 0; }
      input[type="file"] { display: none; }
    `,
  ];
  declare text: string;
  declare disabled: boolean;
  declare running: boolean;
  declare attachable: boolean;
  declare placeholder: string;
  declare maxChars: number;
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.text = "";
    this.disabled = false;
    this.running = false;
    this.attachable = false;
    this.placeholder = "Ask about your documents...";
    this.maxChars = 8000;
  }
  private get input(): HTMLTextAreaElement | null {
    return this.shadowRoot?.querySelector("textarea") ?? null;
  }
  private fire(name: string, detail?: unknown) {
    this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(name, { detail, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
  }
  /** Puts `text` in the box and focuses it (a message given back after a failed send, or a suggested prompt). */
  fill(text: string): void {
    this.text = text;
    void this.updateComplete.then(() => this.input?.focus());
  }
  private get over(): boolean {
    return this.text.length > this.maxChars;
  }
  private submit() {
    const body = this.text.trim();
    if (body === "" || this.disabled || this.over) return;
    this.fire("dm-send", { text: body });
    this.text = "";
  }
  private onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey && !e.isComposing && e.keyCode !== 229) {
      e.preventDefault();
      this.submit();
    } else if (e.key === "Escape" && this.running) {
      e.preventDefault();
      this.fire("dm-stop");
    }
  }
  private onFiles(e: Event) {
    const input = e.target as HTMLInputElement;
    const files = [...(input.files ?? [])];
    if (files.length > 0) this.fire("dm-attach", { files });
    try {
      input.value = "";
    } catch {
      // some environments refuse to clear a file input
    }
  }
  render() {
    const rows = Math.min(8, Math.max(2, this.text.split("\n").length));
    const cannot = this.disabled || this.text.trim() === "" || this.over;
    return html`<div class="box">
        <textarea aria-label="Message" rows=${rows} placeholder=${this.placeholder} ?disabled=${this.disabled} .value=${this.text}
          @input=${(e: Event) => (this.text = (e.target as HTMLTextAreaElement).value)} @keydown=${this.onKey}></textarea>
        <div class="bar">
          ${this.attachable
            ? html`<button class="btn icon" type="button" aria-label="Attach documents" title="Attach - uploads to your library and adds it to this chat" ?disabled=${this.disabled}
                  @click=${() => this.shadowRoot?.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>("input[type=file]")?.click()}><dm-icon name="paperclip"></dm-icon></button>
                <input type="file" multiple @change=${this.onFiles} />`
            : nothing}
          <slot name="toolbar"></slot>
          <span class="spacer"></span>
          ${this.text.length >= this.maxChars * 0.9 ? html`<span data-count ?data-over=${this.over}>${this.text.length} / ${this.maxChars}</span>` : nothing}
          ${this.running
            ? html`<button class="btn secondary send" type="button" aria-label="Stop generating" @click=${() => this.fire("dm-stop")}><dm-icon name="stop"></dm-icon></button>`
            : html`<button class="btn primary send" type="button" aria-label="Send message" ?disabled=${cannot} @click=${() => this.submit()}><dm-icon name="arrow-up"></dm-icon></button>`}
        </div>
      </div>
      ${this.over ? html`<div class="error" role="alert"><dm-icon name="warning-circle"></dm-icon>Shorten your message to ${this.maxChars} characters or fewer.</div>` : nothing}
      <div class="hint">Answers cite your documents - Shift + Enter for a new line</div>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-composer", DmComposer);
```

  The unavailable placeholder: the page passes `placeholder="Chat is not available right now."` and `disabled` together.

- [ ] **Step 4: Replace `dm-chat-page.ts` with the final file.**

```ts
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { LitElement, css, html, nothing } from "lit";
import { ChatController, type SendOutcome, sourcesOf } from "../state/chat";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import type { DmComposer } from "./dm-composer";
import "./dm-chat-view";
import "./dm-composer";
import "./dm-icon";
import "./dm-source-panel";

const PROMPTS = ["Summarize my latest upload", "What are the key points across my documents?", "Where do my documents mention deadlines?"];
const SEND_TOAST: Record<Exclude<SendOutcome, "sent">, string> = {
  busy: "Too many messages are waiting. Try again in a moment.",
  unavailable: "Chat is not available right now.",
  failed: "Could not send your message.",
};

export class DmChatPage extends LitElement {
  static properties = { docked: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: flex; flex-direction: column; height: 100%; min-height: 0; }
      .bar { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; min-height: 56px; padding: 0 20px 0 40px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); }
      h1 { font-size: 15px; font-weight: 500; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
      .body { position: relative; flex: 1; min-height: 0; display: flex; }
      .main { flex: 1; min-width: 0; min-height: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
      .view { flex: 1; min-height: 0; }
      .write { width: 100%; max-width: calc(var(--dm-measure) + 80px); margin: 0 auto; padding: 0 40px 18px; }
      .empty { flex: 1; display: grid; align-content: center; justify-items: center; gap: 14px; padding: 24px 40px; text-align: center; }
      .empty h2 { font-size: 28px; }
      .prompts { display: grid; gap: 8px; width: min(520px, 100%); }
      .prompts .btn { justify-content: flex-start; padding: 10px 14px; min-height: 44px; }
      dm-source-panel.docked { flex: none; width: var(--dm-panel-w); }
      dm-source-panel.overlay { position: absolute; right: 0; top: 0; bottom: 0; width: var(--dm-panel-w-overlay); z-index: 2; background: var(--dm-surface); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-lg); }
      .scrim { position: absolute; inset: 0; z-index: 1; background: var(--dm-scrim); }
    `,
  ];
  declare docked: boolean;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.router, s.sessions]);
  private ctl: ChatController | undefined;
  private chatId: string | undefined;
  private off: (() => void) | undefined;
  private left = false;
  private mq: MediaQueryList | undefined;
  private readonly onMq = () => {
    this.docked = this.mq?.matches ?? true;
  };

  constructor() {
    super();
    this.docked = true;
  }
  connectedCallback() {
    super.connectedCallback();
    this.mq = window.matchMedia("(min-width: 1200px)");
    this.docked = this.mq.matches;
    this.mq.addEventListener("change", this.onMq);
  }
  disconnectedCallback() {
    this.mq?.removeEventListener("change", this.onMq);
    this.off?.();
    this.ctl?.close();
    this.ctl = undefined;
    this.chatId = undefined;
    super.disconnectedCallback();
  }

  private routeChatId(): string | undefined {
    const route = this.ctx.services?.router.get();
    return route?.name === "chat" ? route.chatId : undefined;
  }
  private composer(): DmComposer | null {
    return (this.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-composer") as DmComposer | null) ?? null;
  }
  private attach(id: string | undefined) {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    this.off?.();
    this.ctl?.close();
    this.ctl = undefined;
    this.chatId = id;
    this.left = false;
    if (!svc || !id) return;
    const ctl = new ChatController({ api: svc.api, chatId: id, open: svc.open, onActivity: () => void svc.sessions.touch(id) });
    this.ctl = ctl;
    this.off = ctl.state.subscribe((s) => {
      this.requestUpdate();
      if (s.problem === "gone" && !this.left) {
        this.left = true;
        svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "That chat no longer exists." });
        svc.router.navigate({ name: "chat" }, { replace: true });
      }
    });
    void ctl.start().then(async () => {
      const waiting = svc.outbox.get();
      if (waiting && waiting.chatId === id && this.ctl === ctl) {
        svc.outbox.set(undefined);
        this.afterSend(await ctl.send(waiting.text), waiting.text);
      }
    });
  }
  protected willUpdate() {
    const id = this.routeChatId();
    if (this.ctx.services && id !== this.chatId) this.attach(id);
  }

  private afterSend(outcome: SendOutcome, text: string) {
    if (outcome === "sent") return;
    this.composer()?.fill(text);
    this.ctx.services?.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: SEND_TOAST[outcome] });
  }
  private async submit(text: string) {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc) return;
    if (this.ctl) return this.afterSend(await this.ctl.send(text), text);
    // A new chat: create it, go there, and send once its controller is running (through the outbox, since any page may start a chat).
    try {
      const chat = await svc.sessions.create("all");
      svc.outbox.set({ chatId: chat.id, text });
      svc.router.navigate({ name: "chat", chatId: chat.id }, { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      this.composer()?.fill(text);
      svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: err instanceof ApiError && err.code === "chat_unavailable" ? "Chat is not available right now." : "Could not start the chat." });
    }
  }
  private async regenerate(entryId: string) {
    const outcome = await this.ctl?.regenerate(entryId);
    if (outcome && outcome !== "sent") this.ctx.services?.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: outcome === "busy" ? SEND_TOAST.busy : "Could not regenerate the answer." });
  }
  private async copy(text: string) {
    const toasts = this.ctx.services?.toasts;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      toasts?.push({ kind: "ok", text: "Copied." });
    } catch {
      toasts?.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not copy." });
    }
  }

  render() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc) return html``;
    const ctl = this.ctl;
    const state = ctl?.state.get();
    const { entry, passageId } = state ? sourcesOf(state) : { entry: undefined, passageId: undefined };
    const title = svc.sessions.get().items.find((c) => c.id === this.chatId)?.title ?? "New chat";
    const count = entry?.citations.length ?? 0;
    const showPanel = !!state && state.sourcesOpen && (count > 0 || state.running);
    const loading = !!state && state.running && !state.focus;
    const empty = !ctl || (!!state && !state.loading && !state.problem && state.entries.length === 0 && !state.pending);
    const blocked = state?.problem === "unavailable" || state?.closed === true;
    return html`
      <header class="bar"><h1>${title}</h1>
        <button class="btn secondary" data-sources-toggle aria-pressed=${showPanel} @click=${() => ctl?.toggleSources()}><dm-icon name="stack"></dm-icon>Sources${count > 0 ? html` <span class="tag accent">${count}</span>` : nothing}</button></header>
      <div class="body" data-layout=${this.docked ? "docked" : "overlay"}>
        <div class="main">
          <div class="view">
            ${empty
              ? html`<div class="empty"><h2>Ask your documents anything</h2><p class="muted">Answers cite the passages they come from.</p>
                  <div class="prompts">${PROMPTS.map((p) => html`<button class="btn secondary" data-prompt @click=${() => this.composer()?.fill(p)}>${p}</button>`)}</div></div>`
              : state && ctl
                ? html`<dm-chat-view .state=${state} .selectedPassage=${passageId}
                    @dm-load-older=${() => void ctl.loadOlder()}
                    @dm-retry-load=${() => this.attach(this.chatId)}
                    @dm-regenerate=${(e: CustomEvent<{ entryId: string }>) => void this.regenerate(e.detail.entryId)}
                    @dm-copy=${(e: CustomEvent<{ text: string }>) => void this.copy(e.detail.text)}
                    @dm-select-citation=${(e: CustomEvent<{ entryId: string; passageId: string }>) => ctl.select(e.detail.entryId, e.detail.passageId)}
                    @dm-open-sources=${(e: CustomEvent<{ entryId: string }>) => ctl.select(e.detail.entryId)}></dm-chat-view>`
                : nothing}
          </div>
          <div class="write">
            <dm-composer ?disabled=${blocked} ?running=${state?.running ?? false} placeholder=${blocked ? "Chat is not available right now." : empty ? "Ask about your documents..." : "Ask a follow-up..."}
              @dm-send=${(e: CustomEvent<{ text: string }>) => void this.submit(e.detail.text)} @dm-stop=${() => void ctl?.stop()}></dm-composer>
          </div>
        </div>
        ${showPanel && ctl
          ? html`${this.docked ? nothing : html`<div class="scrim" data-scrim @click=${() => ctl.toggleSources(false)}></div>`}
            <dm-source-panel class=${this.docked ? "docked" : "overlay"} .entry=${loading ? undefined : entry} .selected=${passageId} .loading=${loading}
              @dm-select-citation=${(e: CustomEvent<{ passageId: string }>) => entry && ctl.select(entry.id, e.detail.passageId)}
              @dm-close-sources=${() => ctl.toggleSources(false)}></dm-source-panel>`
          : nothing}
      </div>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-chat-page", DmChatPage);
```

### Task 193: `DocumentsStore`: the library's state, its live progress, and the upload queue

**Spec:** §5.1 (upload: 50 MB per file, quotas, dedupe returns the existing document), §5.2 (progress over `GET /api/documents/events`), §9 items 42 to 44. Decisions D3, D4; Finding U10.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/state/documents.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/services.ts` (add `documents`)
- Test: `apps/web/src/state/documents.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Api.listDocuments/getDocument/deleteDocument/retryDocument/uploadDocument`, `OpenSse`, `DocumentDto`, `DocumentProgressEvent`, `ApiError`.
- Produces: `DocumentsStore`, `UploadItem`, `UploadReason`, `UploadResult`, `statusLabel` (catalogue). Constants: `MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 50 * 1024 * 1024`, `UPLOAD_CONCURRENCY = 2`, `UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS = ["txt","md","org","doc","docx","pdf","png","jpg","jpeg","webp"]`. `new DocumentsStore(api, open)`.
  - `upload(files)` never rejects. A file over the size cap or with an extension not in the list becomes a failed item at once (`too_large`, `unsupported`) without a request; the rest go through a pool of `UPLOAD_CONCURRENCY`. A success removes its item and puts the document first in `items` (a `duplicate` answer keeps the existing document's place if it is already listed); a failure keeps its item with a `reason`: 403 `quota_exceeded` is `quota`, 413 `too_large`, 415 `unsupported`, status 0 `network`, anything else `server`. It returns one `UploadResult` per file, in the order given.
  - `startEvents()` opens `GET /api/documents/events`. A `progress` frame updates the listed document's status in place; a `ready` or `failed` status (or a document not in the list) fetches that one document to get its summary, statistics and error text; after a reconnect the first page is reloaded, since frames may have been missed.
  - `statusLabel`: `uploaded` is "Queued", `parsing` "Parsing", `embedding` "Indexing", `ready` "Ready", `failed` "Failed".

```ts
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { SseOptions } from "../api/sse";
import { DocumentsStore, MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES, statusLabel } from "./documents";

const id = (n: number) => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const doc = (n: number, over: Partial<DocumentDto> = {}): DocumentDto => ({
  id: id(n), title: `T${n}`, filename: `f${n}.pdf`, mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 10, status: "ready", error: null, stats: null, summary: null,
  createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});
const file = (name: string, size = 3) => { const f = new File(["abc"], name); Object.defineProperty(f, "size", { value: size }); return f; };

function setup(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const sse: { opts?: SseOptions; closed: number; opens: number } = { closed: 0, opens: 0 };
  const api = {
    listDocuments: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [doc(2), doc(1)], nextCursor: "c1" as string | null })),
    getDocument: vi.fn(async (i: string) => doc(Number(i.slice(-2)), { title: "fresh" })),
    deleteDocument: vi.fn(async () => {}),
    retryDocument: vi.fn(async (i: string) => doc(Number(i.slice(-2)), { status: "uploaded" })),
    uploadDocument: vi.fn(async (f: File) => ({ document: doc(9, { filename: f.name }), duplicate: false })),
    ...over,
  };
  const open = vi.fn((opts: SseOptions) => { sse.opts = opts; sse.opens++; return { close: () => { sse.closed++; } }; });
  const store = new DocumentsStore(api as never, open as never);
  const frame = (data: unknown, event = "progress") => sse.opts?.onFrame({ event, data: JSON.stringify(data), id: undefined });
  return { store, api, sse, frame };
}

describe("statusLabel", () => {
  it.each([["uploaded", "Queued"], ["parsing", "Parsing"], ["embedding", "Indexing"], ["ready", "Ready"], ["failed", "Failed"]] as const)("%s is %s", (s, label) => expect(statusLabel(s)).toBe(label));
});

describe("DocumentsStore: listing", () => {
  it("loads the first page, appends the next, and refuses a second load while one is running", async () => {
    const { store, api } = setup();
    const first = store.load();
    void store.load(); // ignored while loading
    await first;
    expect(api.listDocuments).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(store.get()).toMatchObject({ loaded: true, loading: false, error: false, nextCursor: "c1" });
    api.listDocuments.mockResolvedValueOnce({ items: [doc(0)], nextCursor: null });
    await store.loadMore();
    expect(api.listDocuments).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ cursor: "c1" });
    expect(store.get().items.map((d) => d.title)).toEqual(["T2", "T1", "T0"]);
    await store.loadMore();
    expect(api.listDocuments).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });
  it("a failed load keeps the list and says so", async () => {
    const { store } = setup({ listDocuments: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(500, "internal"); }) });
    await store.load();
    expect(store.get()).toMatchObject({ loaded: true, error: true, items: [] });
  });
  it("refreshOne replaces a listed document in place, puts a new one first, and drops one the server no longer has", async () => {
    const { store, api } = setup();
    await store.load();
    expect(await store.refreshOne(id(1))).toBe("ok");
    expect(store.get().items.map((d) => d.title)).toEqual(["T2", "fresh"]);
    await store.refreshOne(id(7));
    expect(store.get().items[0]?.id).toBe(id(7));
    api.getDocument.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(404, "not_found"));
    expect(await store.refreshOne(id(2))).toBe("gone");
    expect(store.get().items.some((d) => d.id === id(2))).toBe(false);
    api.getDocument.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(500, "internal"));
    expect(await store.refreshOne(id(1))).toBe("error");
    expect(store.get().items.some((d) => d.id === id(1))).toBe(true); // an error does not drop it
  });
  it("remove deletes on the server and from the list, and a 404 is as good as done", async () => {
    const { store, api } = setup();
    await store.load();
    await store.remove(id(1));
    expect(api.deleteDocument).toHaveBeenCalledWith(id(1));
    expect(store.get().items.map((d) => d.id)).toEqual([id(2)]);
    api.deleteDocument.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(404, "not_found"));
    await store.remove(id(2));
    expect(store.get().items).toEqual([]);
    api.deleteDocument.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(500, "internal"));
    await expect(store.remove(id(5))).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 500 });
  });
  it("retry swaps in the document the server queued again", async () => {
    const { store } = setup({ listDocuments: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [doc(1, { status: "failed", error: "Could not open the file." })], nextCursor: null })) });
    await store.load();
    await store.retry(id(1));
    expect(store.get().items[0]).toMatchObject({ status: "uploaded" });
  });
});

describe("DocumentsStore: upload", () => {
  it("refuses a file that is too big or of an unknown type without asking the server, and keeps the reason", async () => {
    const { store, api } = setup();
    const out = await store.upload([file("big.pdf", MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1), file("run.exe"), file("ok.TXT")]);
    expect(out.map((r) => r.reason ?? "ok")).toEqual(["too_large", "unsupported", "ok"]);
    expect(api.uploadDocument).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(store.get().uploads.map((u) => [u.name, u.state, u.reason])).toEqual([["big.pdf", "failed", "too_large"], ["run.exe", "failed", "unsupported"]]);
  });

  it("uploads at most two at a time, reports progress, removes a finished item and lists its document first", async () => {
    const gates: { resolve(): void; progress(p: number): void }[] = [];
    const uploadDocument = vi.fn((f: File, o: { onProgress(p: number): void }) =>
      new Promise((resolve) => {
        const n = gates.length;
        gates.push({ resolve: () => resolve({ document: doc(10 + n, { filename: f.name }), duplicate: false }), progress: o.onProgress });
      }),
    );
    const { store } = setup({ uploadDocument });
    const done = store.upload([file("a.txt"), file("b.txt"), file("c.txt"), file("d.txt")]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(gates.length).toBe(2));
    gates[0]?.progress(40);
    expect(store.get().uploads.find((u) => u.name === "a.txt")).toMatchObject({ state: "uploading", percent: 40 });
    expect(store.get().uploads.find((u) => u.name === "c.txt")).toMatchObject({ state: "queued", percent: 0 });
    gates[0]?.resolve();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(gates.length).toBe(3));
    gates[1]?.resolve();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(gates.length).toBe(4));
    gates[2]?.resolve();
    gates[3]?.resolve();
    const results = await done;
    expect(results.map((r) => r.file.name)).toEqual(["a.txt", "b.txt", "c.txt", "d.txt"]);
    expect(store.get().uploads).toEqual([]);
    expect(store.get().items.length).toBe(4);
  });

  it("a duplicate is reported and does not list the document twice", async () => {
    const { store } = setup({ uploadDocument: vi.fn(async () => ({ document: doc(1), duplicate: true })) });
    await store.load();
    const [r] = await store.upload([file("again.pdf")]);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ duplicate: true });
    expect(store.get().items.filter((d) => d.id === id(1)).length).toBe(1);
  });

  it.each([
    [new ApiError(403, "quota_exceeded"), "quota"], [new ApiError(413, "too_large"), "too_large"], [new ApiError(415, "unsupported_type"), "unsupported"],
    [new ApiError(0, "network"), "network"], [new ApiError(500, "internal"), "server"], [new ApiError(429, "too_many_requests"), "server"],
  ])("maps %o to the reason '%s', keeps the item, and lets it be dismissed", async (err, reason) => {
    const { store } = setup({ uploadDocument: vi.fn(async () => { throw err; }) });
    const [r] = await store.upload([file("a.txt")]);
    expect(r?.reason).toBe(reason);
    const [item] = store.get().uploads;
    expect(item).toMatchObject({ state: "failed", reason });
    store.dismissUpload(item?.key as string);
    expect(store.get().uploads).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("DocumentsStore: live progress", () => {
  it("updates a listed document's status in place from a progress frame, without fetching it", async () => {
    const { store, api, frame } = setup({ listDocuments: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [doc(1, { status: "uploaded" })], nextCursor: null })) });
    await store.load();
    store.startEvents();
    frame({ documentId: id(1), step: "parse", status: "parsing" });
    expect(store.get().items[0]?.status).toBe("parsing");
    frame({ documentId: id(1), step: "embed", status: "embedding" });
    expect(store.get().items[0]?.status).toBe("embedding");
    expect(api.getDocument).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("fetches the document when it becomes ready or failed, or when it is not listed", async () => {
    const { store, api, frame } = setup({ listDocuments: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [doc(1, { status: "embedding" })], nextCursor: null })) });
    await store.load();
    store.startEvents();
    frame({ documentId: id(1), step: "finalize", status: "ready" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.getDocument).toHaveBeenCalledWith(id(1)));
    frame({ documentId: id(5), step: "parse", status: "parsing" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.getDocument).toHaveBeenCalledWith(id(5)));
    frame({ documentId: id(1), status: "failed", detail: "corrupt_content" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.getDocument).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3));
  });
  it("ignores a frame that is not a progress event, reloads after a reconnect, and closes when stopped", async () => {
    const { store, api, sse, frame } = setup();
    await store.load();
    store.startEvents();
    store.startEvents(); // already open
    expect(sse.opens).toBe(1);
    const before = store.get();
    frame({ nope: true });
    frame({ documentId: id(1), status: "ready" }, "other");
    expect(store.get()).toBe(before);
    sse.opts?.onOpen?.(); // first open: nothing to catch up on
    expect(api.listDocuments).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    sse.opts?.onOpen?.(); // a reconnect
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.listDocuments).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2));
    store.stopEvents();
    expect(sse.closed).toBe(1);
  });
});

Note: the test expects an upload item state `"queued"` for files waiting for a slot, so `UploadItem["state"]` is `"queued" | "uploading" | "failed"` (the catalogue's `"done"` is never stored: a finished item is removed). Update the type in `documents.ts` accordingly.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `state/documents.ts`.**

```ts
import { DocumentProgressEvent, type DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { Api } from "../api/client";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { OpenSse } from "../api/sse";
import { Store } from "./store";

export const MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 50 * 1024 * 1024;
export const UPLOAD_CONCURRENCY = 2;
export const UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS = ["txt", "md", "org", "doc", "docx", "pdf", "png", "jpg", "jpeg", "webp"] as const;

export type UploadReason = "too_large" | "unsupported" | "quota" | "network" | "server";
export interface UploadItem {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  size: number;
  percent: number;
  state: "queued" | "uploading" | "failed";
  reason?: UploadReason;
}
export interface UploadResult {
  file: File;
  document?: DocumentDto;
  duplicate?: boolean;
  reason?: UploadReason;
}
export interface DocumentsState {
  items: DocumentDto[];
  nextCursor: string | null;
  loading: boolean;
  loaded: boolean;
  error: boolean;
  uploads: UploadItem[];
}

const LABELS: Record<DocumentDto["status"], string> = { uploaded: "Queued", parsing: "Parsing", embedding: "Indexing", ready: "Ready", failed: "Failed" };
export const statusLabel = (s: DocumentDto["status"]): string => LABELS[s];

const reasonOf = (err: unknown): UploadReason => {
  if (err instanceof ApiError) {
    if (err.status === 403 && err.code === "quota_exceeded") return "quota";
    if (err.status === 413) return "too_large";
    if (err.status === 415) return "unsupported";
    if (err.status === 0) return "network";
  }
  return "server";
};
const extensionOf = (name: string) => name.slice(name.lastIndexOf(".") + 1).toLowerCase();

export class DocumentsStore extends Store<DocumentsState> {
  private events: { close(): void } | undefined;
  private opens = 0;
  private seq = 0;
  constructor(
    private readonly api: Pick<Api, "listDocuments" | "getDocument" | "deleteDocument" | "retryDocument" | "uploadDocument">,
    private readonly open: OpenSse,
  ) {
    super({ items: [], nextCursor: null, loading: false, loaded: false, error: false, uploads: [] });
  }

  async load(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.get().loading) return;
    this.update((s) => ({ ...s, loading: true, error: false }));
    try {
      const page = await this.api.listDocuments({ limit: 50 });
      this.update((s) => ({ ...s, items: page.items, nextCursor: page.nextCursor, loading: false, loaded: true }));
    } catch {
      this.update((s) => ({ ...s, loading: false, loaded: true, error: true }));
    }
  }
  async loadMore(): Promise<void> {
    const { nextCursor, loading } = this.get();
    if (nextCursor === null || loading) return;
    this.update((s) => ({ ...s, loading: true }));
    try {
      const page = await this.api.listDocuments({ cursor: nextCursor });
      this.update((s) => ({ ...s, items: [...s.items, ...page.items.filter((d) => !s.items.some((x) => x.id === d.id))], nextCursor: page.nextCursor, loading: false }));
    } catch {
      this.update((s) => ({ ...s, loading: false, error: true }));
    }
  }

  /** A listed document is replaced where it stands; one the list has not seen goes first (newest first). */
  private upsert(next: DocumentDto): void {
    this.update((s) =>
      s.items.some((d) => d.id === next.id)
        ? { ...s, items: s.items.map((d) => (d.id === next.id ? next : d)) }
        : { ...s, items: [next, ...s.items] },
    );
  }
  /** Fetches one document into the list: "ok", "gone" (the server has no such document: it is dropped from the list) or "error". */
  async refreshOne(id: string): Promise<"ok" | "gone" | "error"> {
    try {
      this.upsert(await this.api.getDocument(id));
      return "ok";
    } catch (err) {
      if (!(err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404)) return "error";
      this.update((s) => ({ ...s, items: s.items.filter((d) => d.id !== id) }));
      return "gone";
    }
  }
  async remove(id: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.api.deleteDocument(id);
    } catch (err) {
      if (!(err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404)) throw err;
    }
    this.update((s) => ({ ...s, items: s.items.filter((d) => d.id !== id) }));
  }
  async retry(id: string): Promise<void> {
    this.upsert(await this.api.retryDocument(id));
  }

  dismissUpload(key: string): void {
    this.update((s) => ({ ...s, uploads: s.uploads.filter((u) => u.key !== key) }));
  }
  private patchUpload(key: string, patch: Partial<UploadItem>): void {
    this.update((s) => ({ ...s, uploads: s.uploads.map((u) => (u.key === key ? { ...u, ...patch } : u)) }));
  }

  async upload(files: File[]): Promise<UploadResult[]> {
    const results: UploadResult[] = files.map((file) => ({ file }));
    const queue: { file: File; key: string; at: number }[] = [];
    files.forEach((file, at) => {
      const key = `u${++this.seq}`;
      const reason: UploadReason | undefined = file.size > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES ? "too_large" : (UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS as readonly string[]).includes(extensionOf(file.name)) ? undefined : "unsupported";
      this.update((s) => ({ ...s, uploads: [...s.uploads, { key, name: file.name, size: file.size, percent: 0, state: reason ? "failed" : "queued", ...(reason && { reason }) }] }));
      if (reason) (results[at] as UploadResult).reason = reason;
      else queue.push({ file, key, at });
    });
    const worker = async () => {
      for (let next = queue.shift(); next; next = queue.shift()) {
        const { file, key, at } = next;
        this.patchUpload(key, { state: "uploading" });
        try {
          const out = await this.api.uploadDocument(file, { onProgress: (percent) => this.patchUpload(key, { percent }) });
          this.upsert(out.document);
          this.dismissUpload(key);
          results[at] = { file, document: out.document, duplicate: out.duplicate };
        } catch (err) {
          const reason = reasonOf(err);
          this.patchUpload(key, { state: "failed", reason, percent: 0 });
          results[at] = { file, reason };
        }
      }
    };
    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(UPLOAD_CONCURRENCY, queue.length) }, worker));
    return results;
  }

  startEvents(): void {
    if (this.events) return;
    this.events = this.open({
      url: "/api/documents/events",
      onOpen: () => {
        if (++this.opens > 1) void this.load(); // a reconnect: frames may have been missed
      },
      onFrame: (frame) => {
        if (frame.event !== "progress") return;
        let data: unknown;
        try {
          data = JSON.parse(frame.data);
        } catch {
          return;
        }
        const parsed = DocumentProgressEvent.safeParse(data);
        if (!parsed.success) return;
        const { documentId, status } = parsed.data;
        const listed = this.get().items.some((d) => d.id === documentId);
        if (!listed || status === "ready" || status === "failed") void this.refreshOne(documentId);
        else this.update((s) => ({ ...s, items: s.items.map((d) => (d.id === documentId ? { ...d, status } : d)) }));
      },
    });
  }
  stopEvents(): void {
    this.events?.close();
    this.events = undefined;
    this.opens = 0;
  }
}
```

  `services.ts`: add `documents: DocumentsStore` (`o.documents ?? new DocumentsStore(api, open)`, where `open` is the one already in `createServices`).

git add apps/web
