### Task 194: `dm-scope-picker`, choosing which documents a chat searches

**Spec:** §9 item 41 ("scope picker"), §6.1 (`POST /api/chats {scope?}`, `PATCH /api/chats/:id {scope}`; `ChatScope` is `"all"` or `{documentIds}` of 1 to 100), `ui-mocks.md` §4 Scope picker (segmented control and chips; the popover is not drawn, so it follows the component sheet), §6 deviation 2.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-scope-picker.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.ts` (scope state, picker in the empty chat and in the composer, tag in the header), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-scope-picker.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.test.ts` (new cases)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DocumentsStore` (193: `items`, `loaded`, `nextCursor`, `load()`, `loadMore()`), `Api.getChat/patchChat`, `ChatScope`.
- Produces: `scopeLabel(scope: ChatScope): string` ("All documents", "1 document", "N documents"); `<dm-scope-picker .scope .variant="segmented"|"button" ?disabled>`; it fires `dm-scope-change {scope}` only with a valid scope (never an empty list). `variant="button"` is a button with the stack icon, the label and a caret (`aria-haspopup="dialog"`, `aria-expanded`); `variant="segmented"` is a radio group "All documents" / "Specific documents" with chips and an "Add document" button. Both open the same popover: a dialog named "Choose documents" with a radio pair, a search box (label "Search documents", filters by title), a checkbox per loaded document (title as text, with its status when it is not ready), "Load more" while the library has more, and "Done" (disabled until at least one document is checked in "Specific"). Escape or a click outside closes it and returns focus to the trigger. Picking more than 100 says "You can pick at most 100 documents." and does not add it. Removing the last chip means "All documents".

```ts
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { DocumentsStore } from "../state/documents";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import { scopeLabel } from "./dm-scope-picker";
import "./dm-scope-picker";

const id = (n: number) => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const doc = (n: number, over: Partial<DocumentDto> = {}): DocumentDto => ({
  id: id(n), title: `Report ${n}`, filename: `r${n}.pdf`, mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 1, status: "ready", error: null, stats: null, summary: null,
  createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});

async function setup(props: Record<string, unknown>, docs = [doc(1), doc(2), doc(3, { status: "embedding" })], nextCursor: string | null = null) {
  const api = { listDocuments: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: docs, nextCursor })) };
  const documents = new DocumentsStore(api as never, (() => ({ close() {} })) as never);
  await documents.load();
  const services = createServices({ api: api as never, documents, fetch: (async () => new Response("{}")) as never });
  const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-scope-picker", props);
  return { el, api, documents };
}
const root = (el: Element) => el.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot;
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => root(el).querySelector(sel) as T;
const qa = (el: Element, sel: string) => [...root(el).querySelectorAll(sel)];
const settle = (el: Element) => (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;
const heard = (el: Element) => { const fn = vi.fn(); el.addEventListener("dm-scope-change", (e) => fn((e as CustomEvent).detail.scope)); return fn; };
const open = async (el: Element) => { q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "[data-open]").click(); await settle(el); };

describe("scopeLabel", () => {
  it("names the scope in words", () => {
    expect(scopeLabel("all")).toBe("All documents");
    expect(scopeLabel({ documentIds: [id(1)] })).toBe("1 document");
    expect(scopeLabel({ documentIds: [id(1), id(2)] })).toBe("2 documents");
  });
});

describe("dm-scope-picker: the button", () => {
  it("shows the scope, and opens a named dialog from a trigger that says so", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup({ scope: { documentIds: [id(1), id(2)] }, variant: "button" });
    const trigger = q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "[data-open]");
    expect(trigger.textContent).toContain("2 documents");
    expect(trigger.getAttribute("aria-haspopup")).toBe("dialog");
    expect(trigger.getAttribute("aria-expanded")).toBe("false");
    await open(el);
    expect(trigger.getAttribute("aria-expanded")).toBe("true");
    expect(q(el, "[role=dialog]").getAttribute("aria-label")).toBe("Choose documents");
  });

  it("picks specific documents and reports them, and Done waits for at least one", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup({ scope: "all", variant: "button" });
    const changed = heard(el);
    await open(el);
    q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[name=mode][value=specific]").click();
    await settle(el);
    expect(q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "[data-done]").disabled).toBe(true);
    (qa(el, "input[type=checkbox]")[0] as HTMLInputElement).click();
    (qa(el, "input[type=checkbox]")[2] as HTMLInputElement).click();
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[data-list]").textContent).toContain("Indexing"); // a document that is not ready says so
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "[data-done]").click();
    expect(changed).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ documentIds: [id(1), id(3)] });
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[role=dialog]")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("'All documents' reports all, and nothing is reported when the dialog is just closed", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup({ scope: { documentIds: [id(1)] }, variant: "button" });
    const changed = heard(el);
    await open(el);
    q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[name=mode][value=all]").click();
    await settle(el);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "[data-done]").click();
    expect(changed).toHaveBeenCalledWith("all");
    await open(el);
    q(el, "[role=dialog]").dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "Escape", bubbles: true }));
    await settle(el);
    expect(changed).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(q(el, "[role=dialog]")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("filters by title, says when nothing matches, and loads more of a long library", async () => {
    const { el, api } = await setup({ scope: "all", variant: "button" }, [doc(1), doc(2)], "c1");
    await open(el);
    q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[name=mode][value=specific]").click();
    await settle(el);
    const search = q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[aria-label='Search documents']");
    search.value = "report 2";
    search.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    await settle(el);
    expect(qa(el, "[data-list] li").map((l) => l.textContent?.trim())).toEqual(["Report 2"]);
    search.value = "nothing";
    search.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[data-list]").textContent).toContain("No documents match.");
    api.listDocuments.mockResolvedValueOnce({ items: [doc(5)], nextCursor: null });
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "[data-more]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.listDocuments).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2));
  });

  it("refuses a hundred-and-first document with a sentence", async () => {
    const docs = Array.from({ length: 101 }, (_, n) => doc(n + 1));
    const { el } = await setup({ scope: "all", variant: "button" }, docs);
    await open(el);
    q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[name=mode][value=specific]").click();
    await settle(el);
    for (const box of qa(el, "input[type=checkbox]")) (box as HTMLInputElement).click();
    await settle(el);
    expect(qa(el, "input[type=checkbox]").filter((b) => (b as HTMLInputElement).checked).length).toBe(100);
    expect(q(el, "[data-limit]").textContent).toContain("You can pick at most 100 documents.");
  });

  it("closes on a click outside and puts focus back on the trigger", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup({ scope: "all", variant: "button" });
    await open(el);
    document.body.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("pointerdown", { bubbles: true }));
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[role=dialog]")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("dm-scope-picker: the segmented control with chips", () => {
  it("shows the two choices, chips for the chosen documents, and 'Add document'", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup({ scope: { documentIds: [id(1), id(2)] }, variant: "segmented" });
    expect(qa(el, "input[name=seg]").map((r) => (r as HTMLInputElement).checked)).toEqual([false, true]);
    expect(qa(el, "[data-chip]").map((c) => c.getAttribute("data-title"))).toEqual(["Report 1", "Report 2"]);
    expect(q(el, "button[data-add]")).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("removing a chip reports the rest, and removing the last one means all documents", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup({ scope: { documentIds: [id(1), id(2)] }, variant: "segmented" });
    const changed = heard(el);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "[data-chip] button").click();
    expect(changed).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ documentIds: [id(2)] });
    (el as unknown as { scope: unknown }).scope = { documentIds: [id(2)] };
    await settle(el);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "[data-chip] button").click();
    expect(changed).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("all");
  });

  it("choosing 'All documents' reports all; choosing 'Specific documents' with nothing picked opens the chooser instead of reporting", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup({ scope: { documentIds: [id(1)] }, variant: "segmented" });
    const changed = heard(el);
    q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[name=seg][value=all]").click();
    expect(changed).toHaveBeenCalledWith("all");
    (el as unknown as { scope: unknown }).scope = "all";
    await settle(el);
    q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[name=seg][value=specific]").click();
    await settle(el);
    expect(changed).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(q(el, "[role=dialog]")).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("a chip whose document is not loaded yet still has a name", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup({ scope: { documentIds: [id(42)] }, variant: "segmented" });
    expect(q(el, "[data-chip]").getAttribute("data-title")).toBe("A document");
  });
});
```

```ts
describe("dm-chat-page: scope", () => {
  const chatDto = (scope: unknown) => ({ id: ID, title: "Plan", scope, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", lastMessageAt: null, messageCount: 2 });
  it("shows the chat's scope in the header and in the composer, and changes it on the server", async () => {
    media(true);
    const t = setup();
    (t.services.api as unknown as { getChat: unknown }).getChat = vi.fn(async () => chatDto({ documentIds: [id(1)] }));
    (t.services.api as unknown as { patchChat: unknown }).patchChat = vi.fn(async (_i: string, req: { scope: unknown }) => chatDto(req.scope));
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("[data-scope-tag]")?.textContent).toContain("1 document"));
    const picker = el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-scope-picker[variant=button]") as HTMLElement;
    picker.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-scope-change", { detail: { scope: "all" }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.services.api.patchChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ID, { scope: "all" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("[data-scope-tag]")?.textContent).toContain("All documents"));
  });
  it("keeps the old scope and says so when the change fails", async () => {
    media(true);
    const t = setup();
    (t.services.api as unknown as { getChat: unknown }).getChat = vi.fn(async () => chatDto("all"));
    (t.services.api as unknown as { patchChat: unknown }).patchChat = vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(400, "invalid_scope"); });
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("[data-scope-tag]")).not.toBeNull());
    (el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-scope-picker[variant=button]") as HTMLElement).dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-scope-change", { detail: { scope: { documentIds: [id(1)] } }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.services.toasts.get().at(-1)?.text).toBe("Could not change the scope."));
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("[data-scope-tag]")?.textContent).toContain("All documents");
  });
  it("a new chat is created with the scope chosen before the first message", async () => {
    media(true);
    const t = setup();
    (t.services.router as unknown as { navigate: (r: unknown, o?: unknown) => void }).navigate({ name: "chat" }, { replace: true });
    (t.services.sessions as unknown as { create: unknown }).create = vi.fn(async () => ({ id: ID, title: null, scope: { documentIds: [id(2)] }, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", lastMessageAt: null, messageCount: 0 }));
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(t.services, "dm-chat-page");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-scope-picker[variant=segmented]")).not.toBeNull());
    (el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-scope-picker[variant=segmented]") as HTMLElement).dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-scope-change", { detail: { scope: { documentIds: [id(2)] } }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
    const composer = el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-composer") as HTMLElement;
    composer.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-send", { detail: { text: "Hi" }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect((t.services.sessions as unknown as { create: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).create).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ documentIds: [id(2)] }));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `dm-scope-picker.ts`.**

```ts
import type { ChatScope } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { statusLabel } from "../state/documents";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

const MAX_DOCS = 100;

export function scopeLabel(scope: ChatScope): string {
  if (scope === "all") return "All documents";
  const n = scope.documentIds.length;
  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? "document" : "documents"}`;
}

export class DmScopePicker extends LitElement {
  static properties = {
    scope: { attribute: false },
    variant: { type: String, reflect: true },
    disabled: { type: Boolean },
    opened: { state: true },
    mode: { state: true },
    picked: { state: true },
    filter: { state: true },
    limit: { state: true },
  };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { position: relative; display: inline-block; }
      .seg { display: inline-flex; gap: 4px; padding: 3px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-sm); }
      .seg label { margin: 0; padding: 7px 12px; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 13px; cursor: pointer; color: var(--dm-text); }
      .seg input { position: absolute; opacity: 0; }
      .seg input:checked + span { color: var(--dm-accent); }
      .seg label:has(input:checked) { box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--dm-accent); }
      .seg label:has(input:focus-visible) { outline: var(--dm-focus-ring); outline-offset: 2px; }
      .chips { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; margin-top: 10px; }
      [data-chip] { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; min-height: 30px; padding: 0 4px 0 10px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); background: var(--dm-surface); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-sm); font-size: 13px; }
      [data-chip] .btn { width: 22px; min-height: 22px; padding: 0; }
      [data-add] { border: 1px dashed var(--dm-divider); }
      [data-open] { font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--dm-muted); min-height: 28px; }
      [role="dialog"] { position: absolute; z-index: 5; bottom: calc(100% + 6px); left: 0; width: min(360px, 90vw); padding: 14px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-lg); background: var(--dm-surface); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-lg); display: grid; gap: 10px; }
      :host([variant="segmented"]) [role="dialog"] { bottom: auto; top: calc(100% + 6px); }
      .modes { display: flex; gap: 14px; }
      .modes label { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; margin: 0; font-size: 13px; color: var(--dm-text); }
      [data-list] { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; max-height: 220px; overflow: auto; display: grid; gap: 2px; }
      [data-list] li { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 4px 6px; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 13px; }
      [data-list] label { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin: 0; color: var(--dm-text); font-size: 13px; cursor: pointer; flex: 1; min-width: 0; }
      .state { color: var(--dm-muted); font-size: 12px; margin-left: auto; }
      .actions { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; gap: 8px; }
    `,
  ];
  declare scope: ChatScope;
  declare variant: "segmented" | "button";
  declare disabled: boolean;
  declare opened: boolean;
  declare mode: "all" | "specific";
  declare picked: string[];
  declare filter: string;
  declare limit: boolean;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.documents]);
  private readonly outside = (e: Event) => {
    if (this.opened && !e.composedPath().includes(this)) this.close();
  };
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.scope = "all";
    this.variant = "button";
    this.disabled = false;
    this.opened = false;
    this.mode = "all";
    this.picked = [];
    this.filter = "";
    this.limit = false;
  }
  connectedCallback() {
    super.connectedCallback();
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", this.outside);
  }
  disconnectedCallback() {
    document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", this.outside);
    super.disconnectedCallback();
  }
  protected firstUpdated() {
    const documents = this.ctx.services?.documents;
    if (documents && !documents.get().loaded) void documents.load();
  }
  private fire(scope: ChatScope) {
    this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-scope-change", { detail: { scope }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
  }
  private openChooser(mode?: "all" | "specific") {
    this.mode = mode ?? (this.scope === "all" ? "all" : "specific");
    this.picked = this.scope === "all" ? [] : [...this.scope.documentIds];
    this.filter = "";
    this.limit = false;
    this.opened = true;
  }
  private close() {
    this.opened = false;
    void this.updateComplete.then(() => this.shadowRoot?.querySelector<HTMLElement>("[data-open], [data-add]")?.focus());
  }
  private done() {
    if (this.mode === "all") this.fire("all");
    else if (this.picked.length > 0) this.fire({ documentIds: this.picked });
    this.close();
  }
  private toggle(input: HTMLInputElement, id: string) {
    const on = input.checked; // a click has already flipped the box
    if (on && this.picked.length >= MAX_DOCS) {
      input.checked = false;
      this.limit = true;
      return;
    }
    this.limit = false;
    this.picked = on ? [...this.picked, id] : this.picked.filter((x) => x !== id);
  }
  private title(id: string): string {
    return this.ctx.services?.documents.get().items.find((d) => d.id === id)?.title ?? "A document";
  }
  private chooser() {
    const docs = this.ctx.services?.documents;
    const state = docs?.get();
    const items = (state?.items ?? []).filter((d) => d.title.toLowerCase().includes(this.filter.trim().toLowerCase()));
    return html`<div role="dialog" aria-label="Choose documents" @keydown=${(e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === "Escape") { e.stopPropagation(); this.close(); } }}>
      <div class="modes">
        <label><input type="radio" name="mode" value="all" .checked=${this.mode === "all"} @click=${() => (this.mode = "all")} />All documents</label>
        <label><input type="radio" name="mode" value="specific" .checked=${this.mode === "specific"} @click=${() => (this.mode = "specific")} />Specific documents</label>
      </div>
      ${this.mode === "specific"
        ? html`<input class="input" type="search" aria-label="Search documents" placeholder="Search documents" .value=${this.filter} @input=${(e: Event) => (this.filter = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value)} />
          <ul data-list>
            ${items.length === 0 ? html`<li>No documents match.</li>` : nothing}
            ${items.map((d) => html`<li><label><input type="checkbox" .checked=${this.picked.includes(d.id)} @click=${(e: Event) => this.toggle(e.target as HTMLInputElement, d.id)} /><span>${d.title}</span></label>${d.status !== "ready" ? html`<span class="state">${statusLabel(d.status)}</span>` : nothing}</li>`)}
          </ul>
          ${this.limit ? html`<div class="error" data-limit role="alert">You can pick at most ${MAX_DOCS} documents.</div>` : nothing}
          ${state?.nextCursor ? html`<button class="btn secondary" data-more @click=${() => void docs?.loadMore()}>Load more</button>` : nothing}`
        : nothing}
      <div class="actions"><span class="muted">${this.mode === "specific" ? `${this.picked.length} selected` : ""}</span>
        <button class="btn primary" data-done ?disabled=${this.mode === "specific" && this.picked.length === 0} @click=${() => this.done()}>Done</button></div>
    </div>`;
  }
  render() {
    if (this.variant === "button")
      return html`<button class="btn ghost" data-open aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-expanded=${this.opened} ?disabled=${this.disabled} @click=${() => (this.opened ? this.close() : this.openChooser())}>
          <dm-icon name="stack"></dm-icon>${scopeLabel(this.scope)}<dm-icon name="caret-down"></dm-icon></button>${this.opened ? this.chooser() : nothing}`;
    const specific = this.scope !== "all";
    return html`<div role="radiogroup" aria-label="Scope" class="seg">
        <label><input type="radio" name="seg" value="all" .checked=${!specific} ?disabled=${this.disabled} @click=${() => this.fire("all")} /><span>All documents</span></label>
        <label><input type="radio" name="seg" value="specific" .checked=${specific} ?disabled=${this.disabled} @click=${() => { if (!specific) this.openChooser("specific"); }} /><span>Specific documents</span></label>
      </div>
      ${this.scope !== "all"
        ? html`<div class="chips">
            ${this.scope.documentIds.map((id) => html`<span data-chip data-title=${this.title(id)}>${this.title(id)}<button class="btn icon" aria-label="Remove document" @click=${() => this.remove(id)}><dm-icon name="x"></dm-icon></button></span>`)}
            <button class="btn secondary" data-add @click=${() => this.openChooser("specific")}><dm-icon name="plus"></dm-icon>Add document</button></div>`
        : nothing}
      ${this.opened ? this.chooser() : nothing}`;
  }
  private remove(id: string) {
    if (this.scope === "all") return;
    const rest = this.scope.documentIds.filter((x) => x !== id);
    this.fire(rest.length > 0 ? { documentIds: rest } : "all");
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-scope-picker", DmScopePicker);
```

  `dm-chat-page.ts` changes:
  - add `private scope: ChatScope = "all"` and import `type ChatScope`, `scopeLabel`;
  - in `attach(id)`: set `this.scope = "all"`; when `id` is set, `void svc.api.getChat(id).then((c) => { if (this.chatId === id) { this.scope = c.scope; this.requestUpdate(); } }, () => {})`;
  - handler:

```ts
  private async changeScope(scope: ChatScope) {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc) return;
    if (!this.chatId) {
      this.scope = scope; // a chat that does not exist yet is created with it
      this.requestUpdate();
      return;
    }
    try {
      const next = await svc.api.patchChat(this.chatId, { scope });
      this.scope = next.scope;
      void svc.sessions.touch(this.chatId);
    } catch {
      svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not change the scope." });
    }
    this.requestUpdate();
  }
```

### Task 195: `dm-upload-zone`, `dm-status-badge` and `dm-file-tile`

**Spec:** §9 item 42 ("Upload zone"), §5.1 (50 MB, the type list), `ui-mocks.md` §4 Upload dropzone, Status badge and File-type icon. Decisions D6, D7.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-file-tile.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-status-badge.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-upload-zone.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/dm-source-panel.ts` (file tiles from the documents the page knows), `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.ts` (pass them), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-file-tile.test.ts`, `dm-status-badge.test.ts`, `dm-upload-zone.test.ts`, and new cases in `dm-source-panel.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `fileKind(filename: string | undefined, mime?: string): { label: string; kind: string }`: the label is the file's real extension in capitals (`PDF`, `DOCX`, `DOC`, `TXT`, `MD`, `ORG`, `PNG`, `JPG`, `WEBP`); a file without a known extension falls back to the MIME type (`application/pdf` is `PDF`), else `FILE`. (The mocks label the same type differently on cards and in the table; one rule everywhere is what this does.) `<dm-file-tile filename mime size="sm"|"md"|"lg">` draws it; the label carries the meaning and the hue only helps scanning.
  - `<dm-status-badge status="uploaded"|"parsing"|"embedding"|"ready"|"failed">` or `<dm-status-badge uploading percent>`: height 22, icon and text (`Queued`, `Parsing`, `Indexing`, `Ready`, `Failed`, `Uploading 40%`); busy states spin; an uploading badge adds a `<progress>` with an accessible name.
  - `<dm-upload-zone variant="compact"|"empty" ?disabled>`: fires `dm-files {files: File[]}` from the browse button, the file input or a drop (a dropped folder is ignored). While a drag is over it, it says "Release to upload N files". It does not upload anything itself.
  - `dm-source-panel` gains `.docs: ReadonlyMap<string, { filename: string; mime: string }>`; a card's tile uses it, and a card whose document is unknown keeps the neutral `file-text` icon.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import "./dm-status-badge";

type Badge = HTMLElement & { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> };
async function mount(props: Record<string, unknown>) {

  Object.assign(el, props);

}
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as T;

describe("dm-status-badge", () => {

    ["uploaded", "Queued", false], ["parsing", "Parsing", true], ["embedding", "Indexing", true], ["ready", "Ready", false], ["failed", "Failed", false],
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import "./dm-upload-zone";

type Zone = HTMLElement & { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> };
async function mount(props: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const el = document.createElement("dm-upload-zone") as Zone;
  Object.assign(el, props);
  document.body.append(el);
  await el.updateComplete;
  return el;
}
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as T;
const file = (n: string) => new File(["x"], n);
const drag = (type: string, files: File[], items?: { kind: string; webkitGetAsEntry?: () => { isDirectory: boolean } | null }[]) => {
  const e = new Event(type, { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }) as Event & { dataTransfer?: unknown };
  e.dataTransfer = { files, items: items ?? files.map(() => ({ kind: "file" })), types: ["Files"], dropEffect: "none" };
  return e;
};
const heard = (el: Element) => { const fn = vi.fn(); el.addEventListener("dm-files", (e) => fn((e as CustomEvent).detail.files)); return fn; };

describe("dm-upload-zone", () => {
  it("states what it takes and how big, and has a named browse button", async () => {
    const el = await mount();
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("PDF, DOCX, DOC, TXT, Markdown, Org, PNG, JPG, WEBP - up to 50 MB each");
    expect(q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-browse]").textContent).toContain("browse");
  });
  it("reports the files chosen with the browse button, and clears the input so the same file can be chosen again", async () => {
    const el = await mount();
    const got = heard(el);
    const input = q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[type=file]");
    expect(input.multiple).toBe(true);
    let clicks = 0;
    input.click = () => { clicks++; };
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-browse]").click();
    expect(clicks).toBe(1);
    const a = file("a.pdf");
    Object.defineProperty(input, "files", { value: [a], configurable: true });
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event("change"));
    expect(got).toHaveBeenCalledWith([a]);
  });
  it("says how many files it would take while a drag is over it, and forgets when the drag leaves", async () => {
    const el = await mount();
    const zone = q<HTMLElement>(el, "[data-zone]");
    zone.dispatchEvent(drag("dragenter", [file("a.pdf"), file("b.pdf")]));
    zone.dispatchEvent(drag("dragover", [file("a.pdf"), file("b.pdf")]));
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(zone.textContent).toContain("Release to upload 2 files");
    expect(zone.hasAttribute("data-dragging")).toBe(true);
    zone.dispatchEvent(drag("dragleave", []));
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(zone.hasAttribute("data-dragging")).toBe(false);
  });
  it("a drop reports the files, and leaves a dropped folder out", async () => {
    const el = await mount();
    const got = heard(el);
    const zone = q<HTMLElement>(el, "[data-zone]");
    const [a, dir] = [file("a.pdf"), file("folder")];
    const e = drag("drop", [a, dir], [{ kind: "file", webkitGetAsEntry: () => ({ isDirectory: false }) }, { kind: "file", webkitGetAsEntry: () => ({ isDirectory: true }) }]);
    zone.dispatchEvent(e);
    expect(e.defaultPrevented).toBe(true);
    expect(got).toHaveBeenCalledWith([a]);
  });
  it("disabled does nothing", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ disabled: true });
    const got = heard(el);
    expect(q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-browse]").disabled).toBe(true);
    q<HTMLElement>(el, "[data-zone]").dispatchEvent(drag("drop", [file("a.pdf")]));
    expect(got).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("the empty-library variant is a card that invites the first upload and lists the types", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ variant: "empty" });
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Upload your first documents");
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Up to 50 MB per file");
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll("[data-type]").length).toBe(9);
  });
});
```

`dm-file-tile.ts`:

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { classMap } from "lit/directives/class-map.js";

const BY_EXT: Record<string, [label: string, kind: string]> = {
  pdf: ["PDF", "pdf"], docx: ["DOCX", "doc"], doc: ["DOC", "doc"], txt: ["TXT", "txt"], md: ["MD", "md"], org: ["ORG", "org"],
  png: ["PNG", "img"], jpg: ["JPG", "img"], jpeg: ["JPG", "img"], webp: ["WEBP", "img"],
};
const BY_MIME: Record<string, string> = {
  "application/pdf": "pdf", "text/plain": "txt", "text/markdown": "md", "application/msword": "doc",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document": "docx", "image/png": "png", "image/jpeg": "jpg", "image/webp": "webp",
};

/** The tile's label is the file's real extension; the MIME type is the fallback; `FILE` when neither is known. */
export function fileKind(filename: string | undefined, mime?: string): { label: string; kind: string } {
  const ext = filename?.includes(".") ? filename.slice(filename.lastIndexOf(".") + 1).toLowerCase() : "";
  const hit = BY_EXT[ext] ?? BY_EXT[BY_MIME[mime ?? ""] ?? ""];
  return hit ? { label: hit[0], kind: hit[1] } : { label: "FILE", kind: "file" };
}

export class DmFileTile extends LitElement {
  static properties = { filename: { type: String }, mime: { type: String }, size: { type: String } };
  static styles = css`
    :host { display: inline-block; }
    .tile { display: inline-grid; place-items: center; font: 500 8px/1 var(--dm-font); border-radius: 5px; background: var(--dm-ftype-bg); color: var(--dm-ftype-fg); width: 20px; height: 20px; }
    .tile.md { width: 28px; height: 28px; font-size: 9px; border-radius: 6px; }
    .tile.lg { width: 44px; height: 44px; font-size: 11px; border-radius: 10px; }
    .k-pdf { --h: 25; } .k-doc { --h: 255; } .k-md { --h: 200; } .k-org { --h: 140; } .k-img { --h: 70; } .k-txt, .k-file { --h: 0; --dm-ftype-bg: color-mix(in srgb, var(--dm-neutral-700) 40%, transparent); --dm-ftype-fg: var(--dm-text); }
  `;
  declare filename: string | undefined;
  declare mime: string | undefined;
  declare size: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.size = "sm";
  }
  render() {
    const { label, kind } = fileKind(this.filename, this.mime);
    return html`<span class=${classMap({ tile: true, [`k-${kind}`]: true, [this.size]: true })}>${label}</span>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-file-tile", DmFileTile);
```

  (The `--dm-ftype-bg` and `--dm-ftype-fg` tokens read `var(--h, 0)`; a class that sets `--h` is how the hue reaches them without a `style` attribute. The label font is 8 to 11 px by design (the mocks' tile text); it is a tile label, not body text, and the contrast test of Task 178a covers its colors.)

`dm-status-badge.ts`:

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { statusLabel } from "../state/documents";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

const ICON = { uploaded: "arrow-up", parsing: "circle-notch", embedding: "circle-notch", ready: "check-circle", failed: "warning-circle" } as const;

export class DmStatusBadge extends LitElement {
  static properties = { status: { type: String }, uploading: { type: Boolean }, percent: { type: Number } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: inline-grid; gap: 4px; }
      .badge { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 5px; height: 22px; padding: 0 8px; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 12px; background: var(--dm-busy-bg); color: var(--dm-busy); }
      .badge.ready { background: var(--dm-ok-bg); color: var(--dm-ok); }
      .badge.failed { background: var(--dm-err-bg); color: var(--dm-err); }
      progress { width: 110px; height: 3px; appearance: none; border: 0; border-radius: 2px; background: var(--dm-neutral-800); }
      progress::-webkit-progress-bar { background: var(--dm-neutral-800); border-radius: 2px; }
      progress::-webkit-progress-value { background: var(--dm-accent); border-radius: 2px; }
      progress::-moz-progress-bar { background: var(--dm-accent); border-radius: 2px; }
    `,
  ];
  declare status: "uploaded" | "parsing" | "embedding" | "ready" | "failed";
  declare uploading: boolean;
  declare percent: number;
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.status = "uploaded";
    this.uploading = false;
    this.percent = 0;
  }
  render() {
    const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(this.percent)));
    if (this.uploading)
      return html`<span class="badge"><dm-icon name="arrow-up"></dm-icon>Uploading ${pct}%</span><progress max="100" .value=${pct} aria-label="Upload progress"></progress>`;
    const busy = this.status === "parsing" || this.status === "embedding";
    return html`<span class="badge ${this.status}"><dm-icon class=${busy ? "spin" : ""} name=${ICON[this.status]}></dm-icon>${statusLabel(this.status)}</span>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-status-badge", DmStatusBadge);
```

`dm-upload-zone.ts`:

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS } from "../state/documents";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

const TYPES = ["PDF", "DOCX", "DOC", "TXT", "Markdown", "Org", "PNG", "JPG", "WEBP"];

export class DmUploadZone extends LitElement {
  static properties = { variant: { type: String, reflect: true }, disabled: { type: Boolean }, dragging: { state: true }, count: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; }
      [data-zone] { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 14px; padding: 14px 18px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-lg); border: 1.5px dashed var(--dm-neutral-700); }
      [data-zone][data-dragging] { border-color: var(--dm-accent); background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--dm-accent) 10%, transparent); }
      :host([variant="empty"]) [data-zone] { flex-direction: column; justify-content: center; text-align: center; padding: 56px 64px; gap: 12px; background: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 0, var(--dm-accent-900), transparent 70%); }
      .text { flex: 1; display: grid; gap: 2px; }
      h2 { font-size: 26px; }
      .hint { font-size: 12px; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      .types { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 6px; justify-content: center; }
      input[type="file"] { display: none; }
    `,
  ];
  declare variant: "compact" | "empty";
  declare disabled: boolean;
  declare dragging: boolean;
  declare count: number;
  private depth = 0;
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.variant = "compact";
    this.disabled = false;
    this.dragging = false;
    this.count = 0;
  }
  private emit(files: File[]) {
    if (files.length > 0) this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-files", { detail: { files }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
  }
  private files(e: Event): File[] {
    const dt = (e as DragEvent).dataTransfer;
    if (!dt) return [];
    const items = [...(dt.items ?? [])] as { kind: string; webkitGetAsEntry?: () => { isDirectory: boolean } | null }[];
    return [...dt.files].filter((_f, i) => items[i]?.webkitGetAsEntry?.()?.isDirectory !== true);
  }
  private over = (e: Event) => {
    if (this.disabled) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const dt = (e as DragEvent).dataTransfer;
    this.count = dt?.items?.length ?? dt?.files?.length ?? 0;
    this.dragging = true;
  };
  private enter = (e: Event) => {
    this.depth++;
    this.over(e);
  };
  private leave = () => {
    this.depth = Math.max(0, this.depth - 1);
    if (this.depth === 0) this.dragging = false;
  };
  private drop = (e: Event) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    this.depth = 0;
    this.dragging = false;
    if (!this.disabled) this.emit(this.files(e));
  };
  /** Opens the file chooser (the page's own Upload button calls this too). */
  browse = (): void => this.shadowRoot?.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>("input[type=file]")?.click();
  private chosen(e: Event) {
    const input = e.target as HTMLInputElement;
    this.emit([...(input.files ?? [])]);
    try {
      input.value = "";
    } catch {
      // some environments refuse to clear a file input
    }
  }
  render() {
    const empty = this.variant === "empty";
    const accept = UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS.map((x) => `.${x}`).join(",");
    return html`<div data-zone ?data-dragging=${this.dragging} @dragenter=${this.enter} @dragover=${this.over} @dragleave=${this.leave} @drop=${this.drop}>
      <dm-icon name=${this.dragging ? "arrow-fat-line-down" : empty ? "files" : "upload-simple"}></dm-icon>
      <div class="text">
        ${this.dragging
          ? html`<strong>Release to upload ${this.count} ${this.count === 1 ? "file" : "files"}</strong>`
          : empty
            ? html`<h2>Upload your first documents</h2><p class="muted">Drop files here, or browse. DocMind reads them, summarizes them and makes them searchable.</p>`
            : html`<strong>Drop files here or <button class="btn ghost" data-browse ?disabled=${this.disabled} @click=${this.browse}>browse</button></strong>`}
        ${empty ? null : html`<span class="hint">PDF, DOCX, DOC, TXT, Markdown, Org, PNG, JPG, WEBP - up to 50 MB each</span>`}
      </div>
      ${empty
        ? html`<button class="btn primary" data-browse ?disabled=${this.disabled} @click=${this.browse}>Browse files</button>
            <div class="types">${TYPES.map((t) => html`<span class="tag" data-type>${t}</span>`)}</div><span class="hint">Up to 50 MB per file</span>`
        : null}
      <input type="file" multiple accept=${accept} @change=${this.chosen} />
    </div>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-upload-zone", DmUploadZone);
```

  In the compact variant the visible browse control is the `browse` button in the sentence; its text is "browse" so the test's `textContent` contains it; in the empty variant the button reads "Browse files" (the test of the compact variant does not run against it).
  `dm-source-panel.ts`: add `docs: { attribute: false }` (a `ReadonlyMap<string, { filename: string; mime: string }>`, default an empty map) and in the card row replace `<dm-icon name="file-text">` with:

```ts
${this.docs.get(c.documentId) ? html`<dm-file-tile .filename=${this.docs.get(c.documentId)?.filename} .mime=${this.docs.get(c.documentId)?.mime}></dm-file-tile>` : html`<dm-icon name="file-text"></dm-icon>`}
```

  (import `./dm-file-tile`). `dm-chat-page.ts`: the services line becomes `(s) => [s.router, s.sessions, s.documents]`, and the panel gets `.docs=${new Map(svc.documents.get().items.map((d) => [d.id, { filename: d.filename, mime: d.mime }]))}`; the chat page also calls `void svc.documents.load()` in `firstUpdated` when `!svc.documents.get().loaded` (the picker already does; one place is enough: keep it in the page and remove it from the picker's `firstUpdated`, the picker's tests construct a loaded store). `components/index.ts`: the three imports.

## Phase E: documents (spec §9 items 43 and 44)

### Task 196a: `dm-doc-table`

**Spec:** §7 (`dm-doc-table`, "Document table"), §9 item 43, `ui-mocks.md` §4 Document table and §6 deviation 6 (own markup, not `lu-data-table`). Decision D7.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-doc-table.ts`, `apps/web/src/state/format.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-doc-table.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/state/format.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DocumentDto`, `UploadItem`, `UploadReason`, `relativeTime`, `fileKind`, `<dm-file-tile>`, `<dm-status-badge>`.
- Produces: `formatBytes(n: number): string` ("0 B", "512 B", "1.5 KB", "3.2 MB", one decimal from 1 KB); `uploadReasonText(r: UploadReason): string`; `type DocRow = { kind: "doc"; doc: DocumentDto } | { kind: "upload"; item: UploadItem }`; `<dm-doc-table .rows .selected .now>`: a real `<table>` with a visually hidden caption "Documents", column headers (checkbox, Name, Type, Size, Uploaded, Status, and an actions column named "Actions"), a "select all" checkbox with a name, and per row: a checkbox named "Select <title>", the file tile, the title as a link to `/documents/<id>`, the extension label, the size, the relative time, the status badge (`Failed` rows add a Retry button and the error text under the title) and a Delete button named "Delete <title>". An upload row has no checkbox or link: its name, size, an uploading badge with the percent (or "Waiting" while queued), or its failure reason with a Dismiss button. Events (bubble, composed): `dm-select {id, on}`, `dm-select-all {on}`, `dm-retry {id}`, `dm-delete {id}`, `dm-dismiss-upload {key}`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { formatBytes } from "./format";

describe("formatBytes", () => {
  it.each([[0, "0 B"], [512, "512 B"], [1024, "1.0 KB"], [1536, "1.5 KB"], [3.2 * 1024 * 1024, "3.2 MB"], [50 * 1024 * 1024, "50.0 MB"], [2 * 1024 ** 3, "2.0 GB"]])("%d is %s", (n, text) => expect(formatBytes(n)).toBe(text));
  it("is never negative or NaN", () => {
    expect(formatBytes(-5)).toBe("0 B");
    expect(formatBytes(Number.NaN)).toBe("0 B");
  });
});
```

`dm-doc-table.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { UploadItem } from "../state/documents";
import type { DocRow } from "./dm-doc-table";
import "./dm-doc-table";

const id = (n: number) => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const doc = (n: number, over: Partial<DocumentDto> = {}): DocumentDto => ({
  id: id(n), title: `Report <b>${n}</b>`, filename: `r${n}.pdf`, mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 1536, status: "ready", error: null, stats: null, summary: null,
  createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});
const upload = (key: string, over: Partial<UploadItem> = {}): UploadItem => ({ key, name: `${key}.pdf`, size: 2048, percent: 0, state: "queued", ...over });
const docRow = (d: DocumentDto): DocRow => ({ kind: "doc", doc: d });
const upRow = (u: UploadItem): DocRow => ({ kind: "upload", item: u });

type Table = HTMLElement & { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> };
async function mount(rows: DocRow[], selected: string[] = []) {
  const el = document.createElement("dm-doc-table") as Table;
  Object.assign(el, { rows, selected: new Set(selected), now: new Date("2026-10-21T10:12:00.000Z") });
  document.body.append(el);
  await el.updateComplete;
  return el;
}
const root = (el: Element) => el.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot;
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => root(el).querySelector(sel) as T;
const qa = (el: Element, sel: string) => [...root(el).querySelectorAll(sel)];
const heard = (el: Element, name: string) => { const fn = vi.fn(); el.addEventListener(name, (e) => fn((e as CustomEvent).detail)); return fn; };

describe("dm-doc-table: structure", () => {
  it("is a table with a caption and named columns, and a named select-all", async () => {
    const el = await mount([docRow(doc(1))]);
    expect(q(el, "caption").textContent).toBe("Documents");
    expect(qa(el, "th").map((h) => h.textContent?.trim())).toEqual(["", "Name", "Type", "Size", "Uploaded", "Status", "Actions"]);
    expect(qa(el, "th").every((h) => h.getAttribute("scope") === "col")).toBe(true);
    expect(q(el, "thead input[type=checkbox]").getAttribute("aria-label")).toBe("Select all documents");
  });

  it("draws a document row from text: title link, type, size, relative time and the status", async () => {
    const el = await mount([docRow(doc(1))]);
    const row = q(el, "tbody tr");
    expect(row.querySelector("a")?.textContent).toBe("Report <b>1</b>");
    expect(row.querySelector("a b")).toBeNull();
    expect(row.querySelector("a")?.getAttribute("href")).toBe(`/documents/${id(1)}`);
    const cells = [...row.querySelectorAll("td")].map((c) => c.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim());
    expect(cells[2]).toBe("PDF");
    expect(cells[3]).toBe("1.5 KB");
    expect(cells[4]).toBe("12 min ago");
    expect(row.querySelector("dm-status-badge")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(row.querySelector("input[type=checkbox]")?.getAttribute("aria-label")).toBe("Select Report <b>1</b>");
    expect(row.querySelector("button[data-delete]")?.getAttribute("aria-label")).toBe("Delete Report <b>1</b>");
  });

  it("a failed document shows its reason under the title and a Retry button", async () => {
    const el = await mount([docRow(doc(1, { status: "failed", error: "Could not open the file." }))]);
    expect(q(el, "[data-error]").textContent).toBe("Could not open the file.");
    expect(q(el, "button[data-retry]")).not.toBeNull();
    const ok = await mount([docRow(doc(2))]);
    expect(q(ok, "button[data-retry]")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("an upload row has no checkbox or link, and shows waiting, progress, or its failure with Dismiss", async () => {
    const el = await mount([upRow(upload("a")), upRow(upload("b", { state: "uploading", percent: 40 })), upRow(upload("c", { state: "failed", reason: "too_large" }))]);
    const rows = qa(el, "tbody tr");
    expect(rows.every((r) => r.querySelector("input[type=checkbox]") === null && r.querySelector("a") === null)).toBe(true);
    expect(rows[0]?.textContent).toContain("Waiting");
    const badge = rows[1]?.querySelector("dm-status-badge") as HTMLElement & { uploading: boolean; percent: number };
    expect([badge.uploading, badge.percent]).toEqual([true, 40]);
    expect(rows[2]?.textContent).toContain("Too large (50 MB limit).");
    const dismissed = heard(el, "dm-dismiss-upload");
    (rows[2]?.querySelector("button[data-dismiss]") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    expect(dismissed).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ key: "c" });
  });
});

describe("dm-doc-table: events", () => {
  it("reports selecting a row and selecting all, and shows what is selected", async () => {
    const el = await mount([docRow(doc(1)), docRow(doc(2))], [id(1)]);
    const row = heard(el, "dm-select"), all = heard(el, "dm-select-all");
    const boxes = qa(el, "tbody input[type=checkbox]") as HTMLInputElement[];
    expect(boxes.map((b) => b.checked)).toEqual([true, false]);
    expect(qa(el, "tbody tr")[0]?.hasAttribute("data-selected")).toBe(true);
    boxes[1]?.click();
    expect(row).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ id: id(2), on: true });
    (q(el, "thead input[type=checkbox]") as HTMLInputElement).click();
    expect(all).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ on: true });
  });
  it("select-all is checked only when every row is, and indeterminate when some are", async () => {
    const some = await mount([docRow(doc(1)), docRow(doc(2))], [id(1)]);
    const box = q<HTMLInputElement>(some, "thead input[type=checkbox]");
    expect([box.checked, box.indeterminate]).toEqual([false, true]);
    const every = await mount([docRow(doc(1)), docRow(doc(2))], [id(1), id(2)]);
    expect([q<HTMLInputElement>(every, "thead input[type=checkbox]").checked, q<HTMLInputElement>(every, "thead input[type=checkbox]").indeterminate]).toEqual([true, false]);
  });
  it("reports Retry and Delete with the document's id", async () => {
    const el = await mount([docRow(doc(1, { status: "failed", error: "x" }))]);
    const retry = heard(el, "dm-retry"), del = heard(el, "dm-delete");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-retry]").click();
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-delete]").click();
    expect(retry).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ id: id(1) });
    expect(del).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ id: id(1) });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

`state/format.ts`:

```ts
const UNITS = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB"] as const;

/** "512 B", "1.5 KB", "3.2 MB": one decimal from a kilobyte up. */
export function formatBytes(n: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return "0 B";
  let i = 0;
  let v = n;
  while (v >= 1024 && i < UNITS.length - 1) {
    v /= 1024;
    i++;
  }
  return i === 0 ? `${Math.floor(v)} B` : `${v.toFixed(1)} ${UNITS[i]}`;
}
```

`components/dm-doc-table.ts`:

```ts
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, css, html, nothing } from "lit";
import { formatBytes } from "../state/format";
import type { UploadItem, UploadReason } from "../state/documents";
import { relativeTime } from "../state/time";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import { fileKind } from "./dm-file-tile";
import "./dm-file-tile";
import "./dm-icon";
import "./dm-status-badge";

export type DocRow = { kind: "doc"; doc: DocumentDto } | { kind: "upload"; item: UploadItem };

const REASONS: Record<UploadReason, string> = {
  too_large: "Too large (50 MB limit).",
  unsupported: "File type not supported.",
  quota: "Your storage or document limit is reached.",
  network: "Network error. Try again.",
  server: "Upload failed. Try again.",
};
export const uploadReasonText = (r: UploadReason): string => REASONS[r];

export class DmDocTable extends LitElement {
  static properties = { rows: { attribute: false }, selected: { attribute: false }, now: { attribute: false } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; }
      table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; }
      th { text-align: left; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 400; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.08em; color: var(--dm-muted); padding: 8px 6px; }
      td { padding: 8px 6px; font-size: 13px; vertical-align: middle; }
      td.muted { color: var(--dm-muted); }
      tbody tr { border-top: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); }
      tbody tr:hover { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--dm-text) 4%, transparent); }
      tbody tr[data-selected] { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--dm-accent) 9%, transparent); }
      .name { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; min-width: 0; }
      .name a { color: inherit; font-weight: 500; text-decoration: none; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
      .name a:hover { text-decoration: underline; }
      [data-error] { display: block; font-size: 12px; color: var(--dm-err); }
      input[type="checkbox"] { accent-color: var(--dm-accent); width: 16px; height: 16px; }
      .status { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
      .w { width: 36px; }
    `,
  ];
  declare rows: DocRow[];
  declare selected: ReadonlySet<string>;
  declare now: Date;
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.rows = [];
    this.selected = new Set();
    this.now = new Date();
  }
  private fire(name: string, detail: unknown) {
    this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(name, { detail, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
  }
  private docRow(d: DocumentDto) {
    const on = this.selected.has(d.id);
    return html`<tr ?data-selected=${on}>
      <td class="w"><input type="checkbox" aria-label=${`Select ${d.title}`} .checked=${on} @click=${(e: Event) => this.fire("dm-select", { id: d.id, on: (e.target as HTMLInputElement).checked })} /></td>
      <td><div class="name"><dm-file-tile size="md" .filename=${d.filename} .mime=${d.mime}></dm-file-tile>
        <div><a href="/documents/${d.id}">${d.title}</a>${d.status === "failed" && d.error ? html`<span data-error>${d.error}</span>` : nothing}</div></div></td>
      <td class="muted">${fileKind(d.filename, d.mime).label}</td>
      <td class="muted">${formatBytes(d.sizeBytes)}</td>
      <td class="muted">${relativeTime(d.createdAt, this.now)}</td>
      <td><div class="status"><dm-status-badge .status=${d.status}></dm-status-badge>${d.status === "failed" ? html`<button class="btn ghost" data-retry @click=${() => this.fire("dm-retry", { id: d.id })}><dm-icon name="arrow-clockwise"></dm-icon>Retry</button>` : nothing}</div></td>
      <td class="w"><button class="btn icon" data-delete aria-label=${`Delete ${d.title}`} @click=${() => this.fire("dm-delete", { id: d.id })}><dm-icon name="trash"></dm-icon></button></td>
    </tr>`;
  }
  private uploadRow(u: UploadItem) {
    return html`<tr>
      <td class="w"></td>
      <td><div class="name"><dm-file-tile size="md" .filename=${u.name}></dm-file-tile><div><span>${u.name}</span>${u.state === "failed" && u.reason ? html`<span data-error>${uploadReasonText(u.reason)}</span>` : nothing}</div></div></td>
      <td class="muted">${fileKind(u.name).label}</td>
      <td class="muted">${formatBytes(u.size)}</td>
      <td class="muted"></td>
      <td>${u.state === "uploading" ? html`<dm-status-badge uploading .percent=${u.percent}></dm-status-badge>` : u.state === "queued" ? html`<span class="muted">Waiting</span>` : html`<span class="muted">Not uploaded</span>`}</td>
      <td class="w">${u.state === "failed" ? html`<button class="btn icon" data-dismiss aria-label=${`Dismiss ${u.name}`} @click=${() => this.fire("dm-dismiss-upload", { key: u.key })}><dm-icon name="x"></dm-icon></button>` : nothing}</td>
    </tr>`;
  }
  render() {
    const ids = this.rows.flatMap((r) => (r.kind === "doc" ? [r.doc.id] : []));
    const count = ids.filter((i) => this.selected.has(i)).length;
    return html`<table>
      <caption class="sr-only">Documents</caption>
      <thead><tr>
        <th scope="col" class="w"><input type="checkbox" aria-label="Select all documents" .checked=${ids.length > 0 && count === ids.length} .indeterminate=${count > 0 && count < ids.length} @click=${(e: Event) => this.fire("dm-select-all", { on: (e.target as HTMLInputElement).checked })} /></th>
        <th scope="col">Name</th><th scope="col">Type</th><th scope="col">Size</th><th scope="col">Uploaded</th><th scope="col">Status</th><th scope="col">Actions</th>
      </tr></thead>
      <tbody>${this.rows.map((r) => (r.kind === "doc" ? this.docRow(r.doc) : this.uploadRow(r.item)))}</tbody>
    </table>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-doc-table", DmDocTable);
```

  (The header test expects the first `th` text to be `""`: the checkbox has no text. The "Actions" header is visible text; if the design prefers it hidden, give it the `sr-only` class instead and the test still reads "Actions".)

### Task 196b: `dm-documents-page`: the library

**Spec:** §9 item 43 ("Document library table"), `ui-mocks.md` §1 (Library states), §2 Library layout, §4 Table toolbar, Bulk bar, Dialogs, Toasts, §6 deviations 3 and 4. Decisions D5, D7.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-documents-page.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/views.ts` (`documents: "dm-documents-page"`), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-documents-page.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DocumentsStore` (193), `SessionsStore.create` (185a), `<dm-doc-table>` (196a), `<dm-upload-zone>` (195), `<dm-dialog>` (184a), `fileKind`, `statusLabel`.
- Produces: `<dm-documents-page>` (route `documents`). Title "Documents" with the subtitle "N documents" or "N documents, M processing" (processing = Queued, Parsing or Indexing); an Upload button that opens the zone's file chooser; the upload zone (the card variant while the library is empty and loaded, the row variant otherwise); a toolbar with a search box ("Search documents": title or filename), a Type select ("All types" or one of the types present) and a Status select; the table (uploads first, then the documents the filters leave); a bulk bar ("N selected", Chat with selected, Delete, Clear) while something is selected; a delete confirmation naming the count; Retry; Load more. It starts the live progress stream when it appears and stops it when it leaves, and keeps a dropped file from navigating the browser away.
  Messages (fixed): upload done: "N file(s) uploaded." and "N already in your library."; delete done: "N document(s) deleted."; failures: "Could not delete N document(s)."; "Could not retry that document."; chat with selected over 100: "A chat can search at most 100 documents."; "Could not start the chat.".

```ts
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { SseOptions } from "../api/sse";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { DocumentsStore } from "../state/documents";
import { SessionsStore } from "../state/sessions";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import "./dm-documents-page";

const id = (n: number) => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const doc = (n: number, over: Partial<DocumentDto> = {}): DocumentDto => ({
  id: id(n), title: `Report ${n}`, filename: `r${n}.pdf`, mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 2048, status: "ready", error: null, stats: null, summary: null,
  createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(), ...over,
});

async function setup(docs: DocumentDto[] = [doc(1), doc(2, { filename: "n.md", mime: "text/markdown", title: "Notes" }), doc(3, { status: "embedding" })], nextCursor: string | null = null) {
  const sse: { closes: number; opened: number } = { closes: 0, opened: 0 };
  const api = {
    listDocuments: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: docs, nextCursor })),
    getDocument: vi.fn(), deleteDocument: vi.fn(async () => {}), retryDocument: vi.fn(async (i: string) => doc(Number(i.slice(-2)), { status: "uploaded" })),
    uploadDocument: vi.fn(async (f: File) => ({ document: doc(9, { title: f.name, filename: f.name }), duplicate: false })),
    createChat: vi.fn(async (req: { scope: unknown }) => ({ id: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10", title: null, scope: req.scope, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", lastMessageAt: null, messageCount: 0 })),
  };
  const open = vi.fn((_o: SseOptions) => { sse.opened++; return { close: () => { sse.closes++; } }; });
  const documents = new DocumentsStore(api as never, open as never);
  const services = createServices({ api: api as never, documents, sessions: new SessionsStore(api as never), open: open as never, fetch: (async () => new Response("{}")) as never });
  const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-documents-page");
  await vi.waitFor(() => expect(documents.get().loaded).toBe(true));
  await (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;
  return { el, api, documents, services, sse };
}
const root = (el: Element) => el.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot;
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => root(el).querySelector(sel) as T;
const qa = (el: Element, sel: string) => [...root(el).querySelectorAll(sel)];
const settle = (el: Element) => (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;
const table = (el: Element) => q<HTMLElement & { rows: { kind: string; doc?: DocumentDto }[]; updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }>(el, "dm-doc-table");
const rowTitles = (el: Element) => table(el).rows.filter((r) => r.kind === "doc").map((r) => r.doc?.title);
const fire = (target: Element, name: string, detail: unknown) => target.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(name, { detail, bubbles: true, composed: true }));

describe("dm-documents-page: the list", () => {
  it("shows the documents, a subtitle that counts what is processing, and starts the live stream", async () => {
    const { el, sse } = await setup();
    expect(q(el, "h1").textContent).toBe("Documents");
    expect(q(el, "[data-subtitle]").textContent).toBe("3 documents, 1 processing");
    expect(rowTitles(el)).toEqual(["Report 1", "Notes", "Report 3"]);
    expect(sse.opened).toBe(1);
    el.remove();
    expect(sse.closes).toBe(1);
  });

  it("an empty library shows the invitation card instead of a table", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup([]);
    expect((q(el, "dm-upload-zone") as HTMLElement & { variant: string }).variant).toBe("empty");
    expect(q(el, "dm-doc-table")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("says when the list could not be loaded, and tries again on request", async () => {
    const { el, api, documents } = await setup();
    api.listDocuments.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(500, "internal"));
    await documents.load();
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[role=alert]").textContent).toContain("Could not load your documents.");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "[role=alert] button").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(documents.get().error).toBe(false));
  });

  it("loads more when the library has more", async () => {
    const { el, api } = await setup([doc(1)], "c1");
    api.listDocuments.mockResolvedValueOnce({ items: [doc(2)], nextCursor: null });
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-more]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.listDocuments).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ cursor: "c1" }));
  });
});

describe("dm-documents-page: filters", () => {
  it("narrows by search text, type and status, and says when nothing matches", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup();
    const search = q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[aria-label='Search documents']");
    search.value = "notes";
    search.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    await settle(el);
    expect(rowTitles(el)).toEqual(["Notes"]);
    search.value = "";
    search.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    const type = q<HTMLSelectElement>(el, "select[aria-label='Type']");
    expect([...type.options].map((o) => o.textContent)).toEqual(["All types", "MD", "PDF"]);
    type.value = "PDF";
    type.dispatchEvent(new Event("change"));
    await settle(el);
    expect(rowTitles(el)).toEqual(["Report 1", "Report 3"]);
    const status = q<HTMLSelectElement>(el, "select[aria-label='Status']");
    status.value = "embedding";
    status.dispatchEvent(new Event("change"));
    await settle(el);
    expect(rowTitles(el)).toEqual(["Report 3"]);
    search.value = "zzz";
    search.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[data-no-match]").textContent).toContain("No documents match.");
  });

  it("with a filter on and more to load, says it is filtering what is loaded", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup([doc(1)], "c1");
    const search = q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[aria-label='Search documents']");
    search.value = "x";
    search.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[data-partial]").textContent).toContain("Only the documents loaded so far are searched.");
  });
});

describe("dm-documents-page: uploading", () => {
  it("uploads the files the zone reports and says how it went", async () => {
    const { el, api, services } = await setup();
    fire(q(el, "dm-upload-zone"), "dm-files", { files: [new File(["a"], "a.pdf"), new File(["b"], "b.pdf")] });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.uploadDocument).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.toasts.get().some((t) => t.text === "2 files uploaded.")).toBe(true));
  });
  it("counts duplicates apart, and shows an upload that failed in the table", async () => {
    const { el, api, services } = await setup();
    api.uploadDocument.mockResolvedValueOnce({ document: doc(1), duplicate: true }).mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(415, "unsupported_type"));
    fire(q(el, "dm-upload-zone"), "dm-files", { files: [new File(["a"], "a.pdf"), new File(["b"], "b.pdf")] });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.toasts.get().some((t) => t.text === "1 already in your library.")).toBe(true));
    await settle(el);
    expect(table(el).rows.some((r) => r.kind === "upload")).toBe(true);
  });
  it("the Upload button opens the zone's chooser", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup();
    const zone = q<HTMLElement & { browse: () => void }>(el, "dm-upload-zone");
    zone.browse = vi.fn();
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-upload]").click();
    expect(zone.browse).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
  it("keeps a file dropped outside the zone from navigating the browser away, and stops doing so when it leaves", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup();
    const over = new Event("dragover", { cancelable: true });
    window.dispatchEvent(over);
    expect(over.defaultPrevented).toBe(true);
    el.remove();
    const after = new Event("dragover", { cancelable: true });
    window.dispatchEvent(after);
    expect(after.defaultPrevented).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("dm-documents-page: selection, chat and delete", () => {
  it("selecting shows a bulk bar with the count, and Clear removes it", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup();
    expect(q(el, "[data-bulk]")).toBeNull();
    fire(table(el), "dm-select", { id: id(1), on: true });
    fire(table(el), "dm-select", { id: id(2), on: true });
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[data-bulk]").textContent).toContain("2 selected");
    fire(table(el), "dm-select-all", { on: true });
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[data-bulk]").textContent).toContain("3 selected");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "[data-bulk] button[data-clear]").click();
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[data-bulk]")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("select-all takes only the rows the filters leave", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup();
    const search = q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[aria-label='Search documents']");
    search.value = "notes";
    search.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    await settle(el);
    fire(table(el), "dm-select-all", { on: true });
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[data-bulk]").textContent).toContain("1 selected");
  });
  it("Chat with selected creates a chat scoped to them and opens it", async () => {
    const { el, api, services } = await setup();
    fire(table(el), "dm-select", { id: id(1), on: true });
    fire(table(el), "dm-select", { id: id(2), on: true });
    await settle(el);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-chat]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.createChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ scope: { documentIds: [id(1), id(2)] } }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "chat", chatId: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10" }));
  });
  it("refuses a chat over more than a hundred documents, and says so when the chat cannot be made", async () => {
    const many = Array.from({ length: 101 }, (_, n) => doc(n + 1));
    const big = await setup(many);
    fire(table(big.el), "dm-select-all", { on: true });
    await settle(big.el);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(big.el, "button[data-chat]").click();
    expect(big.api.createChat).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(big.services.toasts.get().at(-1)?.text).toBe("A chat can search at most 100 documents.");
    const { el, api, services } = await setup();
    api.createChat.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(503, "chat_unavailable"));
    fire(table(el), "dm-select", { id: id(1), on: true });
    await settle(el);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-chat]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.toasts.get().at(-1)?.text).toBe("Could not start the chat."));
  });
  it("asks before deleting, names the count, and deletes only after the confirmation", async () => {
    const { el, api, documents } = await setup();
    fire(table(el), "dm-delete", { id: id(1) });
    await settle(el);
    const dlg = q<HTMLElement & { open: boolean }>(el, "dm-dialog");
    expect(dlg.open).toBe(true);
    expect((dlg as unknown as { heading: string }).heading).toBe("Delete 1 document?");
    expect(api.deleteDocument).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    (dlg.querySelector("button[data-cancel]") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await settle(el);
    expect(dlg.open).toBe(false);
    fire(table(el), "dm-select", { id: id(1), on: true });
    fire(table(el), "dm-select", { id: id(2), on: true });
    await settle(el);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-bulk-delete]").click();
    await settle(el);
    expect((dlg as unknown as { heading: string }).heading).toBe("Delete 2 documents?");
    (dlg.querySelector("button[data-confirm]") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.deleteDocument).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(documents.get().items.map((d) => d.id)).toEqual([id(3)]));
    expect(q(el, "[data-bulk]")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("reports the deletes that failed, and keeps those documents listed and selected", async () => {
    const { el, api, services, documents } = await setup();
    api.deleteDocument.mockResolvedValueOnce(undefined).mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(500, "internal"));
    fire(table(el), "dm-select", { id: id(1), on: true });
    fire(table(el), "dm-select", { id: id(2), on: true });
    await settle(el);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-bulk-delete]").click();
    await settle(el);
    (q(el, "dm-dialog").querySelector("button[data-confirm]") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.toasts.get().some((t) => t.text === "Could not delete 1 document.")).toBe(true));
    expect(services.toasts.get().some((t) => t.text === "1 document deleted.")).toBe(true);
    expect(documents.get().items.map((d) => d.id)).toEqual([id(2), id(3)]);
    expect(q(el, "[data-bulk]").textContent).toContain("1 selected");
  });
  it("retries a failed document, and says so when it cannot", async () => {
    const { el, api, services } = await setup([doc(1, { status: "failed", error: "x" })]);
    fire(table(el), "dm-retry", { id: id(1) });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.retryDocument).toHaveBeenCalledWith(id(1)));
    api.retryDocument.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(409, "not_failed"));
    fire(table(el), "dm-retry", { id: id(1) });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.toasts.get().at(-1)?.text).toBe("Could not retry that document."));
  });
});
```
