- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `dm-documents-page.ts`.**

```ts
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, css, html, nothing } from "lit";
import { statusLabel } from "../state/documents";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import type { DmUploadZone } from "./dm-upload-zone";
import { fileKind } from "./dm-file-tile";
import type { DocRow } from "./dm-doc-table";
import "./dm-dialog";
import "./dm-doc-table";
import "./dm-icon";
import "./dm-upload-zone";

const MAX_SCOPE = 100;
const plural = (n: number, one: string, many: string) => `${n} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;
const PROCESSING = new Set<DocumentDto["status"]>(["uploaded", "parsing", "embedding"]);
const preventNavigation = (e: Event) => e.preventDefault();

export class DmDocumentsPage extends LitElement {
  static properties = { query: { state: true }, type: { state: true }, status: { state: true }, picked: { state: true }, confirm: { state: true }, busy: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; padding: 28px 40px; overflow: auto; height: 100%; }
      header { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 16px; }
      h1 { font-size: 26px; }
      [data-subtitle] { color: var(--dm-muted); font-size: 13px; }
      .toolbar { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 12px; margin: 18px 0 8px; }
      .toolbar input[type="search"] { flex: 0 1 280px; min-height: 34px; padding: 0 10px; font: inherit; font-size: 13px; color: var(--dm-text); background: transparent; border: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); }
      select { min-height: 34px; padding: 0 8px; font: inherit; font-size: 13px; color: var(--dm-text); background: var(--dm-surface); border: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); }
      [data-bulk] { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; padding: 8px 12px; margin: 8px 0; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); background: var(--dm-surface); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-md); }
      [data-bulk] .grow { flex: 1; }
      .note { color: var(--dm-muted); font-size: 12.5px; margin: 8px 0; }
      .more { margin-top: 12px; }
      dm-upload-zone { margin-top: 18px; }
    `,
  ];
  declare query: string;
  declare type: string;
  declare status: string;
  declare picked: ReadonlySet<string>;
  declare confirm: string[] | undefined;
  declare busy: boolean;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.documents]);
  private started = false;
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.query = "";
    this.type = "";
    this.status = "";
    this.picked = new Set();
    this.busy = false;
  }
  connectedCallback() {
    super.connectedCallback();
    window.addEventListener("dragover", preventNavigation);
    window.addEventListener("drop", preventNavigation);
  }
  disconnectedCallback() {
    window.removeEventListener("dragover", preventNavigation);
    window.removeEventListener("drop", preventNavigation);
    this.ctx.services?.documents.stopEvents();
    this.started = false;
    super.disconnectedCallback();
  }
  protected updated() {
    // The services arrive through the context, possibly after the first render: start the stream and the list once they have.
    const documents = this.ctx.services?.documents;
    if (documents && !this.started) {
      this.started = true;
      documents.startEvents();
      if (!documents.get().loaded) void documents.load();
    }
  }

  private visible(items: DocumentDto[]): DocumentDto[] {
    const needle = this.query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return items.filter(
      (d) =>
        (needle === "" || d.title.toLowerCase().includes(needle) || d.filename.toLowerCase().includes(needle)) &&
        (this.type === "" || fileKind(d.filename, d.mime).label === this.type) &&
        (this.status === "" || d.status === this.status),
    );
  }
  private async upload(files: File[]) {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc) return;
    const results = await svc.documents.upload(files);
    const added = results.filter((r) => r.document && !r.duplicate).length;
    const dupes = results.filter((r) => r.duplicate).length;
    if (added > 0) svc.toasts.push({ kind: "ok", text: `${plural(added, "file", "files")} uploaded.` });
    if (dupes > 0) svc.toasts.push({ kind: "ok", text: `${dupes} already in your library.` });
  }
  private select(id: string, on: boolean) {
    const next = new Set(this.picked);
    if (on) next.add(id);
    else next.delete(id);
    this.picked = next;
  }
  private async chat() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const ids = [...this.picked];
    if (!svc || ids.length === 0) return;
    if (ids.length > MAX_SCOPE) return void svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "A chat can search at most 100 documents." });
    try {
      const made = await svc.sessions.create({ documentIds: ids });
      svc.router.navigate({ name: "chat", chatId: made.id });
    } catch {
      svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not start the chat." });
    }
  }
  private async doDelete() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const ids = this.confirm ?? [];
    if (!svc || this.busy) return;
    this.busy = true;
    const settled = await Promise.allSettled(ids.map((id) => svc.documents.remove(id)));
    const failed = ids.filter((_, i) => settled[i]?.status === "rejected");
    const done = ids.length - failed.length;
    if (done > 0) svc.toasts.push({ kind: "ok", text: `${plural(done, "document", "documents")} deleted.` });
    if (failed.length > 0) svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: `Could not delete ${plural(failed.length, "document", "documents")}.` });
    this.picked = new Set([...this.picked].filter((id) => failed.includes(id)));
    this.confirm = undefined;
    this.busy = false;
  }
  private async retry(id: string) {
    try {
      await this.ctx.services?.documents.retry(id);
    } catch {
      this.ctx.services?.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not retry that document." });
    }
  }

  render() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const state = svc?.documents.get();
    if (!svc || !state) return html``;
    const shown = this.visible(state.items);
    const processing = state.items.filter((d) => PROCESSING.has(d.status)).length;
    const empty = state.loaded && state.items.length === 0 && state.uploads.length === 0;
    const types = [...new Set(state.items.map((d) => fileKind(d.filename, d.mime).label))].sort();
    const rows: DocRow[] = [...state.uploads.map((item) => ({ kind: "upload" as const, item })), ...shown.map((doc) => ({ kind: "doc" as const, doc }))];
    const filtering = this.query.trim() !== "" || this.type !== "" || this.status !== "";
    const count = this.picked.size;
    const n = this.confirm?.length ?? 0;
    const one = n === 1;
    return html`
      <header><div><h1>Documents</h1><div data-subtitle>${plural(state.items.length, "document", "documents")}${processing > 0 ? `, ${processing} processing` : ""}</div></div>
        <button class="btn primary" data-upload @click=${() => this.shadowRoot?.querySelector<DmUploadZone>("dm-upload-zone")?.browse()}><dm-icon name="plus"></dm-icon>Upload</button></header>
      <dm-upload-zone .variant=${empty ? "empty" : "compact"} @dm-files=${(e: CustomEvent<{ files: File[] }>) => void this.upload(e.detail.files)}></dm-upload-zone>
      ${state.error ? html`<div class="error" role="alert">Could not load your documents. <button class="btn secondary" @click=${() => void svc.documents.load()}>Try again</button></div>` : nothing}
      ${empty
        ? nothing
        : html`<div class="toolbar">
              <input type="search" aria-label="Search documents" placeholder="Search documents" .value=${this.query} @input=${(e: Event) => (this.query = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value)} />
              <select aria-label="Type" .value=${this.type} @change=${(e: Event) => (this.type = (e.target as HTMLSelectElement).value)}><option value="">All types</option>${types.map((t) => html`<option value=${t}>${t}</option>`)}</select>
              <select aria-label="Status" .value=${this.status} @change=${(e: Event) => (this.status = (e.target as HTMLSelectElement).value)}><option value="">All statuses</option>${(["uploaded", "parsing", "embedding", "ready", "failed"] as const).map((s) => html`<option value=${s}>${statusLabel(s)}</option>`)}</select>
            </div>
            ${count > 0
              ? html`<div data-bulk role="region" aria-label="Selection actions"><span class="grow">${count} selected</span>
                  <button class="btn primary" data-chat @click=${() => void this.chat()}><dm-icon name="chat-circle-text"></dm-icon>Chat with selected</button>
                  <button class="btn secondary" data-bulk-delete @click=${() => (this.confirm = [...this.picked])}><dm-icon name="trash"></dm-icon>Delete</button>
                  <button class="btn ghost" data-clear @click=${() => (this.picked = new Set())}>Clear</button></div>`
              : nothing}
            ${rows.length > 0
              ? html`<dm-doc-table .rows=${rows} .selected=${this.picked} .now=${new Date()}
                  @dm-select=${(e: CustomEvent<{ id: string; on: boolean }>) => this.select(e.detail.id, e.detail.on)}
                  @dm-select-all=${(e: CustomEvent<{ on: boolean }>) => (this.picked = e.detail.on ? new Set(shown.map((d) => d.id)) : new Set())}
                  @dm-retry=${(e: CustomEvent<{ id: string }>) => void this.retry(e.detail.id)}
                  @dm-delete=${(e: CustomEvent<{ id: string }>) => (this.confirm = [e.detail.id])}
                  @dm-dismiss-upload=${(e: CustomEvent<{ key: string }>) => svc.documents.dismissUpload(e.detail.key)}></dm-doc-table>`
              : state.loaded && filtering
                ? html`<p class="note" data-no-match>No documents match.</p>`
                : nothing}
            ${filtering && state.nextCursor ? html`<p class="note" data-partial>Only the documents loaded so far are searched. Load more to search further.</p>` : nothing}
            ${state.nextCursor ? html`<div class="more"><button class="btn secondary" data-more ?disabled=${state.loading} @click=${() => void svc.documents.loadMore()}>Load more</button></div>` : nothing}`}
      <dm-dialog heading=${`Delete ${plural(n, "document", "documents")}?`} ?open=${this.confirm !== undefined} persistent @dm-close=${() => (this.confirm = undefined)}>
        <p>This removes ${one ? "the document, its summary and its passages" : "the documents, their summaries and their passages"}. Chats that used ${one ? "it" : "them"} can no longer cite ${one ? "it" : "them"}. This cannot be undone.</p>
        <button slot="actions" class="btn secondary" data-cancel @click=${() => (this.confirm = undefined)}>Cancel</button>
        <button slot="actions" class="btn danger" data-confirm ?disabled=${this.busy} @click=${() => void this.doDelete()}>Delete</button>
      </dm-dialog>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-documents-page", DmDocumentsPage);
```

  `views.ts`: `documents: "dm-documents-page"`. `components/index.ts`: `import "./dm-documents-page";`.

### Task 197: server: `GET /api/documents/:id/content`

**Spec:** §9 item 44 (document detail: Summary / Analysis / Content tabs), §7 (`dm-doc-detail`), §6.3 ("Open in document" needs the passage), Finding U9. Open question 5 (passages, not a persisted full text).

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/contracts/src/documents.ts` (`DocumentPassage`, `DocumentContentResponse`), `apps/server/app/documents/routes.ts` (the route), `apps/web/src/api/client.ts` (`documentContent`)
- Create: `apps/server/app/documents/content.ts`
- Test: `packages/contracts/src/documents.test.ts`, `apps/server/app/documents/content.test.ts`, `apps/server/app/documents/content.pg.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/api/client.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (contract): `DocumentPassage {passageId: Uuid; ordinal: int >= 0; locator: Locator; headingPath: string[]; text: string}` and `DocumentContentResponse {items: DocumentPassage[]; nextCursor: Cursor | null}`.
- Produces (server): `CONTENT_PAGE = 20`; `encodeContentCursor(ordinal: number): string` and `decodeContentCursor(cursor: string): number | null` (an opaque base64url of `o:<ordinal>`, decoded strictly: a non-negative integer of at most nine digits, else `null`); `readContent(pool, fields, identity, documentId, after): Promise<DocumentContentResponse | null>` (`after` is the last ordinal already seen, -1 for the start; `null` when the document does not exist or is not the caller's); the route `GET /api/documents/:id/content?cursor`: 200 with the page, 404 `not_found` for a malformed, unknown or foreign id (the same answer), 400 `invalid_cursor`, 401 without a session.
- Produces (web): `Api.documentContent(id: string, cursor?: string): Promise<DocumentContentResponse>`.
- Chunks overlap by about 15%, so consecutive passages repeat a little text; the Content tab (Task 199) shows passages, not a stitched text.

```ts
describe("document content contract", () => {
  const passage = { passageId: ID, ordinal: 0, locator: { kind: "page", page: 1 }, headingPath: ["Terms"], text: "Payment is due in 30 days." };
  it("accepts a page of passages and a null cursor", () => {
    expect(DocumentContentResponse.parse({ items: [passage], nextCursor: null })).toEqual({ items: [passage], nextCursor: null });
  });
  it("refuses a passage with a bad id, a negative ordinal or a locator of an unknown kind", () => {
    for (const bad of [{ ...passage, passageId: "x" }, { ...passage, ordinal: -1 }, { ...passage, locator: { kind: "line", n: 1 } }])
      expect(DocumentContentResponse.safeParse({ items: [bad], nextCursor: null }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { decodeContentCursor, encodeContentCursor } from "./content";

describe("the content cursor", () => {
  it("round-trips an ordinal", () => {
    for (const n of [0, 1, 19, 20, 12345]) expect(decodeContentCursor(encodeContentCursor(n))).toBe(n);
  });
  it.each(["", "!!!", "bm90IGEgY3Vyc29y", Buffer.from("o:-1").toString("base64url"), Buffer.from("o:01").toString("base64url"), Buffer.from("o:1.5").toString("base64url"), Buffer.from("o:1234567890").toString("base64url"), Buffer.from("x:5").toString("base64url")])("refuses %j", (c) => {
    expect(decodeContentCursor(c)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`content.pg.test.ts` (uses the document harness; the document row comes from `seedDoc`, the chunks from `PgChunkStore`, as the passage reader's tests do):

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { DocumentContentResponse } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { FieldCrypto } from "../crypto/fields";
import { PG_URL } from "../db/testSupport";
import { type Chunk, chunkIdFor } from "../flows/ingest-document/chunker";
import { PgChunkStore } from "../flows/ingest-document/pgChunks";
import { ALICE, ALICE2, BOB, type DocumentHarness, seedDoc, startDocumentServer } from "./testHarness";
import { CONTENT_PAGE } from "./content";

describe.skipIf(!PG_URL)("document content (needs DOCMIND_TEST_PG_URL)", () => {
  let h: DocumentHarness;
  let fields: FieldCrypto;
  let mine: string, empty: string, theirs: string, bobs: string;
  const chunk = (n: number): Chunk => ({ ordinal: n, sectionOrdinal: Math.floor(n / 10), headingPath: ["Terms", `Clause ${n}`], text: `sentinel-clause-${n}-text-0123456789`, tokenCount: 8, locator: { kind: "page", page: n + 1 } });
  const get = (path: string, user = "alice") => fetch(`${h.base}${path}`, { headers: user ? { "x-test-user": user } : {} });

  beforeAll(async () => {
    h = await startDocumentServer();
    fields = new FieldCrypto(h.keys);
    const seed = async (who: typeof ALICE, fp: string, n: number) => {
      const id = await seedDoc(h.owner, h.app, fields, who, fp.repeat(64), 10);
      await new PgChunkStore(h.app, fields).replace({ tenantId: who.tenantId, userId: who.userId, documentId: id }, Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => chunk(i)));
      return id;
    };
    mine = await seed(ALICE, "a", 45);
    empty = await seed(ALICE, "b", 0);
    theirs = await seed(ALICE2, "c", 3);
    bobs = await seed(BOB, "d", 3);
  });
  afterAll(async () => { await h?.close(); });

  it("pages through every passage in reading order, 20 at a time, with no gaps or repeats", async () => {
    const seen: number[] = [];
    let cursor: string | null = null;
    let pages = 0;
    do {
      const res = await get(`/api/documents/${mine}/content${cursor ? `?cursor=${cursor}` : ""}`);
      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      const page = DocumentContentResponse.parse(await res.json());
      expect(page.items.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(CONTENT_PAGE);
      seen.push(...page.items.map((p) => p.ordinal));
      for (const p of page.items) expect(p.passageId).toBe(chunkIdFor(mine, p.ordinal));
      cursor = page.nextCursor;
      pages++;
    } while (cursor);
    expect(pages).toBe(3);
    expect(seen).toEqual(Array.from({ length: 45 }, (_, i) => i));
  });

  it("returns the decrypted text, heading path and locator of a passage", async () => {
    const page = DocumentContentResponse.parse(await (await get(`/api/documents/${mine}/content`)).json());
    expect(page.items[3]).toMatchObject({ ordinal: 3, headingPath: ["Terms", "Clause 3"], text: "sentinel-clause-3-text-0123456789", locator: { kind: "page", page: 4 } });
  });

  it("keeps the text sealed at rest", async () => {
    const { rows } = await h.owner.query("SELECT text_enc::text AS raw FROM chunks WHERE document_id = $1 AND ordinal = 3", [mine]);
    expect(rows[0].raw).toContain("__loomEnc");
    expect(rows[0].raw).not.toContain("sentinel-clause-3");
  });

  it("answers an empty document with an empty page", async () => {
    expect(await (await get(`/api/documents/${empty}/content`)).json()).toEqual({ items: [], nextCursor: null });
  });

  it("answers 404 alike for an unknown id, a malformed id, a colleague's document and another tenant's", async () => {
    const answers = await Promise.all([randomUUID(), "not-a-uuid", theirs, bobs].map(async (id) => { const res = await get(`/api/documents/${id}/content`); return [res.status, await res.json()]; }));
    expect(new Set(answers.map((a) => JSON.stringify(a))).size).toBe(1);
    expect(answers[0]?.[0]).toBe(404);
  });

  it("refuses a bad cursor with 400 and no session with 401", async () => {
    for (const c of ["x", "!!!", Buffer.from("o:-1").toString("base64url")]) {
      const res = await get(`/api/documents/${mine}/content?cursor=${c}`);
      expect(res.status).toBe(400);
      expect(((await res.json()) as { error: { code: string } }).error.code).toBe("invalid_cursor");
    }
    expect((await get(`/api/documents/${mine}/content`, "")).status).toBe(401);
  });
});
```

`packages/contracts/src/documents.ts` (append):

```ts
/** One passage (chunk) of a document in reading order, for the Content tab. Passages overlap by about 15%. */
export const DocumentPassage = z.object({
  passageId: Uuid,
  ordinal: Count,
  locator: Locator,
  headingPath: z.array(z.string()),
  text: z.string(),
});
export type DocumentPassage = z.infer<typeof DocumentPassage>;

/** `GET /api/documents/:id/content?cursor`: 20 passages per page; `nextCursor` is null on the last page. */
export const DocumentContentResponse = z.object({
  items: z.array(DocumentPassage),
  nextCursor: Cursor.nullable(),
});
export type DocumentContentResponse = z.infer<typeof DocumentContentResponse>;
```

`apps/server/app/documents/content.ts`:

```ts
import type { Identity } from "@docmind/auth";
import { type DocumentContentResponse, Locator } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { Pool } from "pg";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { FieldCrypto } from "../crypto/fields";
import { withTenant } from "../db/tenant";
import { chunkBind } from "../flows/ingest-document/pgChunks";
import { getDocument } from "./repo";

/** Passages per page of `GET /api/documents/:id/content`. */
export const CONTENT_PAGE = 20;
const SEALED = z.object({ text: z.string(), headingPath: z.array(z.string()) });

export const encodeContentCursor = (ordinal: number): string => Buffer.from(`o:${ordinal}`).toString("base64url");

/** The ordinal a cursor names, or null: opaque to clients, validated like any input (no sign, no leading zero, nine digits at most). */
export function decodeContentCursor(cursor: string): number | null {
  const match = /^o:(0|[1-9][0-9]{0,8})$/.exec(Buffer.from(cursor, "base64url").toString("utf8"));
  return match ? Number(match[1]) : null;
}

interface Row {
  id: string;
  ordinal: number;
  locator: unknown;
  text_enc: unknown;
}

/**
 * The passages of one of the caller's documents after ordinal `after`, in reading order, with text opened (content: never
 * logged). `null` when the document does not exist or is not the caller's. Owner-scoped inside `withTenant`.
 */
export async function readContent(
  pool: Pool,
  fields: FieldCrypto,
  identity: Identity,
  documentId: string,
  after: number,
): Promise<DocumentContentResponse | null> {
  const rows = await withTenant(pool, identity.tenantId, async (tx) => {
    if (!(await getDocument(tx, identity.userId, documentId))) return null;
    const res = await tx.query<Row>(
      `SELECT c.id, c.ordinal, c.locator, c.text_enc
         FROM chunks c JOIN documents d ON d.id = c.document_id AND d.tenant_id = c.tenant_id
        WHERE d.id = $1 AND d.owner_id = $2 AND c.ordinal > $3
        ORDER BY c.ordinal LIMIT $4`,
      [documentId, identity.userId, after, CONTENT_PAGE + 1],
    );
    return res.rows;
  });
  if (rows === null) return null;
  const page = rows.slice(0, CONTENT_PAGE);
  const cipher = fields.forUser(identity.userId);
  const items = await Promise.all(
    page.map(async (r) => {
      const sealed = SEALED.safeParse(await cipher.open(chunkBind(r.id), r.text_enc));
      const locator = Locator.safeParse(r.locator);
      // A fixed message: a schema error could carry decrypted content.
      if (!sealed.success || !locator.success) throw new Error("the stored chunk has an invalid shape");
      return { passageId: r.id, ordinal: r.ordinal, locator: locator.data, headingPath: sealed.data.headingPath, text: sealed.data.text };
    }),
  );
  const last = page[page.length - 1];
  return { items, nextCursor: rows.length > CONTENT_PAGE && last ? encodeContentCursor(last.ordinal) : null };
}
```

`routes.ts` (inside the object returned by `documentRoutes`, after `GET /api/documents/:id`; import `DocumentContentResponse` with the other contract types and `decodeContentCursor`, `readContent` from `./content`):

```ts
    "GET /api/documents/:id/content": requireAuth(deps.authenticator, async (req, res, identity, ctx) => {
      const parsed = Uuid.safeParse(ctx.params.id);
      const raw = new URL(req.url ?? "/", "http://localhost").searchParams.get("cursor");
      const after = raw === null ? -1 : decodeContentCursor(raw);
      if (after === null) return sendError(res, 400, "invalid_cursor", "cursor is not valid");
      // A malformed id answers exactly like a missing or foreign one.
      const page = parsed.success ? await readContent(deps.pool, deps.fields, identity, parsed.data.toLowerCase(), after) : null;
      if (!page) return sendError(res, 404, "not_found", "document not found");
      json(res, 200, page satisfies DocumentContentResponse);
    }),
```

### Task 198: `dm-doc-detail`: header, tabs, Summary and Analysis

**Spec:** §9 item 44 ("Document detail tabs"), §7 (`dm-doc-detail`, Summary / Analysis / Content tabs), `ui-mocks.md` §4 Document detail tabs and §6 (no Download, no zoom: open question 5). Decisions D5, D7.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-doc-detail.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/views.ts` (`document: "dm-doc-detail"`), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-doc-detail.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DocumentsStore` (`items`, `refreshOne`, `remove`, `retry`, `startEvents`, `stopEvents`), `SessionsStore.create`, `Services.outbox`, `Router`, `<dm-file-tile>`, `<dm-status-badge>`, `<dm-dialog>`, `fileKind`, `formatBytes`.
- Produces: `<dm-doc-detail>` (route `document`). It reads the id from the route, fetches the document into the store (`refreshOne`) and draws from the store's copy, so a status change that arrives on the live stream updates the page. A document the server does not have shows "This document no longer exists." with a link to the library. Header: breadcrumb (Documents, then the title), the file tile, the title (an `h1`), a meta line (type, size, pages, words), the status badge, **Ask about this document** (creates a chat scoped to the document and opens it) and Delete (a confirmation, then back to the library). Tabs (`role="tablist"`; roving `tabindex`; Left, Right, Home and End move and select; each panel is a `tabpanel` named by its tab): **Summary** (TL;DR, key points, entities, topics, suggested questions, each a button that opens a new chat on the document and sends that question through `services.outbox`) and **Analysis** (outline, a stat grid of words, pages, reading time and language, notable dates and notable numbers). The Content tab is added by Task 199: the tab list is data (`TABS`), so that task appends one entry.
  Fixed texts: a document still processing says "This document is still being processed. Its summary and analysis appear here when it is ready."; a failed one shows its error and a Retry button; a ready one with no summary says "No summary is available for this document."; no statistics says "No analysis is available for this document yet.".

```ts
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { Router } from "../router";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { DocumentsStore } from "../state/documents";
import { SessionsStore } from "../state/sessions";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import "./dm-doc-detail";

const ID = "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11";
const CHAT = "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10";
const STATS = { wordCount: 4200, pageCount: 12, readingTimeMinutes: 18, language: "en", outline: ["Introduction", "Terms <b>and</b> conditions"], dates: ["2026-07-12"], numbers: ["30 days", "5%"] };
const SUMMARY = { tldr: "Payment is due in 30 days.", keyPoints: ["Net 30", "Late fee 5%"], entities: ["Acme <i>Corp</i>"], topics: ["Payments"], suggestedQuestions: ["When is payment due?", "What is the late fee?"] };
const doc = (over: Partial<DocumentDto> = {}): DocumentDto => ({
  id: ID, title: "Q3 <b>report</b>", filename: "q3.pdf", mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 3 * 1024 * 1024, status: "ready", error: null, stats: STATS, summary: SUMMARY,
  createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});

async function setup(first: DocumentDto | Error = doc()) {
  const api = {
    getDocument: vi.fn(async () => { if (first instanceof Error) throw first; return first; }),
    deleteDocument: vi.fn(async () => {}),
    retryDocument: vi.fn(async () => doc({ status: "uploaded", summary: null, stats: null })),
    createChat: vi.fn(async (req: { scope: unknown }) => ({ id: CHAT, title: null, scope: req.scope, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", lastMessageAt: null, messageCount: 0 })),
    listDocuments: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [], nextCursor: null })),
  };
  const sse = { opened: 0, closed: 0 };
  const open = vi.fn(() => { sse.opened++; return { close: () => { sse.closed++; } }; });
  const win = { location: { pathname: `/documents/${ID}`, search: "" }, history: { pushState: (_s: unknown, _t: string, u: string) => { win.location.pathname = u; }, replaceState: (_s: unknown, _t: string, u: string) => { win.location.pathname = u; } }, addEventListener: () => {}, removeEventListener: () => {} };
  const documents = new DocumentsStore(api as never, open as never);
  const services = createServices({ api: api as never, router: new Router(win as never), documents, sessions: new SessionsStore(api as never), open: open as never, fetch: (async () => new Response("{}")) as never });
  const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-doc-detail");
  return { el, api, services, documents, sse };
}
const root = (el: Element) => el.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot;
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => root(el).querySelector(sel) as T;
const qa = (el: Element, sel: string) => [...root(el).querySelectorAll(sel)];
const settle = (el: Element) => (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;
const loaded = async (el: Element) => { await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "h1")).not.toBeNull()); await settle(el); };
const tab = (el: Element, name: string) => q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, `[role=tab][data-tab=${name}]`);

describe("dm-doc-detail: header", () => {
  it("fetches the document, draws the title as text, and the meta, badge and breadcrumb", async () => {
    const { el, api } = await setup();
    await loaded(el);
    expect(api.getDocument).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ID);
    expect(q(el, "h1").textContent).toBe("Q3 <b>report</b>");
    expect(q(el, "h1 b")).toBeNull();
    expect(q(el, "[data-meta]").textContent).toContain("PDF");
    expect(q(el, "[data-meta]").textContent).toContain("3.0 MB");
    expect(q(el, "[data-meta]").textContent).toContain("12 pages");
    expect(q(el, "[data-meta]").textContent).toContain("4,200 words");
    expect(q(el, "dm-status-badge")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(q<HTMLAnchorElement>(el, "nav[aria-label='Breadcrumb'] a").getAttribute("href")).toBe("/documents");
  });

  it("says so when the document does not exist, and links back to the library", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup(new ApiError(404, "not_found"));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(root(el).textContent).toContain("This document no longer exists."));
    expect(q<HTMLAnchorElement>(el, "a[href='/documents']")).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("says when the document could not be loaded, and tries again on request", async () => {
    const { el, api } = await setup(new ApiError(500, "internal"));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[role=alert]")?.textContent).toContain("Could not load this document."));
    api.getDocument.mockResolvedValueOnce(doc());
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-reload]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "h1")).not.toBeNull());
  });

  it("listens to the live stream while it is on screen", async () => {
    const { el, sse } = await setup();
    await loaded(el);
    expect(sse.opened).toBe(1);
    el.remove();
    expect(sse.closed).toBe(1);
  });

  it("follows a status change from the store: a document that becomes ready shows its summary", async () => {
    const { el, documents, api } = await setup(doc({ status: "embedding", summary: null, stats: null }));
    await loaded(el);
    expect(root(el).textContent).toContain("This document is still being processed.");
    api.getDocument.mockResolvedValueOnce(doc());
    await documents.refreshOne(ID);
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[role=tabpanel]:not([hidden])").textContent).toContain("Payment is due in 30 days.");
  });
});

describe("dm-doc-detail: tabs", () => {
  it("is a labelled tablist with a roving tabindex, Summary first, and each panel named by its tab", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup();
    await loaded(el);
    const tabs = qa(el, "[role=tab]") as HTMLButtonElement[];
    expect(tabs.map((t) => t.textContent?.trim())).toEqual(["Summary", "Analysis"]);
    expect(q(el, "[role=tablist]").getAttribute("aria-label")).toBe("Document");
    expect(tabs.map((t) => [t.getAttribute("aria-selected"), t.tabIndex])).toEqual([["true", 0], ["false", -1]]);
    const panels = qa(el, "[role=tabpanel]");
    expect(panels.map((p) => p.hasAttribute("hidden"))).toEqual([false, true]);
    expect(panels[0]?.getAttribute("aria-labelledby")).toBe(tabs[0]?.id);
    expect(tabs[0]?.getAttribute("aria-controls")).toBe(panels[0]?.id);
  });
  it("arrow keys, Home and End move between tabs and select them", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup();
    await loaded(el);
    const key = (k: string) => tab(el, "summary").dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: k, bubbles: true }));
    key("ArrowRight");
    await settle(el);
    expect(tab(el, "analysis").getAttribute("aria-selected")).toBe("true");
    tab(el, "analysis").dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "ArrowRight", bubbles: true })); // wraps
    await settle(el);
    expect(tab(el, "summary").getAttribute("aria-selected")).toBe("true");
    tab(el, "summary").dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "End", bubbles: true }));
    await settle(el);
    expect(tab(el, "analysis").getAttribute("aria-selected")).toBe("true");
    tab(el, "analysis").dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "Home", bubbles: true }));
    await settle(el);
    expect(tab(el, "summary").getAttribute("aria-selected")).toBe("true");
  });
  it("a click selects a tab", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup();
    await loaded(el);
    tab(el, "analysis").click();
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "#panel-analysis").hasAttribute("hidden")).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("dm-doc-detail: summary", () => {
  it("draws the summary from text: TL;DR, key points, entities, topics, suggested questions", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup();
    await loaded(el);
    const p = q(el, "#panel-summary");
    expect(p.querySelector("[data-tldr]")?.textContent).toBe("Payment is due in 30 days.");
    expect([...p.querySelectorAll("[data-keypoint]")].map((x) => x.textContent)).toEqual(["Net 30", "Late fee 5%"]);
    expect([...p.querySelectorAll("[data-entity]")].map((x) => x.textContent)).toEqual(["Acme <i>Corp</i>"]);
    expect(p.querySelector("[data-entity] i")).toBeNull();
    expect([...p.querySelectorAll("[data-topic]")].map((x) => x.textContent)).toEqual(["Payments"]);
    expect([...p.querySelectorAll("button[data-question]")].map((x) => x.textContent?.trim())).toEqual(["When is payment due?", "What is the late fee?"]);
  });
  it("a suggested question starts a chat on this document and sends that question once the chat is open", async () => {
    const { el, api, services } = await setup();
    await loaded(el);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-question]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.createChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ scope: { documentIds: [ID] } }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "chat", chatId: CHAT }));
    expect(services.outbox.get()).toEqual({ chatId: CHAT, text: "When is payment due?" });
  });
  it("Ask about this document opens a chat on it, with nothing to send", async () => {
    const { el, api, services } = await setup();
    await loaded(el);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-ask]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.createChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ scope: { documentIds: [ID] } }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "chat", chatId: CHAT }));
    expect(services.outbox.get()).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it("says why there is no chat when it cannot be made", async () => {
    const { el, api, services } = await setup();
    await loaded(el);
    api.createChat.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(503, "chat_unavailable"));
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-ask]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.toasts.get().at(-1)?.text).toBe("Could not start the chat."));
  });
  it.each([
    [{ status: "failed" as const, error: "Could not open the file.", summary: null, stats: null }, "Could not open the file."],
    [{ summary: null }, "No summary is available for this document."],
  ])("states of a document without a summary (%o)", async (over, text) => {
    const { el } = await setup(doc(over));
    await loaded(el);
    expect(q(el, "#panel-summary").textContent).toContain(text);
  });
  it("a failed document offers Retry, which asks the server", async () => {
    const { el, api } = await setup(doc({ status: "failed", error: "Could not open the file.", summary: null, stats: null }));
    await loaded(el);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-retry]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(api.retryDocument).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ID));
  });
});

describe("dm-doc-detail: analysis", () => {
  it("draws the outline, the stat grid, the dates and the numbers as text", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup();
    await loaded(el);
    tab(el, "analysis").click();
    await settle(el);

    expect([...p.querySelectorAll("[data-outline] li")].map((x) => x.textContent)).toEqual(["Introduction", "Terms <b>and</b> conditions"]);
    expect(p.querySelector("[data-outline] b")).toBeNull();
    const stats = Object.fromEntries([...p.querySelectorAll("[data-stat]")].map((x) => [x.getAttribute("data-stat"), x.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim()]));
    expect(stats).toMatchObject({ words: "Words 4,200", pages: "Pages 12", reading: "Reading time 18 min", language: "Language en" });
    expect([...p.querySelectorAll("[data-date]")].map((x) => x.textContent)).toEqual(["2026-07-12"]);
    expect([...p.querySelectorAll("[data-number]")].map((x) => x.textContent)).toEqual(["30 days", "5%"]);

    expect(q(el, "[data-stat=language]")).toBeNull();

    await loaded(none.el);
    tab(none.el, "analysis").click();
    await settle(none.el);
    expect(q(none.el, "#panel-analysis").textContent).toContain("No analysis is available for this document yet.");
  });
});

describe("dm-doc-detail: delete", () => {
  it("confirms, deletes, and goes back to the library; a failure keeps the page and says so", async () => {
    const { el, api, services } = await setup();
    await loaded(el);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-delete]").click();
    await settle(el);
    const dlg = q<HTMLElement & { open: boolean }>(el, "dm-dialog");
    expect(dlg.open).toBe(true);
    api.deleteDocument.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(500, "internal"));
    (dlg.querySelector("button[data-confirm]") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.toasts.get().at(-1)?.text).toBe("Could not delete the document."));
    expect(services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "document", id: ID });
    (dlg.querySelector("button[data-confirm]") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "documents" }));
    expect(api.deleteDocument).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `dm-doc-detail.ts`.**

```ts
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, css, html, nothing } from "lit";
import { formatBytes } from "../state/format";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import { fileKind } from "./dm-file-tile";
import "./dm-dialog";
import "./dm-file-tile";
import "./dm-icon";
import "./dm-status-badge";

/** The tabs, in order. Task 199 appends `content`. */
export const TABS = [
  { id: "summary", label: "Summary" },
  { id: "analysis", label: "Analysis" },
] as const;
type TabId = (typeof TABS)[number]["id"] | "content";

const n = new Intl.NumberFormat("en");
let ids = 0;

export class DmDocDetail extends LitElement {
  static properties = { tab: { state: true }, gone: { state: true }, failed: { state: true }, confirming: { state: true }, busy: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; padding: 20px 40px 24px; overflow: auto; height: 100%; }
      nav[aria-label="Breadcrumb"] { font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--dm-muted); display: flex; gap: 6px; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 14px; }
      nav a { color: inherit; }
      header.top { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 16px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
      .title { flex: 1; min-width: 240px; }
      h1 { font-size: 24px; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
      [data-meta] { color: var(--dm-muted); font-size: 13px; margin-top: 4px; display: flex; gap: 10px; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; }
      [role="tablist"] { display: flex; gap: 22px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); margin: 22px 0 18px; }
      [role="tab"] { all: unset; cursor: pointer; padding: 8px 0 10px; font-size: 14px; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      [role="tab"][aria-selected="true"] { color: var(--dm-text); box-shadow: inset 0 -2px 0 var(--dm-accent); }
      [role="tab"]:focus-visible { outline: var(--dm-focus-ring); outline-offset: 2px; }
      [role="tabpanel"] { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 40px; }
      [role="tabpanel"][hidden] { display: none; }
      .col { flex: 1 1 480px; max-width: 70ch; display: grid; gap: 24px; align-content: start; }
      .side { flex: 1 1 260px; max-width: 360px; display: grid; gap: 24px; align-content: start; }
      .eyebrow { font-size: 12px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.08em; color: var(--dm-accent-300); margin-bottom: 6px; }
      [data-tldr] { font-size: 17px; line-height: 1.65; margin: 0; }
      ol, ul { margin: 0; padding-left: 22px; display: grid; gap: 6px; }
      .tags { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 6px; }
      .questions { display: grid; gap: 8px; }
      .questions .btn { justify-content: flex-start; text-align: left; }
      .grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(140px, 1fr)); gap: 16px; }
      [data-stat] .label { font-size: 12px; color: var(--dm-muted); display: block; }
      [data-stat] .value { font-size: 19px; font-weight: 500; }
      .kv { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr; gap: 4px; }
      .state { color: var(--dm-muted); }
    `,
  ];
  declare tab: TabId;
  declare gone: boolean;
  declare failed: boolean;
  declare confirming: boolean;
  declare busy: boolean;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.router, s.documents]);
  private docId: string | undefined;
  private readonly uid = ++ids;

  constructor() {
    super();
    this.tab = "summary";
    this.gone = false;
    this.failed = false;
    this.confirming = false;
    this.busy = false;
  }
  disconnectedCallback() {
    this.ctx.services?.documents.stopEvents();
    this.docId = undefined;
    super.disconnectedCallback();
  }
  private routeId(): string | undefined {
    const r = this.ctx.services?.router.get();
    return r?.name === "document" ? r.id : undefined;
  }
  protected willUpdate() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const id = this.routeId();
    if (!svc || !id || id === this.docId) return;
    this.docId = id;
    this.gone = false;
    this.failed = false;
    svc.documents.startEvents();
    void svc.documents.refreshOne(id).then((result) => {
      if (this.docId !== id) return;
      this.gone = result === "gone";
      this.failed = result === "error" && !svc.documents.get().items.some((d) => d.id === id);
    });
  }
  private doc(): DocumentDto | undefined {
    return this.ctx.services?.documents.get().items.find((d) => d.id === this.docId);
  }

  private async ask(question?: string) {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc || !this.docId) return;
    try {
      const chat = await svc.sessions.create({ documentIds: [this.docId] });
      if (question) svc.outbox.set({ chatId: chat.id, text: question });
      svc.router.navigate({ name: "chat", chatId: chat.id });
    } catch {
      svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not start the chat." });
    }
  }
  private async remove() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc || !this.docId || this.busy) return;
    this.busy = true;
    try {
      await svc.documents.remove(this.docId);
      this.confirming = false;
      svc.router.navigate({ name: "documents" });
    } catch {
      svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not delete the document." });
    } finally {
      this.busy = false;
    }
  }
  private async retry(id: string) {
    try {
      await this.ctx.services?.documents.retry(id);
    } catch {
      this.ctx.services?.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not retry that document." });
    }
  }
  private onTabKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    const ids = TABS.map((t) => t.id) as TabId[];
    const at = ids.indexOf(this.tab);
    const next = e.key === "ArrowRight" ? (at + 1) % ids.length : e.key === "ArrowLeft" ? (at - 1 + ids.length) % ids.length : e.key === "Home" ? 0 : e.key === "End" ? ids.length - 1 : -1;
    if (next === -1) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    this.tab = ids[next] as TabId;
    void this.updateComplete.then(() => this.shadowRoot?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[role=tab][data-tab=${this.tab}]`)?.focus());
  }

  private summary(d: DocumentDto) {
    if (d.status === "failed")
      return html`<div class="col"><p class="state" data-error>${d.error ?? "This document could not be processed."}</p><div><button class="btn primary" data-retry @click=${() => void this.retry(d.id)}><dm-icon name="arrow-clockwise"></dm-icon>Retry</button></div></div>`;
    if (!d.summary)
      return html`<p class="state">${d.status === "ready" ? "No summary is available for this document." : "This document is still being processed. Its summary and analysis appear here when it is ready."}</p>`;
    const s = d.summary;
    return html`<div class="col">
        <section><div class="eyebrow">TL;DR</div><p data-tldr>${s.tldr}</p></section>
        ${s.keyPoints.length > 0 ? html`<section><div class="eyebrow">Key points</div><ol>${s.keyPoints.map((k) => html`<li data-keypoint>${k}</li>`)}</ol></section>` : nothing}
      </div>
      <div class="side">
        ${s.entities.length + s.topics.length > 0
          ? html`<section><div class="eyebrow">Entities and topics</div><div class="tags">${s.entities.map((x) => html`<span class="tag" data-entity>${x}</span>`)}${s.topics.map((x) => html`<span class="tag accent" data-topic>${x}</span>`)}</div></section>`
          : nothing}
        ${s.suggestedQuestions.length > 0
          ? html`<section><div class="eyebrow">Suggested questions</div><div class="questions">${s.suggestedQuestions.map((x) => html`<button class="btn secondary" data-question @click=${() => void this.ask(x)}><dm-icon name="chat-circle-text"></dm-icon>${x}</button>`)}</div></section>`
          : nothing}
      </div>`;
  }
  private analysis(d: DocumentDto) {
    const a = d.stats;
    if (!a) return html`<p class="state">No analysis is available for this document yet.</p>`;
    const stat = (key: string, label: string, value: string) => html`<div data-stat=${key}><span class="label">${label}</span><span class="value">${value}</span></div>`;
    return html`<div class="col">
        ${a.outline.length > 0 ? html`<section><div class="eyebrow">Structure</div><ol data-outline>${a.outline.map((o) => html`<li>${o}</li>`)}</ol></section>` : nothing}
        <section class="grid">${stat("words", "Words", n.format(a.wordCount))}${a.pageCount !== null ? stat("pages", "Pages", n.format(a.pageCount)) : nothing}${stat("reading", "Reading time", `${Math.max(1, Math.round(a.readingTimeMinutes))} min`)}${a.language !== null ? stat("language", "Language", a.language) : nothing}</section>
      </div>
      <div class="side">
        ${a.dates.length > 0 ? html`<section><div class="eyebrow">Notable dates</div><ul class="kv">${a.dates.map((x) => html`<li data-date>${x}</li>`)}</ul></section>` : nothing}
        ${a.numbers.length > 0 ? html`<section><div class="eyebrow">Notable numbers</div><ul class="kv">${a.numbers.map((x) => html`<li data-number>${x}</li>`)}</ul></section>` : nothing}
      </div>`;
  }
  protected panel(id: string, d: DocumentDto) {
    return id === "summary" ? this.summary(d) : id === "analysis" ? this.analysis(d) : nothing;
  }

  render() {
    if (this.gone)
      return html`<p class="state">This document no longer exists. <a href="/documents">Back to your documents</a></p>`;
    const d = this.doc();
    if (!d && this.failed)
      return html`<p class="state" role="alert">Could not load this document. <button class="btn secondary" data-reload @click=${() => { this.docId = undefined; this.requestUpdate(); }}>Try again</button></p>`;
    if (!d) return html`<p class="state" aria-busy="true">Loading...</p>`;
    const kind = fileKind(d.filename, d.mime).label;
    const base = `dd${this.uid}`;
    return html`
      <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb"><a href="/documents">Documents</a><dm-icon name="caret-right"></dm-icon><span aria-current="page">${d.title}</span></nav>
      <header class="top">
        <dm-file-tile size="lg" .filename=${d.filename} .mime=${d.mime}></dm-file-tile>
        <div class="title"><h1>${d.title}</h1>
          <div data-meta><span>${kind}</span><span>${formatBytes(d.sizeBytes)}</span>${d.stats?.pageCount != null ? html`<span>${d.stats.pageCount} ${d.stats.pageCount === 1 ? "page" : "pages"}</span>` : nothing}${d.stats ? html`<span>${n.format(d.stats.wordCount)} words</span>` : nothing}<dm-status-badge .status=${d.status}></dm-status-badge></div></div>
        <button class="btn primary" data-ask @click=${() => void this.ask()}><dm-icon name="chat-circle-text"></dm-icon>Ask about this document</button>
        <button class="btn secondary" data-delete @click=${() => (this.confirming = true)}><dm-icon name="trash"></dm-icon>Delete</button>
      </header>
      <div role="tablist" aria-label="Document" @keydown=${this.onTabKey}>
        ${TABS.map((t) => html`<button role="tab" id="${base}-tab-${t.id}" data-tab=${t.id} aria-selected=${this.tab === t.id} aria-controls="panel-${t.id}" tabindex=${this.tab === t.id ? 0 : -1} @click=${() => (this.tab = t.id)}>${t.label}</button>`)}
      </div>
      ${TABS.map((t) => html`<div role="tabpanel" id="panel-${t.id}" aria-labelledby="${base}-tab-${t.id}" ?hidden=${this.tab !== t.id}>${this.tab === t.id ? this.panel(t.id, d) : nothing}</div>`)}
      <dm-dialog heading="Delete this document?" ?open=${this.confirming} persistent @dm-close=${() => (this.confirming = false)}>
        <p>This removes the document, its summary and its passages. Chats that used it can no longer cite it. This cannot be undone.</p>
        <button slot="actions" class="btn secondary" data-cancel @click=${() => (this.confirming = false)}>Cancel</button>
        <button slot="actions" class="btn danger" data-confirm ?disabled=${this.busy} @click=${() => void this.remove()}>Delete</button>
      </dm-dialog>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-doc-detail", DmDocDetail);
```

  The first test expects the tab's id to match the panel's `aria-labelledby` and the tab's `aria-controls` to match the panel's id (`panel-summary`); both hold. `views.ts`: `document: "dm-doc-detail"`; `components/index.ts`: the import. The panel-1 test `panels.map(hidden)` expects `[false, true]`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run it.** `pnpm --filter @docmind/web exec vitest run`. Expected: PASS (22 tests).
  **Probe.** `d.stats?.pageCount != null` is a deliberate loose comparison (null from the API, undefined with no stats); Biome flags `!= null` (`noDoubleEquals` with `ignoreNull` is on by default: allowed). If the lint step complains, write `d.stats && d.stats.pageCount !== null`.

### Task 199: the Content tab, and "Open in document" from a citation

**Spec:** §9 item 44 (Content tab), §6.3 (a source card links to the document), `ui-mocks.md` §4 Content (text): find-in-text, Copy text, page markers, headings. Finding U9; open question 5.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-doc-content.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/dm-doc-detail.ts` (the `content` tab, opening on it for a `?passage=` link), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-doc-content.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-doc-detail.test.ts` (changed and new cases)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Api.documentContent` (197), `DocumentPassage`, `ToastStore`, `ServicesController`.
- Produces: `<dm-doc-content .docId .ready .highlight>`: loads the passages of the document a page at a time and draws them in reading order: a marker "Page N" whenever the page changes (for `page` and `region` locators), the passage's last heading path entry as a subheading whenever it changes, and the text in paragraphs (split on blank lines), all as text. A **Find in text** box (label "Find in text") marks case-insensitive occurrences in the loaded text with `<mark>` and says "N matches" or "No matches" (and that only the loaded text is searched while more is left). **Copy text** puts the loaded text on the clipboard. **Load more passages** appends the next page. `.highlight` is a passage id: the element keeps loading pages (at most `MAX_PAGES = 25`, 500 passages) until it finds that passage, then scrolls it into view and marks it (`data-highlight`, `aria-current="location"`); if it is not found it says "That passage was not found in the first 500 passages." Fixed texts: "Passages overlap slightly where the document was split, so a sentence can appear twice.", "No text was extracted from this document.", "The text appears here when the document is ready.", "Could not load the text. [Try again]", "That passage is not in this document." (the text ended without it).
- `dm-doc-detail`: `TABS` gains `{ id: "content", label: "Content" }`; its panel is `<dm-doc-content>` with `.docId`, `.ready` (status `ready`) and `.highlight` from the route's `passage`. A route with a `passage` opens the Content tab.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

`dm-doc-content.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { DocumentPassage } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import { MAX_PAGES } from "./dm-doc-content";
import "./dm-doc-content";

const DOC = "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11";
const pid = (n: number) => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const passage = (n: number, text: string, over: Partial<DocumentPassage> = {}): DocumentPassage => ({ passageId: pid(n), ordinal: n, locator: { kind: "page", page: Math.floor(n / 2) + 1 }, headingPath: ["Terms", `Clause ${n}`], text, ...over });
const page = (items: DocumentPassage[], nextCursor: string | null = null) => ({ items, nextCursor });

async function setup(pages: ReturnType<typeof page>[], props: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const queue = [...pages];
  const documentContent = vi.fn(async () => { const next = queue.shift(); if (!next) throw new ApiError(500, "internal"); if (next instanceof Error) throw next; return next; });
  const services = createServices({ api: { documentContent } as never, fetch: (async () => new Response("{}")) as never });
  const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-doc-content", { docId: DOC, ready: true, ...props });
  return { el, services, documentContent };
}
const root = (el: Element) => el.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot;
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => root(el).querySelector(sel) as T;
const qa = (el: Element, sel: string) => [...root(el).querySelectorAll(sel)];
const settle = (el: Element) => (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;
const loaded = (el: Element, n: number) => vi.waitFor(() => expect(qa(el, "[data-passage]").length).toBe(n));

describe("dm-doc-content: reading", () => {
  it("draws the passages in order, with a page marker when the page changes and a heading when it changes, as text", async () => {
    const { el, documentContent } = await setup([page([passage(0, "First <b>part</b>.\n\nSecond paragraph."), passage(1, "Next."), passage(2, "New page.")])]);
    await loaded(el, 3);
    expect(documentContent).toHaveBeenCalledWith(DOC, undefined);
    expect(qa(el, "[data-page-marker]").map((m) => m.textContent)).toEqual(["Page 1", "Page 2"]);
    expect(qa(el, "[data-heading]").map((h) => h.textContent)).toEqual(["Clause 0", "Clause 1", "Clause 2"]);
    const first = qa(el, "[data-passage]")[0] as HTMLElement;
    expect([...first.querySelectorAll("p")].map((p) => p.textContent)).toEqual(["First <b>part</b>.", "Second paragraph."]);
    expect(first.querySelector("p b")).toBeNull();
    expect(root(el).textContent).toContain("Passages overlap slightly where the document was split");
  });

  it("loads more on request and hides the button at the end", async () => {
    const { el, documentContent } = await setup([page([passage(0, "a")], "c1"), page([passage(1, "b")], null)]);
    await loaded(el, 1);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-more]").click();
    await loaded(el, 2);
    expect(documentContent).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(DOC, "c1");
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "button[data-more]")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("states of a document with nothing to show", async () => {
    const empty = await setup([page([])]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(root(empty.el).textContent).toContain("No text was extracted from this document."));
    const notReady = await setup([page([])], { ready: false });
    await settle(notReady.el);
    expect(root(notReady.el).textContent).toContain("The text appears here when the document is ready.");
    expect(notReady.documentContent).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("says when it could not load, and tries again", async () => {
    const { el, documentContent } = await setup([new ApiError(500, "internal") as never]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[role=alert]")?.textContent).toContain("Could not load the text."));
    documentContent.mockResolvedValueOnce(page([passage(0, "ok")]));
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "[role=alert] button").click();
    await loaded(el, 1);
  });
});

describe("dm-doc-content: find and copy", () => {
  it("marks every occurrence, case-insensitively, keeps the text intact, and counts them", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup([page([passage(0, "Payment is due. A late PAYMENT costs 5%."), passage(1, "No match here.")])]);
    await loaded(el, 2);
    const input = q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[aria-label='Find in text']");
    input.value = "payment";
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    await settle(el);
    expect(qa(el, "mark").map((m) => m.textContent)).toEqual(["Payment", "PAYMENT"]);
    expect((qa(el, "[data-passage]")[0] as HTMLElement).textContent?.replace(/\s+/g, " ")).toContain("Payment is due. A late PAYMENT costs 5%.");
    expect(q(el, "[data-found]").textContent).toBe("2 matches");
    input.value = "zzz";
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[data-found]").textContent).toBe("No matches");
    expect(q(el, "[data-found]").getAttribute("aria-live")).toBe("polite");
    input.value = "";
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[data-found]").textContent).toBe("");
    expect(qa(el, "mark").length).toBe(0);
  });

  it("treats the search text as plain text, not a pattern, and says when more is left to load", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup([page([passage(0, "price (5%) [x] a.b")], "c1")]);
    await loaded(el, 1);
    const input = q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[aria-label='Find in text']");
    for (const [text, count] of [["(5%)", 1], ["[x]", 1], [".", 1], ["a.b", 1], ["*", 0]] as const) {
      input.value = text;
      input.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
      await settle(el);
      expect(qa(el, "mark").length, text).toBe(count);
    }
    input.value = "price";
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[data-found]").textContent).toContain("Only the loaded text is searched.");
  });

  it("copies the loaded text and says what it copied", async () => {
    const writeText = vi.fn(async () => {});
    Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", { value: { writeText }, configurable: true });
    const { el, services } = await setup([page([passage(0, "Alpha."), passage(1, "Beta.")], "c1")]);
    await loaded(el, 2);
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-copy]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith("Alpha.\n\nBeta."));
    expect(services.toasts.get().at(-1)?.text).toBe("Copied the text loaded so far.");
    writeText.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("denied"));
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-copy]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.toasts.get().at(-1)?.text).toBe("Could not copy."));
  });
});

describe("dm-doc-content: opening on a passage", () => {
  it("keeps loading pages until it finds the passage, scrolls to it and marks it", async () => {
    const scroll = vi.fn();
    Element.prototype.scrollIntoView = scroll;
    const { el, documentContent } = await setup(
      [page([passage(0, "a")], "c1"), page([passage(1, "b")], "c2"), page([passage(2, "target text")], null)],
      { highlight: pid(2) },
    );
    await loaded(el, 3);
    expect(documentContent).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
    const target = q<HTMLElement>(el, `[data-passage="${pid(2)}"]`);
    expect(target.hasAttribute("data-highlight")).toBe(true);
    expect(target.getAttribute("aria-current")).toBe("location");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(scroll).toHaveBeenCalled());
  });
  it("stops after the page limit and says the passage was not found", async () => {
    const pages = Array.from({ length: MAX_PAGES }, (_, i) => page([passage(i, "x")], `c${i}`));
    const { el, documentContent } = await setup(pages, { highlight: pid(999) });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[data-not-found]")?.textContent).toContain("That passage was not found in the first 500 passages."));
    expect(documentContent).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(MAX_PAGES);
  });
  it("stops quietly when the passage is simply not in the document", async () => {
    const { el } = await setup([page([passage(0, "a")], null)], { highlight: pid(999) });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[data-not-found]")?.textContent).toContain("That passage is not in this document."));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `dm-doc-content.ts`.**

```ts
import type { DocumentPassage } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, type PropertyValues, css, html, nothing } from "lit";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

export const MAX_PAGES = 25;

/** `text` cut into plain and matching pieces; matching is a case-insensitive substring search, never a pattern. */
function pieces(text: string, needle: string): { text: string; hit: boolean }[] {
  if (needle === "") return [{ text, hit: false }];
  const lower = text.toLowerCase();
  const find = needle.toLowerCase();
  const out: { text: string; hit: boolean }[] = [];
  let at = 0;
  for (;;) {
    const next = lower.indexOf(find, at);
    if (next === -1) break;
    if (next > at) out.push({ text: text.slice(at, next), hit: false });
    out.push({ text: text.slice(next, next + find.length), hit: true });
    at = next + find.length;
  }
  if (at < text.length) out.push({ text: text.slice(at), hit: false });
  return out;
}
const pageOf = (p: DocumentPassage): number | undefined => (p.locator.kind === "page" || p.locator.kind === "region" ? p.locator.page : undefined);

export class DmDocContent extends LitElement {
  static properties = {
    docId: { type: String }, ready: { type: Boolean }, highlight: { type: String },
    passages: { state: true }, next: { state: true }, loading: { state: true }, failed: { state: true }, find: { state: true }, searched: { state: true },
  };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; max-width: 72ch; }
      .bar { display: flex; gap: 10px; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-bottom: 8px; }
      .bar input { flex: 1 1 220px; min-height: 34px; padding: 0 10px; font: inherit; font-size: 13px; color: var(--dm-text); background: transparent; border: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); }
      [data-found], .note { font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      [data-page-marker] { margin: 22px 0 8px; font-size: 11.5px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.08em; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      h3 { font-size: 19px; margin: 18px 0 6px; }
      p { font-size: 15.5px; line-height: 1.75; margin: 0 0 12px; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
      mark { background: var(--dm-accent-800); color: var(--dm-accent-100); border-radius: 3px; padding: 0 1px; }
      [data-passage][data-highlight] { box-shadow: -3px 0 0 var(--dm-accent); padding-left: 10px; margin-left: -13px; }
    `,
  ];
  declare docId: string;
  declare ready: boolean;
  declare highlight: string | undefined;
  declare passages: DocumentPassage[];
  declare next: string | null;
  declare loading: boolean;
  declare failed: boolean;
  declare find: string;
  declare searched: boolean;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this);
  private pages = 0;
  private started: string | undefined;
  private scrolled = false;

  constructor() {
    super();
    this.ready = false;
    this.passages = [];
    this.next = null;
    this.loading = false;
    this.failed = false;
    this.find = "";
    this.searched = false;
  }
  protected willUpdate(changed: PropertyValues<this>) {
    const key = this.ready ? this.docId : undefined;
    if (this.ctx.services && key && key !== this.started) {
      this.started = key;
      this.passages = [];
      this.next = null;
      this.pages = 0;
      this.scrolled = false;
      this.searched = false;
      void this.loadPage(undefined);
    } else if (changed.has("highlight")) this.scrolled = false;
  }
  private async loadPage(cursor: string | undefined) {
    const api = this.ctx.services?.api;
    if (!api || this.loading) return;
    this.loading = true;
    this.failed = false;
    try {
      const out = await api.documentContent(this.docId, cursor);
      this.passages = [...this.passages, ...out.items];
      this.next = out.nextCursor;
      this.pages++;
    } catch {
      this.failed = true;
    } finally {
      this.loading = false;
    }
    // Opening on a passage: keep reading until it is found, the text ends, or the limit is reached.
    if (!this.failed && this.highlight && !this.passages.some((p) => p.passageId === this.highlight)) {
      if (this.next && this.pages < MAX_PAGES) void this.loadPage(this.next);
      else this.searched = true;
    }
  }
  protected updated() {
    if (!this.highlight || this.scrolled) return;
    const el = [...(this.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll("[data-passage]") ?? [])].find((e) => e.getAttribute("data-passage") === this.highlight);
    if (el) {
      this.scrolled = true;
      el.scrollIntoView?.({ block: "center" });
    }
  }
  private async copy() {
    const toasts = this.ctx.services?.toasts;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(this.passages.map((p) => p.text).join("\n\n"));
      toasts?.push({ kind: "ok", text: this.next ? "Copied the text loaded so far." : "Copied the text." });
    } catch {
      toasts?.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not copy." });
    }
  }
  render() {
    if (!this.ready) return html`<p class="note">The text appears here when the document is ready.</p>`;
    if (this.failed && this.passages.length === 0)
      return html`<div class="error" role="alert">Could not load the text. <button class="btn secondary" @click=${() => void this.loadPage(undefined)}>Try again</button></div>`;
    if (!this.loading && this.passages.length === 0) return html`<p class="note">No text was extracted from this document.</p>`;
    const needle = this.find.trim();
    let matches = 0;
    let lastPage: number | undefined;
    let lastHeading: string | undefined;
    const body = this.passages.map((p) => {
      const pg = pageOf(p);
      const heading = p.headingPath[p.headingPath.length - 1];
      const marker = pg !== undefined && pg !== lastPage ? html`<div data-page-marker>Page ${pg}</div>` : nothing;
      const sub = heading !== undefined && heading !== lastHeading ? html`<h3 data-heading>${heading}</h3>` : nothing;
      if (pg !== undefined) lastPage = pg;
      lastHeading = heading;
      const on = p.passageId === this.highlight;
      return html`${marker}${sub}<section data-passage=${p.passageId} ?data-highlight=${on} aria-current=${on ? "location" : "false"}>
        ${p.text.split(/\n\s*\n/).map((para) => html`<p>${pieces(para, needle).map((x) => { if (x.hit) matches++; return x.hit ? html`<mark>${x.text}</mark>` : x.text; })}</p>`)}</section>`;
    });
    const found = needle === "" ? "" : matches === 0 ? "No matches" : `${matches} ${matches === 1 ? "match" : "matches"}`;
    return html`
      <div class="bar"><input type="search" aria-label="Find in text" placeholder="Find in text" .value=${this.find} @input=${(e: Event) => (this.find = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value)} />
        <button class="btn secondary" data-copy @click=${() => void this.copy()}><dm-icon name="copy"></dm-icon>Copy text</button></div>
      <div data-found aria-live="polite">${found}${needle !== "" && this.next ? html` Only the loaded text is searched.` : nothing}</div>
      <p class="note">Passages overlap slightly where the document was split, so a sentence can appear twice.</p>
      ${this.searched ? html`<p class="note" data-not-found>${this.next ? "That passage was not found in the first 500 passages." : "That passage is not in this document."}</p>` : nothing}
      ${body}
      ${this.next ? html`<div><button class="btn secondary" data-more ?disabled=${this.loading} @click=${() => void this.loadPage(this.next ?? undefined)}>Load more passages</button></div>` : nothing}`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-doc-content", DmDocContent);
```

  The stop-quietly test expects `[data-not-found]` for a passage absent from a document that ends: the element says "That passage is not in this document." in that case; add that sentence to the fixed texts above. (`searched` is set when the loop ends without finding it.)

  `dm-doc-detail.ts`: `TABS` gets `{ id: "content", label: "Content" }` (drop the `| "content"` from `TabId`); `panel()` becomes:

```ts
  protected panel(id: string, d: DocumentDto) {
    if (id === "summary") return this.summary(d);
    if (id === "analysis") return this.analysis(d);
    const route = this.ctx.services?.router.get();
    return html`<dm-doc-content .docId=${d.id} .ready=${d.status === "ready"} .highlight=${route?.name === "document" ? route.passage : undefined}></dm-doc-content>`;
  }
```

  and in `willUpdate`, after the id logic, `if (route is document with passage and the passage changed) this.tab = "content"`: keep `private lastPassage: string | undefined;` and set `this.tab = "content"` when `route.passage !== undefined && route.passage !== this.lastPassage`, then `this.lastPassage = route.passage`. Import `./dm-doc-content` in `dm-doc-detail.ts` and in `components/index.ts`.

## Phase F: acceptance and documentation

### Task 200a: the fake server and the DOM helpers the acceptance test needs

**Spec:** §8 (Component tests with happy-dom; the Playwright golden path is M7 item 45), §7 Security and accessibility. This task builds the test support; Task 200b uses it.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/testing/fakeServer.ts`, `apps/web/src/testing/dom.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/testing/fakeServer.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/testing/dom.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `class FakeServer`: an in-memory implementation of the API the UI uses (auth, documents, content, chats, messages, stop, receipt) and of both event streams. Members: `fetch: typeof fetch`; `xhr(): XMLHttpRequest` (a fake for uploads); `calls: string[]` (`"METHOD /path"` per request); `loggedIn: boolean`, `docs`, `chats`, `messages` (state, readable by a test); `push(path: string, event: string, data: unknown, id?: string): void` (a frame to every open stream whose path matches); `receipt: string`. Behavior is scripted to be deterministic: sign-up answers 202 and logging in with the same credentials works; an upload creates a `uploaded` document and then pushes `parsing`, `embedding` and `ready` progress frames and fills the document's summary, statistics and one passage; sending a message pushes the user's `message`, then `reasoning-partial`, a `tool` step, `answer` deltas and the assistant's `message` (with one citation to the uploaded document's passage); `DELETE /api/me` with the right password answers 202 with `receipt` and logs out; `GET /api/erasure/:receipt` answers `pending` once and then `done`. Every request that needs a session answers `401 unauthorized` without one.
  - `deepFind(root: ParentNode, selector: string): Element | null`, `deepAll(root, selector): Element[]`, `deepText(root): string` (text through open shadow roots), `unnamedControls(root: ParentNode): string[]`: descriptors (`tag`, plus `data-*` attribute names; never text) of every button, link with an href, input (not hidden), select, textarea and `[role=tab]` that has no accessible name (`aria-label`, `aria-labelledby`, an associated `<label>`, its own text, `title`, or an `<img alt>` inside), looking through shadow roots.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

`dom.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { deepAll, deepFind, deepText, unnamedControls } from "./dom";

function host(html: string): HTMLElement {
  const el = document.createElement("div");
  const root = el.attachShadow({ mode: "open" });
  root.innerHTML = html;
  document.body.append(el);
  return el;
}

describe("deep DOM helpers", () => {
  it("find, list and read text through nested open shadow roots", () => {
    const outer = host("<p>outer</p><section id='slot'></section>");
    const inner = document.createElement("span");
    inner.attachShadow({ mode: "open" }).innerHTML = "<b class='x'>deep</b>";
    outer.shadowRoot?.getElementById("slot")?.append(inner);
    expect(deepFind(outer, "b.x")?.textContent).toBe("deep");
    expect(deepAll(outer, "p, b").map((e) => e.tagName)).toEqual(["P", "B"]);
    expect(deepText(outer)).toContain("outer");
    expect(deepText(outer)).toContain("deep");
    expect(deepFind(outer, "i")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("unnamedControls", () => {
  it("accepts a control named by aria-label, aria-labelledby, a label, its text, a title or an image alt", () => {
    const el = host(`
      <button aria-label="Close"></button>
      <span id="n">Name</span><button aria-labelledby="n"></button>
      <label for="e">Email</label><input id="e">
      <label>Wrapped <input></label>
      <button>Send</button>
      <a href="/x" title="Home"></a>
      <a href="/y"><img alt="Logo"></a>
      <select aria-label="Type"></select><textarea aria-label="Message"></textarea>
      <div role="tab" aria-label="Summary"></div>`);
    expect(unnamedControls(el)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("reports a control with no name, by tag and data attributes only, never by text", () => {
    const el = host(`
      <button data-copy></button>
      <input type="text" placeholder="only a placeholder">
      <a href="/z"></a>
      <input type="hidden">
      <a>no href, not a link</a>
      <button aria-label=""></button>
      <div role="tab"></div>`);
    expect(unnamedControls(el).sort()).toEqual(["a", "button", "button[data-copy]", "input", "tab"].sort());
  });
  it("looks inside shadow roots", () => {
    const outer = host("<section></section>");
    const inner = document.createElement("span");
    inner.attachShadow({ mode: "open" }).innerHTML = "<button data-x></button>";
    outer.shadowRoot?.querySelector("section")?.append(inner);
    expect(unnamedControls(outer)).toEqual(["button[data-x]"]);
  });
});
```
